nTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle_Lang] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId AND PT.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) + '),'''')  + '' '' +
									ISNULL((SELECT ISNULL(CCPLN.prsFName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(CCPLN.prsLName,'''') FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons_Lang] CCPLN WHERE CCPLN.CompanyPersonID = CCP.CompanyPersonID AND CCPLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) + '),'''')
							FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
							WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
							)				
					END)CompanyName				
			FROM  [dbo].[CrmCompany] C 
			WHERE C.CompanyID = TCC.CompanyId) CompanyName
			/*RZ14000617- End*/
			  


			  ,[CompanyPersonId]
			  ,(ISNULL((SELECT CPT.PersonTitle FROM dbo.CRMPersonTitle_Lang CPT WHERE CPT.PersonTitleID = 
				(SELECT prsTitleId FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons CCP WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = TCC.CompanyPersonId) AND CPT.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) + '), '''') + '' '' +
				ISNULL((SELECT ISNULL(CCP.prsFName, '''') + ISNULL('' '' + CCP.prsLName, '''') 
				FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons_Lang CCP WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = TCC.CompanyPersonId AND CCP.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) + '),'''')) PersonName			  			    
			  ,[Picture]
			  ,[IsActive]
			  ,[UserId]
			  ,(SELECT ISNULL([FName] + '' '','''') + ISNULL([LName],'''') FROM [dbo].[Users_Lang] U WHERE U.[UserId] = TCC.[UserId] AND U.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) + ') RegisterUser
			  ,[Year1]
			  ,[Month1]
			  ,[RegisterDate]
			  ,[RegisterDatePersian]
			  ,[GUId]
			  ,[AppType]
			  ,[LanguageID]
			  ,[FirstRegisterUserId]
			  ,(SELECT ISNULL([FName] + '' '','''') + ISNULL([LName],'''') FROM [dbo].[Users_Lang] U WHERE U.[UserId] = TCC.[FirstRegisterUserId] AND U.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) + ') FirstRegisterUser			  
			  ,[FirstRegisterDatePersian]
			  ,[FirstRegisterDate]
			  ,(SELECT COUNT(*) FROM [dbo].[CrmTelegBotChatMessage] TBCM
					WHERE TBCM.[ChatId] = TCC.[ChatId]
					AND ISNULL(TBCM.[InComing],0) = 1
					 AND TBCM.[ReadDateTime] IS NULL) UnReadMessageCount
			  ,(SELECT TOP 1 TBCM.[Date]            
					FROM [dbo].[CrmTelegBotChatMessage] TBCM
					WHERE TBCM.[ChatId] = TCC.[ChatId]
					/*AND ISNULL(TBCM.[InComing],0) = 1
					AND TBCM.[ReadDateTime] IS NULL*/
					ORDER BY TBCM.[Date] DESC) LastMessageDate
		  FROM [dbo].[CrmTelegBotContacts] TCC
		  WHERE ISNULL(TCC.[IsActive],0) = 1
		  AND TCC.[TelegBotContactId] = ' + CAST(@TelegBotContactId AS NVARCHAR(MAX))  	
		  SET @Ord = ''
	END		
	ELSE IF(@TelegBotContactId = 0 AND @SearchValue IS NULL)/*all contacts*/
	BEGIN
		SET @Qry ='SELECT [TelegBotContactId]
			  ,[ChatId]
			  ,[FirstName]
			  ,[LastName]
			  ,[UserName]
			  ,(ISNULL([FirstName],'''') + ISNULL('' '' + [LastName],'''')) ContactName
			  ,[PhoneNumber]
			  ,[Type]
			  ,[Title]
			  ,[CompanyId]
			  /*,(ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM dbo.CrmCompanyTitle_Lang CT WHERE CT.CompanyTitleId = 
					(SELECT cmpTitleId FROM dbo.CrmCompany CC WHERE CC.CompanyID = TCC.CompanyId) AND CT.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) + '), '''') + '' '' +
					ISNULL((SELECT CC.CmpName FROM dbo.CrmCompany_Lang CC WHERE CC.CompanyID = TCC.CompanyId AND CC.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) + '),'''')) CompanyName	*/										  		
				/*RZ14000617- Change cmpName based on cmpKind*/
			,(SELECT 
				(CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 0
						THEN ((ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle_Lang] CT WHERE CT.[CompanyTitleID] = C.[cmpTitleId] AND CT.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) + '),'''') + '' '' + 
								ISNULL((SELECT CLN.[cmpName] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany_Lang] CLN WHERE CLN.CompanyID = C.CompanyID AND CLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) + '),'''')))
						ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle_Lang] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId AND PT.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) + '),'''')  + '' '' +
									ISNULL((SELECT ISNULL(CCPLN.prsFName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(CCPLN.prsLName,'''') FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons_Lang] CCPLN WHERE CCPLN.CompanyPersonID = CCP.CompanyPersonID AND CCPLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) + '),'''')
							FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
							WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
							)				
					END)CompanyName				
			FROM  [dbo].[CrmCompany] C 
			WHERE C.CompanyID = TCC.CompanyId) CompanyName
			/*RZ14000617- End*/
			  ,[CompanyPersonId]
			  ,(ISNULL((SELECT CPT.PersonTitle FROM dbo.CRMPersonTitle_Lang CPT WHERE CPT.PersonTitleID = 
				(SELECT prsTitleId FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons CCP WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = TCC.CompanyPersonId) AND CPT.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) + '), '''') + '' '' +
				ISNULL((SELECT ISNULL(CCP.prsFName, '''') + ISNULL('' '' + CCP.prsLName, '''') 
				FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons_Lang CCP WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = TCC.CompanyPersonId AND CCP.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) + '),'''')) PersonName			  			    
			  ,[Picture]
			  ,[IsActive]
			  ,[UserId]
			  ,(SELECT ISNULL([FName] + '' '','''') + ISNULL([LName],'''') FROM [dbo].[Users_Lang] U WHERE U.[UserId] = TCC.[UserId] AND U.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) + ') RegisterUser
			  ,[Year1]
			  ,[Month1]
			  ,[RegisterDate]
			  ,[RegisterDatePersian]
			  ,[GUId]
			  ,[AppType]
			  ,[LanguageID]
			  ,[FirstRegisterUserId]
			  ,(SELECT ISNULL([FName] + '' '','''') + ISNULL([LName],'''') FROM [dbo].[Users_Lang] U WHERE U.[UserId] = TCC.[FirstRegisterUserId] AND U.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) + ') FirstRegisterUser			  
			  ,[FirstRegisterDatePersian]
			  ,[FirstRegisterDate]
			  ,(SELECT COUNT(*) FROM [dbo].[CrmTelegBotChatMessage] TBCM
					WHERE TBCM.[ChatId] = TCC.[ChatId]
					AND ISNULL(TBCM.[InComing],0) = 1
					 AND TBCM.[ReadDateTime] IS NULL) UnReadMessageCount
			  ,(SELECT TOP 1 TBCM.[Date]            
					FROM [dbo].[CrmTelegBotChatMessage] TBCM
					WHERE TBCM.[ChatId] = TCC.[ChatId]
					/*AND ISNULL(TBCM.[InComing],0) = 1
					AND TBCM.[ReadDateTime] IS NULL*/
					ORDER BY TBCM.[Date] DESC) LastMessageDate
		  FROM [dbo].[CrmTelegBotContacts] TCC
		  WHERE ISNULL(TCC.[IsActive],0) = 1 '
		  SET @Ord ='  ORDER BY TCC.[RegisterDate] DESC'
	END	
	ELSE IF(@SearchValue IS NOT NULL)/*search contact*/	  	  			
	BEGIN
		SET @Qry = 'SELECT [TelegBotContactId]
			  ,[ChatId]
			  ,[FirstName]
			  ,[LastName]
			  ,[UserName]
			  ,(ISNULL([FirstName],'''') + ISNULL('' '' + [LastName],'''')) ContactName
			  ,[PhoneNumber]
			  ,[Type]
			  ,[Title]
			  ,[CompanyId]
			  /*,(ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM dbo.CrmCompanyTitle_Lang CT WHERE CT.CompanyTitleId = 
					(SELECT cmpTitleId FROM dbo.CrmCompany CC WHERE CC.CompanyID = TCC.CompanyId) AND CT.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) + '), '''') + '' '' +
					ISNULL((SELECT CC.CmpName FROM dbo.CrmCompany_Lang CC WHERE CC.CompanyID = TCC.CompanyId AND CC.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) + '),'''')) CompanyName		*/									  		
			 /*RZ14000617- Change cmpName based on cmpKind*/
			,(SELECT 
				(CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 0
						THEN ((ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle_Lang] CT WHERE CT.[CompanyTitleID] = C.[cmpTitleId] AND CT.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) + '),'''') + '' '' + 
								ISNULL((SELECT CLN.[cmpName] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany_Lang] CLN WHERE CLN.CompanyID = C.CompanyID AND CLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) + '),'''')))
						ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle_Lang] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId AND PT.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) + '),'''')  + '' '' +
									ISNULL((SELECT ISNULL(CCPLN.prsFName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(CCPLN.prsLName,'''') FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons_Lang] CCPLN WHERE CCPLN.CompanyPersonID = CCP.CompanyPersonID AND CCPLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) + '),'''')
							FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
							WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
							)				
					END)CompanyName				
			FROM  [dbo].[CrmCompany] C 
			WHERE C.CompanyID = TCC.CompanyId) CompanyName
			/*RZ14000617- End*/
			  ,[CompanyPersonId]
			  ,(ISNULL((SELECT CPT.PersonTitle FROM dbo.CRMPersonTitle_Lang CPT WHERE CPT.PersonTitleID = 
				(SELECT prsTitleId FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons CCP WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = TCC.CompanyPersonId) AND CPT.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) + '), '''') + '' '' +
				ISNULL((SELECT ISNULL(CCP.prsFName, '''') + ISNULL('' '' + CCP.prsLName, '''') 
				FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons_Lang CCP WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = TCC.CompanyPersonId AND CCP.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) + '),'''')) PersonName			  			    
			  ,[Picture]
			  ,[IsActive]
			  ,[UserId]
			  ,(SELECT ISNULL([FName] + '' '','''') + ISNULL([LName],'''') FROM [dbo].[Users_Lang] U WHERE U.[UserId] = TCC.[UserId] AND U.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) + ') RegisterUser
			  ,[Year1]
			  ,[Month1]
			  ,[RegisterDate]
			  ,[RegisterDatePersian]
			  ,[GUId]
			  ,[AppType]
			  ,[LanguageID]
			  ,[FirstRegisterUserId]
			  ,(SELECT ISNULL([FName] + '' '','''') + ISNULL([LName],'''') FROM [dbo].[Users_Lang] U WHERE U.[UserId] = TCC.[FirstRegisterUserId] AND U.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) + ') FirstRegisterUser			  
			  ,[FirstRegisterDatePersian]
			  ,[FirstRegisterDate]
			  ,(SELECT COUNT(*) FROM [dbo].[CrmTelegBotChatMessage] TBCM
					WHERE TBCM.[ChatId] = TCC.[ChatId]
					AND ISNULL(TBCM.[InComing],0) = 1
					 AND TBCM.[ReadDateTime] IS NULL) UnReadMessageCount
			  ,(SELECT TOP 1 TBCM.[Date]            
					FROM [dbo].[CrmTelegBotChatMessage] TBCM
					WHERE TBCM.[ChatId] = TCC.[ChatId]
					/*AND ISNULL(TBCM.[InComing],0) = 1
					AND TBCM.[ReadDateTime] IS NULL*/
					ORDER BY TBCM.[Date] DESC) LastMessageDate
		  FROM [dbo].[CrmTelegBotContacts] TCC
		  WHERE ISNULL(TCC.[IsActive],0) = 1
		  AND (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(TCC.[FirstName] AS NVARCHAR(2000)) , NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) 
					Like ''%'' + (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(''' + @SearchValue + ''' AS NVARCHAR(2000)), NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740))+ ''%'')		  		  
		  OR  REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(TCC.[LastName] AS NVARCHAR(2000)) , NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) 
					Like ''%'' + (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(''' + @SearchValue + ''' AS NVARCHAR(2000)), NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740))+ ''%'')		  		  
		  OR  REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(TCC.[UserName] AS NVARCHAR(2000)) , NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) 
					Like ''%'' + (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(''' + @SearchValue + ''' AS NVARCHAR(2000)), NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740))+ ''%'')		  		  
		  OR  REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(TCC.[Title] AS NVARCHAR(2000)) , NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) 
					Like ''%'' + (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(''' + @SearchValue + ''' AS NVARCHAR(2000)), NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740))+ ''%'')		  		  				  
		  OR  TCC.[PhoneNumber] Like ''%'' + ''' + @SearchValue + '''  + ''%''
		  OR  TCC.[CompanyId] IN (
				SELECT CC.CompanyID FROM dbo.[CrmCompany] CC 
				WHERE REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(/*CC.CmpName*/ 
				/*RZ14000617- Change cmpName based on cmpKind*/
				ISNULL(
					(CASE WHEN ISNULL(CC.[cmpKind],0) = 0
						THEN (ISNULL((SELECT CLN.[cmpName] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany_Lang] CLN WHERE CLN.CompanyID = CC.CompanyID AND CLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) + '),''''))
						ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT ISNULL(CCPLN.prsFName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(CCPLN.prsLName,'''') FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons_Lang] CCPLN WHERE CCPLN.CompanyPersonID = CCP.CompanyPersonID AND CCPLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) + '),'''')
							FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
							WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = CC.[cmpMainPersonId]		
							)				
					END)				
				,'''')
				/*RZ14000617- End*/
			  
				
				 AS NVARCHAR(2000)) , NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) 
					Like ''%'' + (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(''' + @SearchValue + ''' AS NVARCHAR(2000)), NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740))+ ''%'')		  		  
		  )		  
		  OR TCC.[CompanyPersonId] IN(
				SELECT CP.CompanyPersonID FROM dbo.[CrmCompanyPersons] CP 
				WHERE REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(CP.prsFName AS NVARCHAR(2000)) , NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) 
					Like ''%'' + (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(''' + @SearchValue + ''' AS NVARCHAR(2000)), NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740))+ ''%'')		  		  
				OR REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(CP.prsLName AS NVARCHAR(2000)) , NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) 
					Like ''%'' + (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(''' + @SearchValue + ''' AS NVARCHAR(2000)), NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740))+ ''%'')		  		  
				OR  CP.prsMobile Like ''%'' + ''' + @SearchValue + '''  + ''%''												
		  )	
		  OR  TCC.[CompanyId] IN (
				SELECT CC.CompanyID FROM dbo.[CrmCompany_Lang] CC 
				WHERE REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(CC.CmpName AS NVARCHAR(2000)) , NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) 
					Like ''%'' + (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(''' + @SearchValue + ''' AS NVARCHAR(2000)), NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740))+ ''%'')		  		  
				AND CC.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) + '
		  )		  
		  OR TCC.[CompanyPersonId] IN(
				SELECT CP.CompanyPersonID FROM dbo.[CrmCompanyPersons_Lang] CP 
				WHERE REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(CP.prsFName AS NVARCHAR(2000)) , NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) 
					Like ''%'' + (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(''' + @SearchValue + ''' AS NVARCHAR(2000)), NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740))+ ''%'')		  		  
				OR REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(CP.prsLName AS NVARCHAR(2000)) , NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) 
					Like ''%'' + (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(''' + @SearchValue + ''' AS NVARCHAR(2000)), NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740))+ ''%'')		  		  																
				AND CP.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) + '
		  )	) '
		  SET @Ord = ' ORDER BY TCC.[RegisterDate] DESC'
	END	
END
/**/
EXECUTE (@Qry + ' ' + ' AND (TCC.[CompanyId] IS NULL OR TCC.[CompanyId] IN (SELECT C.CompanyId FROM dbo.CrmCompany C WHERE 1=1 '+ @cmpDataPermision + '))' + @Ord)
/**/
END






GO
-- [dbo].[CRMspSelectTelegContact] | References permission/temp-filter machinery







/*RZ961011*/
/*RZ9610131016- Add UnReadMessageCount*/
/*RZ961111- Add SettingId*/
/*RZ961114- Add permission data*/
/*RZ14000617- Change cmpName based on cmpKind*/
/*NKR14010515- Add Field*/
/*NKR14030120- Add Field SettingPhoneNumber*/
CREATE Procedure [dbo].[CRMspSelectTelegContact]
(
	/* 
		exec CRMspSelectTelegContact 0,N'', 20, 3, 1065, 1065
	*/		
	@TelegContactId INT,/*id of message in db*/
	@SearchValue NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL,
	/*RZ961111- Add SettingId*/
	@SettingId INT,
	@UserId INT,
	@LanguageID INT = NULL,
	@DefaultLanguageID INT = NULL
)
As
BEGIN
/**/
/*RZ961114- Add permission data*/
DECLARE @Qry VARCHAR(MAX),@cmpDataPermision VARCHAR(MAX) , @Ord VARCHAR(MAX)
EXEC dbo.CRMspSetcmpDataPermission @UserID
/*--*/
SELECT @cmpDataPermision = TXT
FROM dbo.CrmsysDBTempInfo
WHERE TypeId = 20
AND UserId = @UserId
/*--*/	
IF(@LanguageID IS NULL OR @LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID)
BEGIN
	IF(@TelegContactId > 0 AND @SearchValue IS NULL)/*a contact*/
	BEGIN
		SET @Qry = 'SELECT [TelegContactId]
			  ,[ChatId]
			  ,[FirstName]
			  ,[LastName]
			  ,[UserName]
			  ,(ISNULL([FirstName],'''') + ISNULL('' '' + [LastName],'''')) ContactName
			  ,[PhoneNumber]
			  ,[Type]
			  ,[Title]
			  ,[CompanyId]
			  /*,(ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM dbo.CrmCompanyTitle CT WHERE CT.CompanyTitleId = 
					(SELECT cmpTitleId FROM dbo.CrmCompany CC WHERE CC.CompanyID = TCC.CompanyId)), '''') + '' '' +
					(SELECT CC.CmpName FROM dbo.CrmCompany CC WHERE CC.CompanyID = TCC.CompanyId)) CompanyName*/
					
			/*RZ14000617- Change cmpName based on cmpKind*/
			,(SELECT 
				(CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 0
						THEN ((ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle] CT WHERE CT.[CompanyTitleID] = C.[cmpTitleId]),'''') + '' '' + 
								ISNULL(C.[cmpName],'''')))
						ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId),'''')  + '' '' +
									ISNULL(prsFName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(prsLName,'''')
							FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
							WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
							)				
					END)CompanyName				
			FROM  [dbo].[CrmCompany] C
			WHERE C.CompanyID = TCC.CompanyId) CompanyName
			/*RZ14000617- End*/	
																  		
			  ,[CompanyPersonId]
			  ,(ISNULL((SELECT CPT.PersonTitle FROM dbo.CRMPersonTitle CPT WHERE CPT.PersonTitleID = 
				(SELECT prsTitleId FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons CCP WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = TCC.CompanyPersonId)), '''') + '' '' +
				(SELECT ISNULL(CCP.prsFName, '''') + ISNULL('' '' + CCP.prsLName, '''') 
				FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons CCP WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = TCC.CompanyPersonId)) PersonName			  			    
			  ,[Picture]
			  ,[IsActive]
			  ,[UserId]
			  ,(SELECT ISNULL([FName] + '' '','''') + ISNULL([LName],'''') FROM [dbo].[Users] U WHERE U.[UserId] = TCC.[UserId]) RegisterUser
			  ,[Year1]
			  ,[Month1]
			  ,[RegisterDate]
			  ,[RegisterDatePersian]
			  ,[GUId]
			  ,[AppType]
			  ,[LanguageID]
			  ,[FirstRegisterUserId]
			  ,(SELECT ISNULL([FName] + '' '','''') + ISNULL([LName],'''') FROM [dbo].[Users] U WHERE U.[UserId] = TCC.[FirstRegisterUserId]) FirstRegisterUser			  
			  ,[FirstRegisterDatePersian]
			  ,[FirstRegisterDate]
			  /*RZ9610131016- Add UnReadMessageCount*/
			  ,UnReadMessageCount
			  ,LastMessageDate
			  /*RZ961111- Add SettingId*/
			  ,SettingId
			  ,[TelegUserId]/*NKR14010515*/
			  ,(SELECT PhoneNumber FROM CrmTelegSetting TS WHERE TS.TelegSettingId = TCC.SettingId) AS [SettingPhoneNumber]
		  FROM [dbo].[CrmTelegContacts] TCC
		  WHERE ISNULL(TCC.[IsActive],0) = 1
		  AND TCC.[TelegContactId] = ' + CAST(@TelegContactId AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) + ' '
		  
		  IF (ISNULL(@SettingId, 0) <> 0)
			SET @Qry = @Qry + ' AND TCC.[SettingId] =  ' + CAST(@SettingId AS VARCHAR(MAX)) 
		  
		  SET @Ord = ''
	END		
	ELSE IF(@TelegContactId = 0 AND @SearchValue IS NULL)/*all contacts*/
	BEGIN
		SET @Qry = 'SELECT [TelegContactId]
			  ,[ChatId]
			  ,[FirstName]
			  ,[LastName]
			  ,[UserName]
			  ,(ISNULL([FirstName],'''') + ISNULL('' '' + [LastName],'''')) ContactName
			  ,[PhoneNumber]
			  ,[Type]
			  ,[Title]
			  ,[CompanyId]
			  /*,(ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM dbo.CrmCompanyTitle CT WHERE CT.CompanyTitleId = 
					(SELECT cmpTitleId FROM dbo.CrmCompany CC WHERE CC.CompanyID = TCC.CompanyId)), '''') + '' '' +
					(SELECT CC.CmpName FROM dbo.CrmCompany CC WHERE CC.CompanyID = TCC.CompanyId)) CompanyName*/	
			  /*RZ14000617- Change cmpName based on cmpKind*/
			,(SELECT 
				(CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 0
						THEN ((ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle] CT WHERE CT.[CompanyTitleID] = C.[cmpTitleId]),'''') + '' '' + 
								ISNULL(C.[cmpName],'''')))
						ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId),'''')  + '' '' +
									ISNULL(prsFName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(prsLName,'''')
							FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
							WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
							)				
					END)CompanyName				
			FROM  [dbo].[CrmCompany] C
			WHERE C.CompanyID = TCC.CompanyId) CompanyName
			/*RZ14000617- End*/										  		
			  ,[CompanyPersonId]
			  ,(ISNULL((SELECT CPT.PersonTitle FROM dbo.CRMPersonTitle CPT WHERE CPT.PersonTitleID = 
				(SELECT prsTitleId FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons CCP WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = TCC.CompanyPersonId)), '''') + '' '' +
				(SELECT ISNULL(CCP.prsFName, '''') + ISNULL('' '' + CCP.prsLName, '''') 
				FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons CCP WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = TCC.CompanyPersonId)) PersonName			  			    
			  ,[Picture]
			  ,[IsActive]
			  ,[UserId]
			  ,(SELECT ISNULL([FName] + '' '','''') + ISNULL([LName],'''') FROM [dbo].[Users] U WHERE U.[UserId] = TCC.[UserId]) RegisterUser
			  ,[Year1]
			  ,[Month1]
			  ,[RegisterDate]
			  ,[RegisterDatePersian]
			  ,[GUId]
			  ,[AppType]
			  ,[LanguageID]
			  ,[FirstRegisterUserId]
			  ,(SELECT ISNULL([FName] + '' '','''') + ISNULL([LName],'''') FROM [dbo].[Users] U WHERE U.[UserId] = TCC.[FirstRegisterUserId]) FirstRegisterUser			  
			  ,[FirstRegisterDatePersian]
			  ,[FirstRegisterDate]
			  /*RZ9610131016- Add UnReadMessageCount*/
			  ,UnReadMessageCount
			  ,LastMessageDate
			  /*RZ961111- Add SettingId*/
			  ,SettingId
			  ,[TelegUserId]/*NKR14010515*/
			  ,(SELECT PhoneNumber FROM CrmTelegSetting TS WHERE TS.TelegSettingId = TCC.SettingId) AS [SettingPhoneNumber]
		  FROM [dbo].[CrmTelegContacts] TCC
		  WHERE ISNULL(TCC.[IsActive],0) = 1 '

		  IF (ISNULL(@SettingId, 0) <> 0)
			SET @Qry = @Qry + ' AND TCC.[SettingId] =  ' + CAST(@SettingId AS NVARCHAR(MAX))

	END

	BEGIN
		SET @Qry = 'SELECT [TelegContactId]
			  ,[ChatId]
			  ,[FirstName]

			  ,[UserName]

			  ,[PhoneNumber]

					(SELECT CC.CmpName FROM dbo.CrmCompany CC WHERE CC.CompanyID = TCC.CompanyId)) CompanyName*/
					
			  /*RZ14000617- Change cmpName based on cmpKind*/
			,(SELECT 
				(CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 0
						THEN ((ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle] CT WHERE CT.[CompanyTitleID] = C.[cmpTitleId]),'''') + '' '' + 
								ISNULL(C.[cmpName],'''')))
						ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId),'''')  + '' '' +
									ISNULL(prsFName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(prsLName,'''')
							FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
							WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
							)

				(SELECT ISNULL(CCP.prsFName, '''') + ISNULL('' '' + CCP.prsLName, '''')

			  ,[Picture]

			  ,[Year1]
			  ,[Month1]
			  ,[RegisterDate]
			  ,[RegisterDatePersian]
			  ,[GUId]
			  ,[AppType]
			  ,[LanguageID]
			  ,[FirstRegisterUserId]
			  ,(SELECT ISNULL([FName] + '' '','''') + ISNULL([LName],'''') FROM [dbo].[Users] U WHERE U.[UserId] = TCC.[FirstRegisterUserId]) FirstRegisterUser			  
			  ,[FirstRegisterDatePersian]
			  ,[FirstRegisterDate]

			  ,UnReadMessageCount
			  ,LastMessageDate

			  ,(SELECT PhoneNumber FROM CrmTelegSetting TS WHERE TS.TelegSettingId = TCC.SettingId) AS [SettingPhoneNumber]
		  FROM [dbo].[CrmTelegContacts] TCC
		  WHERE ISNULL(TCC.[IsActive],0) = 1
		  AND (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(TCC.[FirstName] AS NVARCHAR(2000)) , NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) 
					Like ''%'' + (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(''' + @SearchValue + '''  AS NVARCHAR(2000)), NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740))+ ''%'')

					Like ''%'' + (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(''' + @SearchValue + ''' AS NVARCHAR(2000)), NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740))+ ''%'')		  		  
		  OR  REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(TCC.[UserName] AS NVARCHAR(2000)) , NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) 
					Like ''%'' + (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(''' + @SearchValue + ''' AS NVARCHAR(2000)), NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740))+ ''%'')		  		  
		  OR  REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(TCC.[Title] AS NVARCHAR(2000)) , NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) 
					Like ''%'' + (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(''' + @SearchValue + ''' AS NVARCHAR(2000)), NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740))+ ''%'')		  		  				  
		  OR  TCC.[PhoneNumber] Like ''%'' + ''' + @SearchValue + '''  + ''%''
		  OR  TCC.[CompanyId] IN (
				SELECT CC.CompanyID FROM dbo.[CrmCompany] CC 
				WHERE REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(/*CC.CmpName */				
				/*RZ14000617- Change cmpName based on cmpKind*/
				ISNULL(
					(CASE WHEN ISNULL(CC.[cmpKind],0) = 0
						THEN (ISNULL(CC.[cmpName],''''))
						ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL(prsFName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(prsLName,'''')
							FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
							WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = CC.[cmpMainPersonId]		
							)				
					END)				
				,'''') 
				/*RZ14000617- End*/
				
				AS NVARCHAR(2000)) , NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) 
					Like ''%'' + (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(''' + @SearchValue + ''' AS NVARCHAR(2000)), NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740))+ ''%'')		  		  
		  )		  
		  OR TCC.[CompanyPersonId] IN(
				SELECT CP.CompanyPersonID FROM dbo.[CrmCompanyPersons] CP 
				WHERE REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(CP.prsFName AS NVARCHAR(2000)) , NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) 
					Like ''%'' + (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(''' + @SearchValue + ''' AS NVARCHAR(2000)), NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740))+ ''%'')		  		  
				OR REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(CP.prsLName AS NVARCHAR(2000)) , NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) 
					Like ''%'' + (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(''' + @SearchValue + ''' AS NVARCHAR(2000)), NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740))+ ''%'')		  		  
				OR  CP.prsMobile Like ''%'' + ''' + @SearchValue + '''  + ''%''												
		  )	)'
		  
		  
		  IF (ISNULL(@SettingId, 0) <> 0)
			SET @Qry = @Qry + ' AND TCC.[SettingId] = ' + CAST(@SettingId AS NVARCHAR(MAX))
		  
		  SET @Ord = ' ORDER BY ISNULL(TCC.[LastMessageDate],TCC.[RegisterDate]) DESC'

				ISNULL(
					(CASE WHEN ISNULL(CC.[cmpKind],0) = 0
						THEN (ISNULL((SELECT CLN.[cmpName] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany_Lang] CLN WHERE CLN.CompanyID = CC.CompanyID AND CLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) + '),''''))
						ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT ISNULL(CCPLN.prsFName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(CCPLN.prsLName,'''') FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons_Lang] CCPLN WHERE CCPLN.CompanyPersonID = CCP.CompanyPersonID AND CCPLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) + '),'''')
							FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
							WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = CC.[cmpMainPersonId]		
							)				
					END)				
				,'''') 
				/*RZ14000617- End*/
				
				 AS NVARCHAR(2000)) , NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) 
					Like ''%'' + (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(''' + @SearchValue + ''' AS NVARCHAR(2000)), NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740))+ ''%'')		  		  
		  )		  
		  OR TCC.[CompanyPersonId] IN(
				SELECT CP.CompanyPersonID FROM dbo.[CrmCompanyPersons] CP 
				WHERE REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(CP.prsFName AS NVARCHAR(2000)) , NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) 
					Like ''%'' + (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(''' + @SearchValue + ''' AS NVARCHAR(2000)), NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740))+ ''%'')		  		  
				OR REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(CP.prsLName AS NVARCHAR(2000)) , NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) 
					Like ''%'' + (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(''' + @SearchValue + ''' AS NVARCHAR(2000)), NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740))+ ''%'')		  		  
				OR  CP.prsMobile Like ''%'' + ''' + @SearchValue + '''  + ''%''												
		  )	
		  OR  TCC.[CompanyId] IN (
				SELECT CC.CompanyID FROM dbo.[CrmCompany_Lang] CC 
				WHERE REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(CC.CmpName AS NVARCHAR(2000)) , NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) 
					Like ''%'' + (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(''' + @SearchValue + ''' AS NVARCHAR(2000)), NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740))+ ''%'')		  		  
				AND CC.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) + '
		  )		  
		  OR TCC.[CompanyPersonId] IN(
				SELECT CP.CompanyPersonID FROM dbo.[CrmCompanyPersons_Lang] CP 
				WHERE REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(CP.prsFName AS NVARCHAR(2000)) , NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) 
					Like ''%'' + (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(''' + @SearchValue + ''' AS NVARCHAR(2000)), NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740))+ ''%'')		  		  
				OR REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(CP.prsLName AS NVARCHAR(2000)) , NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) 
					Like ''%'' + (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(''' + @SearchValue + ''' AS NVARCHAR(2000)), NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740))+ ''%'')		  		  																
				AND CP.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) + '
		  )	)'
		  
		  IF (ISNULL(@SettingId, 0) <> 0)
			SET @Qry = @Qry + ' AND TCC.[SettingId] = ' + CAST(@SettingId AS NVARCHAR(MAX))
		  SET @Ord = ' ORDER BY ISNULL(TCC.[LastMessageDate],TCC.[RegisterDate]) DESC'
	END	
END
/**/
/*RZ961114- Add permission data*/
EXECUTE (@Qry + ' ' + ' AND (TCC.[CompanyId] IS NULL OR TCC.[CompanyId] IN (SELECT C.CompanyId FROM dbo.CrmCompany C WHERE 1=1 '+ @cmpDataPermision + '))' + @Ord)

END







GO
-- [dbo].[CRMspSendCompanyNotifFromRptFilterData] | References permission/temp-filter machinery





/*RZ14000611*/
/*RZ14000621- Change cnvsDesc and Change cmpName based on cmpKind*/
/*RZ14021004- Add OrderView to project*/
Create PROCEDURE [dbo].[CRMspSendCompanyNotifFromRptFilterData]
(
/* 
	exec CRMspSendCompanyNotifFromRptFilterData 		
*/
@MsgLang NVARCHAR(MAX),
@UserId INT,
@RegisterDate DATETIME,
@RegisterDatePersian NVARCHAR(8),
@RegisterDay NVARCHAR(20),
@RegisterTime NVARCHAR(15),
@FirstRegisterDateAddSecond DATETIME,
@InternalMessageYear INT,
@Year1 INT,
@Month1 INT,
@AppType NVARCHAR(20),
@LanguageID INT,
@DefaultLanguageID INT ,
@ErrorMessage NVARCHAR(MAX) OUTPUT
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
SET @ErrorMessage = N''
/*======================== Create table for message ====================*/
DECLARE @IntMsgTmpTable TABLE (
	[NeedErjaRefMsgSubject] NVARCHAR(200),
	[NeedErjaRefMsgBody] NVARCHAR(MAX),	
	[LanguageID] INT
)

IF(ISNULL(@MsgLang,'') <> '')
BEGIN
/**/
	DECLARE @LangId INT = NULL,
			@MsgSubj NVARCHAR(200) = NULL,
			@MsgBdy NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL,
			@RefMsgAllFields NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL,
			@RefMsgField NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL
	WHILE LEN(@MsgLang) > 0 AND (@MsgLang IS NOT NULL)
	BEGIN
		/*@NeedErjaRefMsgBody = N'MsgSubj=subject|@#@|MsgBody=body|@#@|LangId=1065|@#@||^#^|MsgSubj=subject1|@#@|MsgBody=body2|@#@|LangId=1033|@#@||^#^|'*/
		IF PATINDEX('%|^#^|%', @MsgLang) > 0
		BEGIN
			SET @RefMsgAllFields = SUBSTRING(@MsgLang,
							0,
							PATINDEX('%|^#^|%', @MsgLang))
			/*--------------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
			/*---------------------------- BEGIN Fill fields  --------------------------------*/
			/*BEGIN PERSON*/
			DECLARE @RMAllFields NVARCHAR(MAX)
			SET @RMAllFields = @RefMsgAllFields        
			/****************  set null field **************/         
			SET @LangId  = NULL
			SET @MsgSubj  = NULL
			SET @MsgBdy  = NULL			                
			/**/
			WHILE LEN(@RMAllFields) > 0
			BEGIN
			IF PATINDEX('%|@#@|%', @RMAllFields) > 0
			BEGIN
				SET @RefMsgField = SUBSTRING(@RMAllFields,
									0,
									PATINDEX('%|@#@|%', @RMAllFields))
				/*--------------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
				DECLARE @RefMsgFieldValue NVARCHAR(MAX)
				DECLARE @RefMsgFieldName NVARCHAR(200)
				SET @RefMsgFieldName = SUBSTRING(@RefMsgField, 1, CHARINDEX('=',@RefMsgField)-1)                                    
				SET @RefMsgFieldValue = SUBSTRING(@RefMsgField, CHARINDEX('=', @RefMsgField)+1, Len(@RefMsgField))
				/*--------------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
				/*---------------------------- BEGIN Fill variabele ------------------------------*/
				/*BEGIN*/
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @RefMsgFieldName = 'MsgSubj'
				BEGIN
					SELECT @MsgSubj = CASE WHEN @RefMsgFieldValue = '' THEN NULL ELSE LEFT (@RefMsgFieldValue, 200)  END
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @RefMsgFieldName = 'MsgBody'
				BEGIN
					SELECT @MsgBdy = CASE WHEN @RefMsgFieldValue = '' THEN NULL ELSE LEFT ((REPLACE(@RefMsgFieldValue , '<BR>', CHAR(13))), 2000) END /*CHAR(10) + */
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @RefMsgFieldName = 'LangId'
				BEGIN
					SELECT @LangId = CASE WHEN @RefMsgFieldValue = '' THEN NULL ELSE CAST(@RefMsgFieldValue AS INT) END
				END
		
				/*END */
				/*---------------------------- END Fill variabele --------------------------------*/
				SET @RMAllFields = SUBSTRING(@RMAllFields,
								  LEN(@RefMsgField + '|@#@|') + 1,
								  LEN(@RMAllFields))                                                                                                      
		END
			ELSE
			BEGIN
				SET @RefMsgField = @RMAllFields
				SET @RMAllFields = NULL				
			END
		END  
		/************* Insert to tmp table***************/
		INSERT INTO @IntMsgTmpTable 
			 ([NeedErjaRefMsgSubject],
				[NeedErjaRefMsgBody],
				[LanguageID]				
			  )
		VALUES
			  ( @MsgSubj,
				@MsgBdy,
				@LangId					
			)                                              
		/*---------------------------- END Fill fields ------------------------------------*/
		/*---------------------------- Remove current parameter from listparam ------------*/
		SET @MsgLang = SUBSTRING(@MsgLang,
								  LEN(@RefMsgAllFields + '|^#^|') + 1,
								  LEN(@MsgLang))                                  
	END
		ELSE
		BEGIN
			SET @RefMsgAllFields = @MsgLang
			SET @MsgLang = NULL       
		END	
		
	END	
/**/
END
/*======================================================================*/
/*================= Create table base on company Report ================*/
DECLARE @CmpTmpTable TABLE (
	[CompanyID] INT,
	[CompanyPersonId] INT,
	[SendNotify] BIT
)

DECLARE @Term VARCHAR(MAX) ='', @Qry VARCHAR(MAX) = '',@Txt VARCHAR(MAX) = ''
/*========================================================*/

SELECT @Txt=Txt FROM dbo.CrmsysDBTempInfo WHERE UserId = @UserId AND TypeId = 1
IF(ISNULL(@Txt,'') <> '')
BEGIN
	SET @Txt = ' AND (CC.CompanyID IN ((SELECT CompanyId FROM (' + @Txt +') TT))) '
END
/*========================================================*/
SET @Qry = @Qry + 'SELECT DISTINCT 
	CC.CompanyID,	
	(CASE WHEN ISNULL([cmpMainPersonId],0) > 0 
		THEN [cmpMainPersonId]
		ELSE (SELECT TOP 1 CompanyPersonID 
				FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons CP WHERE CP.prsCompanyId = CC.CompanyId AND ISNULL(CP.[prsWorkingOut], 0)  = 0
				ORDER BY ISNULL(prsOrderView, CompanyPersonID))
	END) CompanyPersonId 
FROM dbo.CrmCompany CC
WHERE 1 = 1 '

INSERT INTO @CmpTmpTable([CompanyID],[CompanyPersonId])
EXECUTE(@Qry + @Txt + @Term)
/*========================================================*/
DECLARE @CompanyId INT,@CompanyPersonId INT

/*--------------- Get fields from MobSetting -------------*/
DECLARE @CmpNotifPrjTitle NVARCHAR(100),
@CmpNotifPrjCnvsStatusGroupId INT,@CmpNotifCnvsTopicId INT,
@CmpNotifCnvsReferUserId INT,@CmpNotifCnvsStatusId INT,
@CmpNotifCnvsDesc NVARCHAR(2000)

SELECT TOP 1
       @CmpNotifPrjTitle = [CmpNotifPrjTitle]
      ,@CmpNotifPrjCnvsStatusGroupId = [CmpNotifPrjCnvsStatusGroupId]
      ,@CmpNotifCnvsTopicId = [CmpNotifCnvsTopicId]
      ,@CmpNotifCnvsReferUserId = [CmpNotifCnvsReferUserId]
      ,@CmpNotifCnvsStatusId = [CmpNotifCnvsStatusId]
      ,@CmpNotifCnvsDesc = [CmpNotifCnvsDesc]      
FROM [dbo].[CrmMobSetting]

/*RZ14000621- Change cnvsDesc*/
SET @CmpNotifCnvsDesc = (SELECT TOP 1 Tmp.[NeedErjaRefMsgBody] FROM @IntMsgTmpTable Tmp WHERE Tmp.LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID)	
/*RZ14000621- END*/

IF(ISNULL(@CmpNotifPrjTitle,'') = '' OR 
	ISNULL(@CmpNotifPrjCnvsStatusGroupId,0) = 0 OR
	ISNULL(@CmpNotifCnvsTopicId,0) = 0 OR
	ISNULL(@CmpNotifCnvsReferUserId,0) = 0 OR
	ISNULL(@CmpNotifCnvsStatusId,0) = 0 OR
	ISNULL(@CmpNotifCnvsDesc,'') = '')
BEGIN
/**/
	SET @ErrorMessage = N'NoSetting' 
/**/
END

/*========================================================*/
IF(ISNULL(@ErrorMessage,'') = '')
BEGIN
/**/
	/*========================================================*/
	BEGIN TRANSACTION Main
	BEGIN TRY	
		/*======================================================================*/		
		DECLARE Crsr CURSOR LOCAL FOR
		/**/	
			SELECT 
				 [CompanyID]
				,[CompanyPersonId]
			FROM @CmpTmpTable
			WHERE [CompanyPersonId] IS NOT NULL
		/**/
		OPEN Crsr
		FETCH NEXT FROM Crsr INTO @CompanyId,@CompanyPersonId
		WHILE @@Fetch_Status = 0
		BEGIN
		/**/			
			/*================================================================================*/
			DECLARE @ProjectId INT = NULL, @ConversationId INT = NULL
			/*-------------------------- Project -------------------------------*/
			SET @ProjectId =(SELECT TOP 1 P.ProjectID FROM [dbo].[CrmProjects] P 
							 WHERE P.prjCompanyId = @CompanyId 
							 AND (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(P.prjTitle AS NVARCHAR(2000)) ,
										NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) = /* Like '%' +*/ 
										REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(@CmpNotifPrjTitle AS NVARCHAR(2000)), NCHAR(1740),
										NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) /*+'%'*/))
				
			IF(ISNULL(@ProjectId,0) = 0)
			BEGIN
			/**/
				/*-------------------------------------------------*/
				/*RZ14021004- Add OrderView to project*/
				DECLARE @prjOrderView INT = 0
				EXEC [dbo].[CRMspReturnCompanyProjectOrderView]
				@CompanyId  = @CompanyId,
				@prjOrderView = @prjOrderView  OUTPUT					
				/*RZ14021004- End*/
				/*-------------------------------------------------*/
				INSERT INTO [dbo].[CrmProjects]
					([prjCompanyId]
					,[prjTitle]
					,[prjStartDate]
					,[prjStartDay]
					,[prjStartTime]
					,[prjStartDateTime]
					,[prjStarterUserId]
					,[prjFirstRegisterDate]
					,[prjFirstRegisterUserId]
					,[UserId]
					,[Year1]
					,[Month1]
					,[RegisterDate]
					,[RegisterDatePersian]
					,[AppType]
					,[LanguageID]
					,[FirstRegisterUserId]
					,[FirstRegisterDateTime]
					,[FirstRegisterDatePersian]
					,[prjFirstRegisterDateTime]
					,[FirstRegisterDate]
					,[prjcnvsStatusGroupId]
					,[prjOrderView]/*RZ14021004- Add OrderView to project*/)
				VALUES
					(@CompanyId
					,@CmpNotifPrjTitle
					,@RegisterDatePersian
					,@RegisterDay
					,@RegisterTime
					,@RegisterDate
					,@UserId
					,@RegisterDatePersian
					,@UserId
					,@UserId
					,CAST(@Year1 AS SMALLINT)
					,@Month1
					,@RegisterDate
					,@RegisterDatePersian
					,@AppType
					,@DefaultLanguageID
					,@UserId
					,@RegisterDate
					,@RegisterDatePersian
					,@RegisterDate
					,@RegisterDate
					,@CmpNotifPrjCnvsStatusGroupId
					,@prjOrderView/*RZ14021004- Add OrderView to project*/)
			SET @ProjectId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()
			/**/
			INSERT INTO [dbo].[CrmProjects_Lang]
				([ProjectID]
				,[LanguageID]
				,[prjTitle])
			SELECT 
					@ProjectId
				,L.LanguageID
				,@CmpNotifPrjTitle			
			FROM [dbo].[Languages] L
			WHERE L.LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID
			AND ISNULL(L.IsActive,0) = 1			
			/**/
			END

			/*-------------------------- Conversation ---------------------------*/
			IF(ISNULL(@ProjectId,0) > 0)
			BEGIN
			/*-------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
				INSERT INTO [dbo].[CrmConversation]
					([cnvsProjectId]
					,[cnvsCompanyId]
					,[cnvsDate]
					,[cnvsDay]
					,[cnvsTime]
					,[cnvsDateTime]
					,[cnvsTopicId]
					,[cnvsDesc]
					,[cnvsPersonId]
					,[cnvsUserId]
					,[cnvsReferUserId]
					,[cnvsStatusId]					
					,[cnvsRemindDate]
					,[cnvsRemindDateTime]
					,[cnvsFirstRegisterUserId]
					,[cnvsFirstRegisterDate]
					,[cnvsFirstRegisterDateTime]
					,[UserId]
					,[Year1]
					,[Month1]
					,[RegisterDate]
					,[RegisterDatePersian]
					,[FirstRegisterDate]
					,[AppType]
					,[LanguageID]
					,[FirstRegisterUserId]
					,[FirstRegisterDatePersian])
				VALUES
					(@ProjectId
					,@CompanyId
					,@RegisterDatePersian
					,@RegisterDay
					,@RegisterTime
					,@RegisterDate
					,@CmpNotifCnvsTopicId
					,@CmpNotifCnvsDesc
					,@CompanyPersonId
					,@UserId
					,@CmpNotifCnvsReferUserId
					,@CmpNotifCnvsStatusId					
					,@RegisterDatePersian
					,DATEADD(dd, 0, DATEDIFF(dd, 0, @RegisterDate))
					,@UserId
					,@RegisterDatePersian
					,@RegisterDate
					,@UserId
					,@Year1
					,@Month1
					,@RegisterDate
					,@RegisterDatePersian
					,@RegisterDate
					,@AppType
					,@LanguageID
					,@UserId
					,@RegisterDatePersian)					
				SET @ConversationId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()	   
				/*=================== Insert Lang Table==============*/
				/*INSERT INTO dbo.CrmConversation_Lang 
					([ConversationID]
						,[LanguageID]
						,[cnvsDesc]) 
				SELECT
					@ConversationId,
					LN.LanguageID,
					@CmpNotifCnvsDesc
				FROM dbo.Languages LN 
				WHERE LN.LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID  				
				AND ISNULL(LN.IsActive,0) =1*/
				
				/*RZ14000621- Change cnvsDesc*/
				IF(ISNULL(@ConversationId,0) > 0 )
				BEGIN
					INSERT INTO dbo.CrmConversation_Lang 
						([ConversationID]
							,[LanguageID]
							,[cnvsDesc]) 
					SELECT
						@ConversationId,
						Tmp.LanguageID,
						Tmp.NeedErjaRefMsgBody
					FROM @IntMsgTmpTable Tmp 	
					WHERE Tmp.LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID	

				END
				/*RZ14000621- END*/
				
				    
				/*----------------------------------------------------------------------*/
				/*------------------------- Send InternalMessage -----------------------*/
				DECLARE	@InsertedIntMsgId INT = NULL,		
					@NeedErjaRefMsgSubjectInDefLang NVARCHAR(200) = NULL,
					@NeedErjaRefMsgBodyInDefLang NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL
				SET @NeedErjaRefMsgSubjectInDefLang = (SELECT TOP 1 Tmp.[NeedErjaRefMsgSubject] FROM @IntMsgTmpTable Tmp WHERE Tmp.LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID)	
				SET @NeedErjaRefMsgBodyInDefLang = (SELECT TOP 1 Tmp.[NeedErjaRefMsgBody] FROM @IntMsgTmpTable Tmp WHERE Tmp.LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID)	
				IF(ISNULL(@NeedErjaRefMsgBodyInDefLang,'') <> '')
				BEGIN	
				/**/
					EXEC [dbo].[CRMspInternalMessageSendSingle]
					@msgMasterId = NULL,
					@msgSubject = @NeedErjaRefMsgSubjectInDefLang,
					@msgBody = @NeedErjaRefMsgBodyInDefLang,					
					@msgdTypeId = 10,
					@msgdFromUserId = @UserId,
					@msgdToUserId = @CmpNotifCnvsReferUserId,
					@msgdSendDate = @RegisterDatePersian,
					@msgdSendTime = @RegisterTime,
					@msgdSendDateTime = @RegisterDate,
					@msgdPriorityId = 1,
					@msgdAttachFile = '',
					@msgdAttachRecId = @ConversationId,
					@msgdAttachRecDesc = '',
					@msgdGroupId = NULL,
					@UserId = @UserId,
					@Year1 = @InternalMessageYear,
					@Month1 = @Month1,
					@RegisterDate = @RegisterDate,
					@RegisterDatePersian = @RegisterDatePersian,
					@AppType = @AppType,
					@InsertedID = @InsertedIntMsgId OUTPUT,
					@LanguageID = @LanguageID											
						
					/*=================== Insert Lang Table==============*/	
					IF(@InsertedIntMsgId > 0)
					BEGIN
						INSERT INTO dbo.CrmInternalMessage_Lang (
							[InternalMessageID]
							,[LanguageID]
							,[msgSubject]
							,[msgBody])			    			  					
						SELECT
							@InsertedIntMsgId,
							Tmp.LanguageID,
							Tmp.NeedErjaRefMsgSubject,
							Tmp.NeedErjaRefMsgBody
						FROM @IntMsgTmpTable Tmp 	
						WHERE Tmp.LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID								
					END
				END
			/*-------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
			END				
			/*================================================================================*/					    				 					
		/**/	
		FETCH NEXT FROM Crsr INTO @CompanyId,@CompanyPersonId
		END
		CLOSE Crsr
		DEALLOCATE Crsr
	
		/**/
		COMMIT TRANSACTION Main 
		
	END TRY
	BEGIN CATCH
		ROLLBACK TRANSACTION Main 		
		SET @ErrorMessage = N'Error'
	END CATCH
	/*========================================================*/
/**/
END

/*========================================================*/
/*========================================================*/

/*=========== Return list of company not send notif ===========*/

IF(@LanguageID IS NULL OR @LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID)
BEGIN
	SELECT 
		 [CompanyId]
		,[cmpNumber]
		/*,(ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle] CT WHERE CT.[CompanyTitleID] = C.[cmpTitleId]),'') + ' ' + 
		  ISNULL(C.[cmpName],''))  CompanyName	*/		
		/*RZ14000621- Change cmpName based on cmpKind*/
		,(CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 0
			THEN ((ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle] CT WHERE CT.[CompanyTitleID] = C.[cmpTitleId]),'') + ' ' + 
					ISNULL(C.[cmpName],'')))
			ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId),'')  + ' ' +
						ISNULL(prsFName, '') + ' ' + ISNULL(prsLName,'')
				FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
				WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
				)				
		END) CompanyName
		/*RZ14000621- End*/
		,[cmpMainPersonId]
		,(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId),'')  + ' ' +
				 ISNULL(prsFName, '') + ' ' + ISNULL(prsLName,'')
		  FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
		  WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
		 ) [cmpMainPerson]
		,[cmpEmail]
		,[cmpMobile]		
	FROM  [dbo].[CrmCompany] C  
	WHERE C.[CompanyID] IN(SELECT [CompanyId] 
							FROM @CmpTmpTable 
							WHERE [CompanyPersonId] IS NULL)
	
END
ELSE IF(@LanguageID IS NOT NULL AND @LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID)
BEGIN
	SELECT 
		 [CompanyId]
		,[cmpNumber]
		/*,(ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle_Lang] CT WHERE CT.[CompanyTitleID] = C.[cmpTitleId] AND CT.LanguageID = @LanguageID),'') + ' ' + 
		  ISNULL((SELECT TOP 1 [cmpName] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany_Lang] CLN WHERE CLN.CompanyID = C.CompanyID AND CLN.LanguageID = @LanguageID ),''))  CompanyName		*/
		 /*RZ14000621- Change cmpName based on cmpKind*/
		,(CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 0
			THEN ((ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle_Lang] CT WHERE CT.[CompanyTitleID] = C.[cmpTitleId] AND CT.LanguageID = @LanguageID),'') + ' ' + 
					ISNULL((SELECT CLN.[cmpName] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany_Lang] CLN WHERE CLN.CompanyID = C.CompanyID AND CLN.LanguageID = @LanguageID),'')))
			ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle_Lang] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId AND PT.LanguageID = @LanguageID),'')  + ' ' +
						ISNULL((SELECT ISNULL(CCPLN.prsFName, '') + ' ' + ISNULL(CCPLN.prsLName,'') FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons_Lang] CCPLN WHERE CCPLN.CompanyPersonID = CCP.CompanyPersonID AND CCPLN.LanguageID = @LanguageID),'')
				FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
				WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
				)				
		END)CompanyName	
		/*RZ14000621- End*/
		,[cmpMainPersonId]
		,(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle_Lang] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId AND PT.LanguageID = @LanguageID),'')  + ' ' +
				 ISNULL((SELECT ISNULL(CCPLN.prsFName, '') + ' ' + ISNULL(CCPLN.prsLName,'') FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons_Lang] CCPLN WHERE CCPLN.CompanyPersonID = CCP.CompanyPersonID AND CCPLN.LanguageID = @LanguageID),'')
		  FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
		  WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
		 ) [cmpMainPerson]
		,[cmpEmail]
		,[cmpMobile]		
	FROM  [dbo].[CrmCompany] C  
	WHERE C.[CompanyID] IN(SELECT [CompanyId] 
							FROM @CmpTmpTable 
							WHERE [CompanyPersonId] IS NULL)
END
/*========================================================*/
/*======================================================================*/
/**/
END

-- [dbo].[CRMspSetcmpDataPermission] | Exact target

/*--ADBF9302039410*/
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[CRMspSetcmpDataPermission]
(		/*--	EXEC CRMspSetcmpDataPermission 3 */
@UserId INT
)
AS
BEGIN
DECLARE @Term VARCHAR(4000)
SET @Term = '';
/*--Province*/
IF  (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM dbo.CrmCompanyForbiddenProvince
WHERE CrmUserId = @UserId ) > 0
SET @Term = @Term +
' AND C.cmpProvinceId Not IN (
SELECT ForbiddenProvinceId FROM dbo.CrmCompanyForbiddenProvince
WHERE CrmUserId = '+CAST(@UserId AS VARCHAR(10))+')'
IF  (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM dbo.CrmCompanyJustAccessProvince
WHERE CrmUserId = @UserId ) > 0
SET @Term = @Term +
' AND C.cmpProvinceId IN (
SELECT JustAccessProvinceId FROM dbo.CrmCompanyJustAccessProvince
WHERE CrmUserId = '+CAST(@UserId AS VARCHAR(10))+')'
/*--City*/
IF  (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM dbo.CrmCompanyForbiddenCity
WHERE CrmUserId = @UserId ) > 0
SET @Term = @Term +
' AND C.cmpCityId Not IN (
SELECT ForbiddenCityId FROM dbo.CrmCompanyForbiddenCity
WHERE CrmUserId = '+CAST(@UserId AS VARCHAR(10))+')'
IF  (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM dbo.CrmCompanyJustAccessCity
WHERE CrmUserId = @UserId ) > 0
SET @Term = @Term +
' AND C.cmpCityId IN (
SELECT JustAccessCityId FROM dbo.CrmCompanyJustAccessCity
WHERE CrmUserId = '+CAST(@UserId AS VARCHAR(10))+')'
/*--GroupIV*/
IF  (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM dbo.CrmCompanyForbiddenGroupIV
WHERE CrmUserId = @UserId ) > 0
SET @Term = @Term +
' AND IsNull(C.cmpGroupIdIV, 0) Not IN (
SELECT ForbiddenGroupIVId FROM dbo.CrmCompanyForbiddenGroupIV
WHERE CrmUserId = '+CAST(@UserId AS VARCHAR(10))+')'
IF  (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM dbo.CrmCompanyJustAccessGroupIV
WHERE CrmUserId = @UserId ) > 0
SET @Term = @Term +
' AND IsNull(C.cmpGroupIdIV, 0) IN (
SELECT JustAccessGroupIVId FROM dbo.CrmCompanyJustAccessGroupIV
WHERE CrmUserId = '+CAST(@UserId AS VARCHAR(10))+')'
/*--Company*/
IF  (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM dbo.CrmCompanyForbiddenCompany
WHERE CrmUserId = @UserId ) > 0
SET @Term = @Term +
' AND C.CompanyId Not IN (
SELECT ForbiddenCompanyId FROM dbo.CrmCompanyForbiddenCompany
WHERE CrmUserId = '+CAST(@UserId AS VARCHAR(10))+')'
IF  (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM dbo.CrmCompanyJustAccessCompany
WHERE CrmUserId = @UserId ) > 0
SET @Term = @Term +
' AND C.CompanyId IN (
SELECT JustAccessCompanyId FROM dbo.CrmCompanyJustAccessCompany
WHERE CrmUserId = '+CAST(@UserId AS VARCHAR(10))+')'
/*--Group*/
IF  (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM dbo.CrmCompanyForbiddenGroup
WHERE CrmUserId = @UserId ) > 0
SET @Term = @Term +
' AND IsNull(C.cmpGroupId, 0) Not IN (
SELECT ForbiddenGroupId FROM dbo.CrmCompanyForbiddenGroup
WHERE CrmUserId = '+CAST(@UserId AS VARCHAR(10))+')'
IF  (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM dbo.CrmCompanyJustAccessGroup
WHERE CrmUserId = @UserId ) > 0
SET @Term = @Term +
' AND IsNull(C.cmpGroupId, 0) IN (
SELECT JustAccessGroupId FROM dbo.CrmCompanyJustAccessGroup
WHERE CrmUserId = '+CAST(@UserId AS VARCHAR(10))+')'
/*--SubGroup*/
IF  (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM dbo.CrmCompanyForbiddenSubGroup
WHERE CrmUserId = @UserId ) > 0
SET @Term = @Term +
' AND IsNull(C.cmpSubGroupId, 0) Not IN (
SELECT ForbiddenSubGroupId FROM dbo.CrmCompanyForbiddenSubGroup
WHERE CrmUserId = '+CAST(@UserId AS VARCHAR(10))+')'
IF  (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM dbo.CrmCompanyJustAccessSubGroup
WHERE CrmUserId = @UserId ) > 0
SET @Term = @Term +
' AND IsNull(C.cmpSubGroupId, 0) IN (
SELECT JustAccessSubGroupId FROM dbo.CrmCompanyJustAccessSubGroup
WHERE CrmUserId = '+CAST(@UserId AS VARCHAR(10))+')'
/*--*/
/*--cmpReferUser*/
SET @Term = @Term +
' And C.cmpReferUserId IN( 
(SELECT '+CAST(@UserId AS VARCHAR(10)) + ' UNION 
SELECT LinkedUserId FROM dbo.CrmLinkedUsers WHERE CrmUserId = '+ 
CAST(@UserId AS VARCHAR(10)) + ' AND LinkedTypeId = 14 ) ) '
/*
--
--
--
--
*/
DELETE dbo.CrmsysDBTempInfo
WHERE TypeId = 20
AND UserId = @UserId
/*--*/
INSERT INTO dbo.CrmsysDBTempInfo (
	UserId,	TXT, TypeId)
 VALUES ( @UserId, @Term, 20 ) 
END

-- [dbo].[CRMspSetCompanyDiscountCodeFromRptFilterData] | References permission/temp-filter machinery






/*RZ14021114*/
Create PROCEDURE [dbo].[CRMspSetCompanyDiscountCodeFromRptFilterData]
(
/* 
	exec CRMspSetCompanyDiscountCodeFromRptFilterData 		
*/
@DisTitle NVARCHAR(300),
@DisTitle_Lang NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL,
@DisCode NVARCHAR(50),
@DisAmount NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
@DisAmountPercent NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
@DisExpDate DATETIME,
@DisExpDatePersian NVARCHAR(8),
@DisPrdId INT = NULL,
@DisUseCnt INT = NULL,
@ScoreRate FLOAT = NULL,
@UserId INT,
@RegisterDate DATETIME,
@RegisterDatePersian NVARCHAR(8),
@FPId INT,
@Month1 INT,
@AppType NVARCHAR(20),
@LanguageID INT,
@DefaultLanguageID INT ,
@ErrorMessage NVARCHAR(MAX) OUTPUT
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
SET @ErrorMessage = N''
/*======================================================================*/
/*================= Create table base on company Report ================*/
DECLARE @CmpTmpTable TABLE (
	[CompanyID] INT
)

DECLARE @Term VARCHAR(MAX) ='', @Qry VARCHAR(MAX) = '',@Txt VARCHAR(MAX) = ''
/*========================================================*/

SELECT @Txt=Txt FROM dbo.CrmsysDBTempInfo WHERE UserId = @UserId AND TypeId = 1
IF(ISNULL(@Txt,'') <> '')
BEGIN
	SET @Txt = ' AND (CC.CompanyID IN ((SELECT CompanyId FROM (' + @Txt +') TT))) '
END
/*========================================================*/
SET @Qry = @Qry + 'SELECT DISTINCT 
	CC.CompanyID 
FROM dbo.CrmCompany CC
WHERE 1 = 1 '

INSERT INTO @CmpTmpTable([CompanyID])
EXECUTE(@Qry + @Txt + @Term)
/*========================================================*/
DECLARE @CompanyId INT
/*========================================================*/
IF(ISNULL(@ErrorMessage,'') = '')
BEGIN
/**/
	/*========================================================*/
	BEGIN TRANSACTION Main
	BEGIN TRY	
		/*======================================================================*/		
		DECLARE CrsrCreateDisCode CURSOR LOCAL FOR
		/**/	
			SELECT 
				 [CompanyID]
			FROM @CmpTmpTable			
		/**/
		OPEN CrsrCreateDisCode
		FETCH NEXT FROM CrsrCreateDisCode INTO @CompanyId
		WHILE @@Fetch_Status = 0
		BEGIN
		/**/			
			/*================================================================================*/
			DECLARE @DiscountCodeId INT = NULL
			
			INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSDiscountCode]
				([DisTitle]
				,[DisCode]
				,[DisAmount]
				,[DisAmountPercent]
				,[DisExpDate]
				,[DisExpDatePersian]
				,[DisCmpId]
				,[DisPrdId]
				,[DisUseCnt]
				,[FirstRegisterUserId]
				,[FirstRegisterDate]
				,[FirstRegisterDatePersian]
				,[UserId]
				,[FPId]
				,[Month1]
				,[RegisterDate]
				,[RegisterDatePersian]				
				,[AppType]
				,[LanguageID]
				,[ScoreRate])
			VALUES
				(@DisTitle
				,@DisCode
				,@DisAmount
				,@DisAmountPercent
				,@DisExpDate
				,@DisExpDatePersian
				,@CompanyId
				,@DisPrdId
				,@DisUseCnt
				,@UserId
				,@RegisterDate
				,@RegisterDatePersian
				,@UserId
				,@FPId
				,@Month1
				,@RegisterDate
				,@RegisterDatePersian
				,@AppType
				,@DefaultLanguageID
				,@ScoreRate)

			SET @DiscountCodeId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()	   
			IF(ISNULL(@DiscountCodeId,0) > 0)
			BEGIN
			/**/
				IF (ISNULL(@DisTitle_Lang,'[]') <> '[]'  AND ISNULL(@DisTitle_Lang, '') <> '' AND (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM OPENJSON(ISNULL(@DisTitle_Lang,'[]'))) > 0)
				BEGIN
				/**/
					INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSDiscountCode_Lang]
					   ([DiscountCodeId]
					   ,[LanguageID]
					   ,[DisTitle])
					SELECT
						@DiscountCodeId,
						JS.[LanguageId],
						JS.[Value]
					FROM OPENJSON (ISNULL(@DisTitle_Lang,'[]'))
					WITH(
						[LanguageId] INT '$.LanguageId',
						[Value] NVARCHAR(300) '$.Value'
					)JS
					WHERE JS.[LanguageId] <> @DefaultLanguageID	
				/**/
				END
			/**/
			END
			/*================================================================================*/					    				 					
		/**/	
		FETCH NEXT FROM CrsrCreateDisCode INTO @CompanyId
		END
		CLOSE CrsrCreateDisCode
		DEALLOCATE CrsrCreateDisCode	
		/**/
		COMMIT TRANSACTION Main 
		
	END TRY
	BEGIN CATCH
		ROLLBACK TRANSACTION Main 		
		SET @ErrorMessage = N'Error'
	END CATCH
	/*========================================================*/
/**/
END
/*======================================================================*/
/**/
END









GO

-- [dbo].[CRMspSmsSelectReceiveSMS] | References permission/temp-filter machinery





/*Added By ADBF 9605 */
/*RZ960317 For multi language*/
/*RZ960525- Edit rptUserName in multi language*/
/*RZ990505- Add Permission*/
/*RZ14000618- Change cmpName based on cmpKind*/
/*RZ14000629- Modify TDate + 1*/
Create PROCEDURE [dbo].[CRMspSmsSelectReceiveSMS]
/* 
	Exec CRMspSmsSelectReceiveSMS '2003-03-20 00:00:00', '2021-09-09 23:59:59', N'', 0, 1033, 1065, 3, '''', '''', '''', ''''	
*/
(
@Fdate DateTime,
@TDate DateTime,
@Value NVARCHAR(500),
@IsLike BIT,
@LanguageID INT = NULL,
@DefaultLanguageID INT = NULL,
/*Added By ADBF 9605*/
@UserId int, 
@rptDate varchar(50), 
@rptDay NVarChar(50),
@FDateFa NVarChar(50), 
@TDateFa NVarChar(50)
)
AS 
BEGIN
/**/
/*RZ990505- Add Permission*/
EXEC CRMspSetcmpDataPermission @UserId
DECLARE @cmpDataPermision VARCHAR(MAX),@Qry VARCHAR(MAX) = ''
SELECT @cmpDataPermision = TXT
FROM dbo.CrmsysDBTempInfo
WHERE TypeId = 20
AND UserId = @UserId
/**/
DELETE dbo.CrmsysDBTempInfo
WHERE TypeId = 10
AND UserId = @UserId
/**/	
SET @cmpDataPermision = ' AND (ISNULL(CompanyId,0) = 0 OR CompanyId IN(SELECT CompanyId FROM dbo.CrmCompany C WHERE 1 = 1 ' + @cmpDataPermision + ' )' +
 ' AND (ISNULL(CompanyPersonId,0) = 0 OR CompanyPersonId IN( SELECT CompanyPersonId FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons P WHERE P.FirstRegisterUserId IN (SELECT ' + CAST(@UserId AS VARCHAR(MAX)) + ' UNION 
									SELECT LinkedUserId FROM dbo.CrmLinkedUsers WHERE CrmUserId = ' + CAST(@UserId AS VARCHAR(MAX)) + ' AND LinkedTypeId = 23)))' +  
') '
/*RZ990505- END*/

/*RZ990505- Add Permission*/
IF(@LanguageID IS NULL OR @LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID)
BEGIN
	IF @Value = ''
	BEGIN
		SET @Qry = 'SELECT 
			/*Added By ADBF 9605 B..*/
			''' + @rptDate + ''' rptDate,''' + @rptDay + ''' rptDay,' + CAST(@UserID AS VARCHAR(50))   + ' rptUserId, (SELECT ISNULL(FName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(LName , '''')
			From Users Us Where Us.UserId = ' + CAST(@UserID AS VARCHAR(50)) + ') rptUserName, 
			CAST(''' + CAST(@Fdate AS VARCHAR(50)) + ''' AS DATETIME) FDate, CAST(''' + CAST(@TDate AS VARCHAR(50)) + '''AS DATETIME) TDate, 
			'''+@FDateFa +''' FDateFa, ''' +@TDateFa+''' TDateFa, 
			/*Added By ADBF 9605 E..*/
			Id, 
			OrderNum, 
			SmsNumber, 
			SmsDesc, 
			GetId,
			FromNumber, 
			ReceiveDate, 
			ReceiveDatePersian, 
			ReceiveDatePersianPreview,
			SendDate, 
			SendDatePersian, 
			SendDatePersianPreview, 
			RegisterDate, 
			RegisterDatePersian, 
			CompanyPersonId, 
			CompanyId, 
			ReceiveDay, 
			ReceiveTime, 
			UserId, 
			Year1,	
			Month1, 
			ISNULL(((SELECT PersonTitle FROM dbo.CrmPersonTitle WHERE PersonTitleId = 
				(SELECT prsTitleId FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons CP WHERE CP.CompanyPersonId = R.CompanyPersonId))) + '' ''	, '''')  +
				ISNULL((SELECT prsFName FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons CP WHERE CP.CompanyPersonId = R.CompanyPersonId)+ '' '' , '''') +
				ISNULL((SELECT prsLName FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons CP WHERE CP.CompanyPersonId = R.CompanyPersonId) , '''') 
			PersonInfo,
			/*ISNULL(((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM dbo.CrmCompanyTitle WHERE CompanyTitleID = 
				(SELECT cmpTitleId FROM dbo.CrmCompany WHERE CompanyId = R.CompanyId)))+ '' '' , '''')  +
				ISNULL((SELECT CmpName FROM dbo.CrmCompany WHERE CompanyId = R.CompanyId)  , '''') 
			CompanyInfo*/

			/*RZ14000618- Change cmpName based on cmpKind*/
			(SELECT 
				(CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 0
					   THEN ((ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle] CT WHERE CT.[CompanyTitleID] = C.[cmpTitleId]),'''') + '' '' + 
							  ISNULL(C.[cmpName],'''')))
					   ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId),'''')  + '' '' +
									ISNULL(prsFName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(prsLName,'''')
							FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
							WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
							)				
				 END)CompanyName				
			FROM  [dbo].[CrmCompany] C
			WHERE C.CompanyID = R.CompanyId) CompanyInfo 
			/*RZ14000618- End*/

		FROM SmsReceive R
		/*WHERE SendDate BETWEEN ''' + CAST(@Fdate AS VARCHAR(50)) + ''' AND ''' + CAST((@TDate+1) AS VARCHAR(50)) + '''*/
		WHERE SendDate BETWEEN ''' + CAST(@Fdate AS VARCHAR(50)) + ''' AND ''' + CAST((@TDate) AS VARCHAR(50)) + ''' /*RZ14000629- Modify TDate + 1*/
		AND IsDeleted IS NULL' +@cmpDataPermision+ '/*RZ990505*/
		/*--ORDER BY ReceiveDatePersian	*/
		/*Added By ADBF 9605 */
		ORDER BY SendDate Desc'
	END	
	ELSE
	IF @Value <> ''
	BEGIN
		IF @IsLike = 1
		BEGIN
			SET @Qry = 'SELECT 
				/*Added By ADBF 9605 B..*/
				'''+@rptDate+''' rptDate, '''+@rptDay+''' rptDay, '+CAST(@UserID AS VARCHAR(50))+' rptUserId, (SELECT ISNULL(FName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(LName , '' '')
				From Users Us Where Us.UserId = '+CAST(@UserID AS VARCHAR(50))+') rptUserName, 
				CAST(''' + CAST(@Fdate AS VARCHAR(50)) + '''AS DATETIME) FDate, CAST(''' + CAST(@TDate AS VARCHAR(50)) + '''AS DATETIME) TDate, 
				'''+@FDateFa+''' FDateFa, '''+@TDateFa+''' TDateFa, 
				/*Added By ADBF 9605 E..*/
				Id, 
				OrderNum, 
				SmsNumber, 
				SmsDesc, 
				GetId,
				FromNumber, 
				ReceiveDate, 
				ReceiveDatePersian, 
				ReceiveDatePersianPreview,
				SendDate, 
				SendDatePersian, 
				SendDatePersianPreview, 
				RegisterDate, 
				RegisterDatePersian, 
				CompanyPersonId, 
				CompanyId, 
				ReceiveDay, 
				ReceiveTime, 
				UserId, 
				Year1, 
				Month1,  
				ISNULL((( SELECT PersonTitle FROM dbo.CrmPersonTitle WHERE PersonTitleId = 
					(SELECT prsTitleId FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons CP WHERE CP.CompanyPersonId = R.CompanyPersonId))) + '' ''	, '''')  +
					ISNULL((SELECT prsFName FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons CP WHERE CP.CompanyPersonId = R.CompanyPersonId)+ '' '' , '''') +
					ISNULL( (SELECT prsLName FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons CP WHERE CP.CompanyPersonId = R.CompanyPersonId) , '''') 
				PersonInfo,
				/*ISNULL(((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM dbo.CrmCompanyTitle WHERE CompanyTitleID = 
					(SELECT cmpTitleId FROM dbo.CrmCompany WHERE CompanyId = R.CompanyId)))+ '' '' , '''')  +
					ISNULL((SELECT CmpName FROM dbo.CrmCompany WHERE CompanyId = R.CompanyId)  , '''') 
				CompanyInfo*/
				/*RZ14000618- Change cmpName based on cmpKind*/
				(SELECT 
					(CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 0
						   THEN ((ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle] CT WHERE CT.[CompanyTitleID] = C.[cmpTitleId]),'''') + '' '' + 
								  ISNULL(C.[cmpName],'''')))
						   ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId),'''')  + '' '' +
										ISNULL(prsFName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(prsLName,'''')
								FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
								WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
								)				
					 END)CompanyName				
				FROM  [dbo].[CrmCompany] C
				WHERE C.CompanyID = R.CompanyId) CompanyInfo 
				/*RZ14000618- End*/
			FROM SmsReceive R
			/*WHERE SendDate BETWEEN ''' + CAST(@Fdate AS VARCHAR(50)) + ''' AND ''' + CAST((@TDate+1) AS VARCHAR(50)) + '''*//* @Fdate AND @TDate+1	*/
			WHERE SendDate BETWEEN ''' + CAST(@Fdate AS VARCHAR(50)) + ''' AND ''' + CAST((@TDate) AS VARCHAR(50)) + ''' /*RZ14000629- Modify TDate + 1*/
			/*AND SmsDesc LIKE (''%' + @Value + '%'')*/	

			/*RZ14000618- Convert character*/
			 AND (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(SmsDesc AS NVARCHAR(2000)) ,
				NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)))
				 LIKE (N''%' + (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(@Value AS NVARCHAR(2000)) ,
				NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740))) + '%'') 	
			/*RZ14000618- End*/
			AND IsDeleted IS NULL' +@cmpDataPermision+ '/*RZ990505*/
			/*Added By ADBF 9605 */
			ORDER BY SendDate Desc'	
		END
		ELSE
		BEGIN
			SET @Qry = 'SELECT 
				/*Added By ADBF 9605 B..*/				
				''' + @rptDate + ''' rptDate,''' + @rptDay + ''' rptDay,' + CAST(@UserID AS VARCHAR(50))   + ' rptUserId, (SELECT ISNULL(FName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(LName , '''')
				From Users Us Where Us.UserId = '+CAST(@UserID AS VARCHAR(50))+') rptUserName, 
				CAST(''' + CAST(@Fdate AS VARCHAR(50)) + ''' AS DATETIME) FDate, CAST(''' + CAST(@TDate AS VARCHAR(50)) + '''AS DATETIME) TDate, 
				'''+@FDateFa +''' FDateFa, '''+@TDateFa+''' TDateFa, 
				/*Added By ADBF 9605 E..*/
				Id, 
				OrderNum, 
				SmsNumber, 
				SmsDesc, 
				GetId,
				FromNumber, 
				ReceiveDate, 
				ReceiveDatePersian, 
				ReceiveDatePersianPreview,
				SendDate, 
				SendDatePersian, 
				SendDatePersianPreview, 
				RegisterDate, 
				RegisterDatePersian, 
				CompanyPersonId, 
				CompanyId, 
				ReceiveDay, 
				ReceiveTime, 
				UserId, 
				Year1, 
				Month1,  
				ISNULL(((SELECT PersonTitle FROM dbo.CrmPersonTitle WHERE PersonTitleId = 
					(SELECT prsTitleId FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons CP WHERE CP.CompanyPersonId = R.CompanyPersonId))) + '' ''	, '''')  +
					ISNULL((SELECT prsFName FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons CP WHERE CP.CompanyPersonId = R.CompanyPersonId)+ '' '' , '''') +
					ISNULL((SELECT prsLName FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons CP WHERE CP.CompanyPersonId = R.CompanyPersonId) , '''') 
				PersonInfo,				
				/*ISNULL(((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM dbo.CrmCompanyTitle WHERE CompanyTitleID = 
					(SELECT cmpTitleId FROM dbo.CrmCompany WHERE CompanyId = R.CompanyId)))+ '' '' , '''')  +
					ISNULL((SELECT CmpName FROM dbo.CrmCompany WHERE CompanyId = R.CompanyId)  , '''') 
				CompanyInfo*/
				/*RZ14000618- Change cmpName based on cmpKind*/
				(SELECT 
					(CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 0
						   THEN ((ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle] CT WHERE CT.[CompanyTitleID] = C.[cmpTitleId]),'''') + '' '' + 
								  ISNULL(C.[cmpName],'''')))
						   ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId),'''')  + '' '' +
										ISNULL(prsFName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(prsLName,'''')
								FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
								WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
								)				
					 END)CompanyName				
				FROM  [dbo].[CrmCompany] C
				WHERE C.CompanyID = R.CompanyId) CompanyInfo 
				/*RZ14000618- End*/
			FROM SmsReceive R
			/*WHERE SendDate BETWEEN ''' + CAST(@Fdate AS VARCHAR(50)) + ''' AND ''' + CAST((@TDate+1) AS VARCHAR(50)) + '''*//*@Fdate AND @TDate+1	*/
			WHERE SendDate BETWEEN ''' + CAST(@Fdate AS VARCHAR(50)) + ''' AND ''' + CAST((@TDate) AS VARCHAR(50)) + ''' /*RZ14000629- Modify TDate + 1*/
			/*AND SmsDesc = '''+ @Value +'''*/
			/*RZ14000618- Convert character*/
			 AND (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(SmsDesc AS NVARCHAR(2000)) ,
				NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)))
				  = ''' + (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(@Value AS NVARCHAR(2000)) ,
				NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740))) + ''' 	
			/*RZ14000618- End*/
			AND IsDeleted IS NULL ' +@cmpDataPermision+ '/*RZ990505*/
			/*Added By ADBF 9605 */
			ORDER BY SendDate Desc'
		END
	END
END
ELSE IF(@LanguageID IS NOT NULL AND @LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID)
BEGIN/*RZ960317 For multi language*/
	IF @Value = ''
	BEGIN
		Set @Qry = 'SELECT 
			/*Added By ADBF 9605 B..*/
			'''+@rptDate+''' rptDate, '''+@rptDay+''' rptDay, '+CAST(@UserID AS VARCHAR(50))+' rptUserId, (SELECT ISNULL(FName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(LName , '' '')
			/*From Users_Lang Us Where Us.UserId = @UserID ) rptUserName, */
			/*RZ960525- Edit rptUserName in multi language*/
			From Users_Lang Us Where Us.UserId =  '+CAST(@UserID AS VARCHAR(50))+' AND Us.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(MAX))+ ') rptUserName, 
			CAST(''' + CAST(@Fdate AS VARCHAR(50)) + ''' AS DATETIME) FDate, CAST(''' + CAST(@TDate AS VARCHAR(50)) + '''AS DATETIME) TDate, 
			'''+@FDateFa +''' FDateFa, '''+@TDateFa+''' TDateFa, 
			/*Added By ADBF 9605 E..*/
			Id, 
			OrderNum, 
			SmsNumber, 
			SmsDesc,

			SendDate,

			ISNULL(((SELECT PersonTitle FROM dbo.CrmPersonTitle_Lang PTLN WHERE PTLN.PersonTitleId = 
				(SELECT prsTitleId FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons CP WHERE CP.CompanyPersonId = R.CompanyPersonId) AND PTLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(MAX))+ ')) + '' ''	, '''')  +
				ISNULL((SELECT prsFName FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons_Lang CP WHERE CP.CompanyPersonId = R.CompanyPersonId AND CP.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(MAX))+ ')+ '' '' , '''') +
				ISNULL((SELECT prsLName FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons_Lang CP WHERE CP.CompanyPersonId = R.CompanyPersonId AND CP.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(MAX))+ ') , '''') 
			PersonInfo,
			/*ISNULL(((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM dbo.CrmCompanyTitle_Lang CTLN WHERE CTLN.CompanyTitleID = 
				(SELECT cmpTitleId FROM dbo.CrmCompany WHERE CompanyId = R.CompanyId) AND CTLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(MAX))+ '))+ '' '' , '''')  +
				ISNULL((SELECT CmpName FROM dbo.CrmCompany_Lang CCLN WHERE CCLN.CompanyId = R.CompanyId AND CCLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(MAX))+ ')  , '''') 
			CompanyInfo*/
			/*RZ14000618- Change cmpName based on cmpKind*/
			(SELECT 
				(CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 0
						THEN ((ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle_Lang] CT WHERE CT.[CompanyTitleID] = C.[cmpTitleId] AND CT.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(MAX))+ '),'''') + '' '' + 
								ISNULL((SELECT CLN.[cmpName] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany_Lang] CLN WHERE CLN.CompanyID = C.CompanyID AND CLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(MAX))+ '),'''')))
						ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle_Lang] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId AND PT.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(MAX))+ '),'''')  + '' '' +
									ISNULL((SELECT ISNULL(CCPLN.prsFName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(CCPLN.prsLName,'''') FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons_Lang] CCPLN WHERE CCPLN.CompanyPersonID = CCP.CompanyPersonID AND CCPLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(MAX))+ '),'''')
							FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
							WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
							)				
					END)CompanyName				
			FROM  [dbo].[CrmCompany] C 
			WHERE C.CompanyID = R.CompanyId) CompanyInfo
			/*RZ14000618- End*/
		FROM SmsReceive R
		/*WHERE SendDate BETWEEN ''' + CAST(@Fdate AS VARCHAR(50)) + ''' AND ''' + CAST((@TDate+1) AS VARCHAR(50)) + '''*//*@Fdate AND @TDate+1*/
		WHERE SendDate BETWEEN ''' + CAST(@Fdate AS VARCHAR(50)) + ''' AND ''' + CAST((@TDate) AS VARCHAR(50)) + ''' /*RZ14000629- Modify TDate + 1*/
		AND IsDeleted IS NULL ' +@cmpDataPermision+ '/*RZ990505*/
		/*--ORDER BY ReceiveDatePersian	*/
		/*Added By ADBF 9605 */
		ORDER BY SendDate Desc'
	END	
	ELSE
	IF @Value <> ''
	BEGIN
		IF @IsLike = 1
		BEGIN
			SET @Qry = 'SELECT 
				/*Added By ADBF 9605 B..*/
				'''+@rptDate+''' rptDate, '''+@rptDay+''' rptDay, '+CAST(@UserID AS VARCHAR(50))+' rptUserId, (SELECT ISNULL(FName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(LName , '' '')
				/*From Users_Lang Us Where Us.UserId = @UserID) rptUserName,*/ 
				/*RZ960525- Edit rptUserName in multi language*/
				From Users_Lang Us Where Us.UserId = '+CAST(@UserID AS VARCHAR(50))+' AND Us.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(MAX))+ ') rptUserName, 
				CAST(''' + CAST(@Fdate AS VARCHAR(50)) + '''AS DATETIME) FDate, CAST(''' + CAST(@TDate AS VARCHAR(50)) + '''AS DATETIME) TDate, 
				'''+@FDateFa+''' FDateFa, '''+@TDateFa +''' TDateFa, 
				/*Added By ADBF 9605 E..*/
				Id, 
				OrderNum, 
				SmsNumber, 
				SmsDesc, 
				GetId,
				FromNumber, 
				ReceiveDate, 
				ReceiveDatePersian, 
				ReceiveDatePersianPreview,
				SendDate,

				CompanyPersonId,

				ReceiveDay, 
				ReceiveTime, 
				UserId,

				Month1,  
				ISNULL((( SELECT PersonTitle FROM dbo.CrmPersonTitle_Lang PTLN WHERE PTLN.PersonTitleId = 
					(SELECT prsTitleId FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons CP WHERE CP.CompanyPersonId = R.CompanyPersonId) AND PTLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(MAX))+ ')) + '' ''	, '''')  +
					ISNULL((SELECT prsFName FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons_Lang CP WHERE CP.CompanyPersonId = R.CompanyPersonId AND CP.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(MAX))+ ')+ '' '' , '''') +
					ISNULL((SELECT prsLName FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons_Lang CP WHERE CP.CompanyPersonId = R.CompanyPersonId AND CP.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(MAX))+ ') , '''') 
				PersonInfo,
				/*ISNULL(((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM dbo.CrmCompanyTitle_Lang CTLN WHERE CTLN.CompanyTitleID = 
					(SELECT cmpTitleId FROM dbo.CrmCompany WHERE CompanyId = R.CompanyId) AND CTLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(MAX))+ '))+ '' '' , '''')  +
					ISNULL((SELECT CmpName FROM dbo.CrmCompany_Lang CCLN WHERE CCLN.CompanyId = R.CompanyId AND CCLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(MAX))+ ')  , '''') 
				CompanyInfo*/
				/*RZ14000618- Change cmpName based on cmpKind*/
				(SELECT 
					(CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 0
							THEN ((ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle_Lang] CT WHERE CT.[CompanyTitleID] = C.[cmpTitleId] AND CT.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(MAX))+ '),'''') + '' '' + 
									ISNULL((SELECT CLN.[cmpName] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany_Lang] CLN WHERE CLN.CompanyID = C.CompanyID AND CLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(MAX))+ '),'''')))
							ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle_Lang] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId AND PT.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(MAX))+ '),'''')  + '' '' +
										ISNULL((SELECT ISNULL(CCPLN.prsFName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(CCPLN.prsLName,'''') FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons_Lang] CCPLN WHERE CCPLN.CompanyPersonID = CCP.CompanyPersonID AND CCPLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(MAX))+ '),'''')
								FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
								WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
								)				
						END)CompanyName				
				FROM  [dbo].[CrmCompany] C 
				WHERE C.CompanyID = R.CompanyId) CompanyInfo
				/*RZ14000618- End*/
			FROM SmsReceive R
			/*WHERE SendDate BETWEEN ''' + CAST(@Fdate AS VARCHAR(50)) + ''' AND ''' + CAST((@TDate+1) AS VARCHAR(50)) + '''*//*@Fdate AND @TDate+1	*/
			WHERE SendDate BETWEEN ''' + CAST(@Fdate AS VARCHAR(50)) + ''' AND ''' + CAST((@TDate) AS VARCHAR(50)) + ''' /*RZ14000629- Modify TDate + 1*/ 
			/*AND SmsDesc LIKE (''%' + @Value + '%'')*/	
			/*RZ14000618- Convert character*/
			 AND (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(SmsDesc AS NVARCHAR(2000)) ,
				NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)))
				 LIKE (N''%' + (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(@Value AS NVARCHAR(2000)) ,
				NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740))) + '%'') 	
			/*RZ14000618- End*/
			AND IsDeleted IS NULL' +@cmpDataPermision+ '/*RZ990505*/	
			/*Added By ADBF 9605 */
			ORDER BY SendDate Desc'
		END
		ELSE
		BEGIN
			SEt @Qry = 'SELECT 
				/*Added By ADBF 9605 B..*/
				
				'''+@rptDate+''' rptDate, '''+@rptDay+''' rptDay, '+CAST(@UserID AS VARCHAR(50))+' rptUserId, (SELECT ISNULL(FName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(LName , '' '')
				/*From Users_Lang Us Where Us.UserId = @UserID) rptUserName, */
				/*RZ960525- Edit rptUserName in multi language*/
				From Users_Lang Us Where Us.UserId = '+CAST(@UserID AS VARCHAR(50))+' AND Us.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(MAX))+ ') rptUserName, 
				CAST(''' + CAST(@Fdate AS VARCHAR(50)) + ''' AS DATETIME) FDate, CAST(''' + CAST(@TDate AS VARCHAR(50)) + '''AS DATETIME) TDate, 
				'''+@FDateFa +''' FDateFa, '''+ @TDateFa+''' TDateFa, 
				/*Added By ADBF 9605 E..*/
				Id, 
				OrderNum, 
				SmsNumber, 
				SmsDesc, 
				GetId,
				FromNumber, 
				ReceiveDate, 
				ReceiveDatePersian, 
				ReceiveDatePersianPreview,
				SendDate, 
				SendDatePersian, 
				SendDatePersianPreview, 
				RegisterDate, 
				RegisterDatePersian, 
				CompanyPersonId, 
				CompanyId, 
				ReceiveDay, 
				ReceiveTime, 
				UserId, 
				Year1, 
				Month1,  
				ISNULL(((SELECT PersonTitle FROM dbo.CrmPersonTitle_Lang PTLN WHERE PTLN.PersonTitleId = 
					(SELECT prsTitleId FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons CP WHERE CP.CompanyPersonId = R.CompanyPersonId) AND PTLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(MAX))+ ')) + '' ''	, '''')  +
					ISNULL((SELECT prsFName FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons_Lang CP WHERE CP.CompanyPersonId = R.CompanyPersonId AND CP.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(MAX))+ ')+ '' '' , '''') +
					ISNULL((SELECT prsLName FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons_Lang CP WHERE CP.CompanyPersonId = R.CompanyPersonId AND CP.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(MAX))+ ') , '''') 
				PersonInfo,				
				/*ISNULL(((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM dbo.CrmCompanyTitle_Lang CTLN WHERE CTLN.CompanyTitleID = 
					(SELECT cmpTitleId FROM dbo.CrmCompany WHERE CompanyId = R.CompanyId) AND CTLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(MAX))+ '))+ '' '' , '''')  +
					ISNULL((SELECT CmpName FROM dbo.CrmCompany_Lang CCLN WHERE CCLN.CompanyId = R.CompanyId AND CCLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(MAX))+ ')  , '''') 
				CompanyInfo*/
				/*RZ14000618- Change cmpName based on cmpKind*/
				(SELECT 
					(CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 0
							THEN ((ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle_Lang] CT WHERE CT.[CompanyTitleID] = C.[cmpTitleId] AND CT.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(MAX))+ '),'''') + '' '' + 
									ISNULL((SELECT CLN.[cmpName] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany_Lang] CLN WHERE CLN.CompanyID = C.CompanyID AND CLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(MAX))+ '),'''')))
							ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle_Lang] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId AND PT.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(MAX))+ '),'''')  + '' '' +
										ISNULL((SELECT ISNULL(CCPLN.prsFName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(CCPLN.prsLName,'''') FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons_Lang] CCPLN WHERE CCPLN.CompanyPersonID = CCP.CompanyPersonID AND CCPLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(MAX))+ '),'''')
								FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
								WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
								)				
						END)CompanyName				
				FROM  [dbo].[CrmCompany] C 
				WHERE C.CompanyID = R.CompanyId) CompanyInfo
				/*RZ14000618- End*/
			FROM SmsReceive R
			/*WHERE SendDate BETWEEN ''' + CAST(@Fdate AS VARCHAR(50)) + ''' AND ''' + CAST((@TDate+1) AS VARCHAR(50)) + '''*//*@Fdate AND @TDate+1	*/
			WHERE SendDate BETWEEN ''' + CAST(@Fdate AS VARCHAR(50)) + ''' AND ''' + CAST((@TDate) AS VARCHAR(50)) + ''' /*RZ14000629- Modify TDate + 1*/
			/*AND SmsDesc = '''+ @Value		+'''*/		
			/*RZ14000618- Convert character*/
			 AND (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(SmsDesc AS NVARCHAR(2000)) ,
				NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)))
				 = ''' + (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(@Value AS NVARCHAR(2000)) ,
				NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740))) + ''' 	
			/*RZ14000618- End*/
			AND IsDeleted IS NULL' +@cmpDataPermision+ '/*RZ990505*/
			/*Added By ADBF 9605 */
			ORDER BY SendDate Desc'
		END
	END
