-- [dbo].[CRMspTransferMobAndEmailFromMainPrsToCmpRep] | References permission/temp-filter machinery





/*RZ14000511*/
/*RZ14000621- Change cmpName based on cmpKind*/
Create Procedure [dbo].[CRMspTransferMobAndEmailFromMainPrsToCmpRep]
(		
/* 
	exec CRMspTransferMobAndEmailFromMainPrsToCmpRep 3,1033,1065
*/
@UserId INT,
@LanguageID INT = NULL,
@DefaultLanguageID INT = NULL  
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
DECLARE @Term VARCHAR(MAX) ='', @Qry VARCHAR(MAX) = '',@Txt VARCHAR(MAX) = ''
/*========================================================*/
SELECT @Txt=Txt FROM dbo.CrmsysDBTempInfo WHERE UserId = @UserId AND TypeId = 1
IF(ISNULL(@Txt,'') <> '')
BEGIN
	SET @Txt = ' AND (CC.CompanyID IN ((SELECT CompanyId FROM (' + @Txt +') TT))) '
END
/*========================================================*/

DECLARE @TmpTable TABLE (
		[CompanyId] INT,
		[cmpMainPersonId] NVARCHAR(MAX),
		[TypeError] INT
)
/*========================================================*/
SET @Qry = @Qry + N'SELECT DISTINCT 
						CC.CompanyID,	
						CC.[cmpMainPersonId]	
					FROM dbo.CrmCompany CC
					WHERE 1 = 1 
					AND (ISNULL(CC.cmpMobile,'''') = '''' AND ISNULL(CC.cmpEmail,'''') = '''')
					
					'
/*EXECUTE(@Qry + @Txt)*/
INSERT INTO @TmpTable ([CompanyId],[cmpMainPersonId])
EXECUTE(@Qry + @Txt)
/*========================================================*/
DECLARE @CompanyId INT, @cmpMainPersonId INT
DECLARE Crsr CURSOR LOCAL FOR				
	SELECT 
		[CompanyId],
		[cmpMainPersonId]
	FROM @TmpTable	
OPEN Crsr
FETCH NEXT FROM Crsr INTO @CompanyId,@cmpMainPersonId
WHILE @@Fetch_Status = 0
BEGIN
	/*--------------------------------------------*/
	DECLARE @cmpMobile NVARCHAR(100) = NULL,@cmpEmail NVARCHAR(50) = NULL
	,@prsMobile NVARCHAR(100) = NULL,@prsEmail NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,@prsEmailII  NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
	@FindCmpIdByEmail INT = NULL,@FindCmpIdByMob INT = NULL
	SELECT TOP 1 
		   @prsMobile = [prsMobile]
		  ,@prsEmail = [prsEmail]
		  ,@prsEmailII = [prsEmailII]      
	FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons]
	WHERE [CompanyPersonID] = @cmpMainPersonId
	/*Set mobile and email*/
	SET @cmpMobile = @prsMobile
	SET @cmpEmail = ISNULL(@prsEmail,@prsEmailII)
	/*Find duplicate*/
	SELECT TOP 1
		 @FindCmpIdByEmail = [CompanyId]					
	FROM  [dbo].[CrmCompany] C  
	WHERE C.[CompanyID] <> @CompanyId
	AND C.[cmpEmail] = @CmpEmail
	/**/
	SELECT TOP 1
			@FindCmpIdByMob = [CompanyId]				
	FROM  [dbo].[CrmCompany] C  
	WHERE C.[CompanyID] <> @CompanyId
	AND C.[cmpMobile] = @CmpMobile
	/*SET ERROR*/
	IF(ISNULL(@cmpMainPersonId,0) = 0) /*not has mainPerson*/
	BEGIN
	/**/
		UPDATE @TmpTable 
		SET [TypeError] = 1
		WHERE [CompanyId] = @CompanyId	
		/**/
		FETCH NEXT FROM Crsr INTO @CompanyId,@cmpMainPersonId
		CONTINUE	
	/**/
	END
	ELSE IF(ISNULL(@cmpMobile,'') = '' AND ISNULL(@CmpEmail,'') = '') /*not has mobile or email*/
	BEGIN
	/**/
		UPDATE @TmpTable 
		SET [TypeError] = 2
		WHERE [CompanyId] = @CompanyId
		/**/
		FETCH NEXT FROM Crsr INTO @CompanyId,@cmpMainPersonId
		CONTINUE		
	/**/
	END	
	ELSE IF(ISNULL(@FindCmpIdByMob,0) > 0) /*mobile is dup*/
	BEGIN
	/**/
		UPDATE @TmpTable 
		SET [TypeError] = 3
		WHERE [CompanyId] = @CompanyId	
		/**/
		FETCH NEXT FROM Crsr INTO @CompanyId,@cmpMainPersonId
		CONTINUE	
	/**/
	END
	ELSE IF(ISNULL(@FindCmpIdByEmail,0) > 0) /*email is dup*/
	BEGIN
	/**/
		UPDATE @TmpTable 
		SET [TypeError] = 4
		WHERE [CompanyId] = @CompanyId	
		/**/
		FETCH NEXT FROM Crsr INTO @CompanyId,@cmpMainPersonId
		CONTINUE	
	/**/
	END
	/*ELSE IF(ISNULL(@cmpMobile,'') = '') /-*not has mobile*-/
	BEGIN
	/**/
		UPDATE @TmpTable 
		SET [TypeError] = 5
		WHERE [CompanyId] = @CompanyId
		/**/
		FETCH NEXT FROM Crsr INTO @CompanyId,@cmpMainPersonId
		CONTINUE		
	/**/
	END
	ELSE IF(ISNULL(@CmpEmail,'') = '') /-*not has email*-/
	BEGIN
	/**/
		UPDATE @TmpTable 
		SET [TypeError] = 6
		WHERE [CompanyId] = @CompanyId
		/**/
		FETCH NEXT FROM Crsr INTO @CompanyId,@cmpMainPersonId
		CONTINUE			
	/**/
	END*/
	/*-----------------------------------------------------------*/
	/*Update Company for mobile and email*/
	IF(ISNULL(@FindCmpIdByEmail,0) = 0 AND ISNULL(@FindCmpIdByMob,0) = 0)
	BEGIN
	/**/
		UPDATE [dbo].[CrmCompany] 
		SET [cmpMobile] = @CmpMobile,
			[cmpEmail] = @CmpEmail
		WHERE CompanyID = @CompanyId
	/**/
	END	
	/*--------------------------------------------*/
	FETCH NEXT FROM Crsr INTO @CompanyId,@cmpMainPersonId
END
CLOSE Crsr
DEALLOCATE Crsr

/*============= Return list of company not have email or mobile ===============*/

IF(@LanguageID IS NULL OR @LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID)
BEGIN
	SELECT 
		 [CompanyId]
		,[cmpNumber]
		/*,(ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle] CT WHERE CT.[CompanyTitleID] = C.[cmpTitleId]),'') + ' ' + 
		  ISNULL(C.[cmpName],''))  CompanyName*/	

		/*RZ14000621- Change cmpName based on cmpKind*/
		,(CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 0
			THEN ((ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle] CT WHERE CT.[CompanyTitleID] = C.[cmpTitleId]),'') + ' ' + 
					ISNULL(C.[cmpName],'')))
			ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId),'')  + ' ' +
						ISNULL(prsFName, '') + ' ' + ISNULL(prsLName,'')
				FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
				WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
				)				
		END) CompanyName
		/*RZ14000621- End*/

		,[cmpMainPersonId]
		,(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId),'')  + ' ' +
				 ISNULL(prsFName, '') + ' ' + ISNULL(prsLName,'')
		  FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
		  WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
		 ) [cmpMainPerson]
		,[cmpEmail]
		,[cmpMobile]
		,(SELECT [TypeError] FROM @TmpTable TM WHERE TM.[CompanyId] = C.CompanyID) [TypeError]
	FROM  [dbo].[CrmCompany] C  
	WHERE C.[CompanyID] IN(SELECT [CompanyId] FROM @TmpTable)
	AND (ISNULL(C.[cmpEmail],'') = '' AND ISNULL([cmpMobile],'') = '')
END
ELSE IF(@LanguageID IS NOT NULL AND @LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID)
BEGIN
	SELECT 
		 [CompanyId]
		,[cmpNumber]
		/*,(ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle_Lang] CT WHERE CT.[CompanyTitleID] = C.[cmpTitleId] AND CT.LanguageID = @LanguageID),'') + ' ' + 
		  ISNULL((SELECT TOP 1 [cmpName] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany_Lang] CLN WHERE CLN.CompanyID = C.CompanyID AND CLN.LanguageID = @LanguageID ),''))  CompanyName	*/
		/*RZ14000621- Change cmpName based on cmpKind*/
		,(CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 0
			THEN ((ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle_Lang] CT WHERE CT.[CompanyTitleID] = C.[cmpTitleId] AND CT.LanguageID = @LanguageID),'') + ' ' + 
					ISNULL((SELECT CLN.[cmpName] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany_Lang] CLN WHERE CLN.CompanyID = C.CompanyID AND CLN.LanguageID = @LanguageID),'')))
			ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle_Lang] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId AND PT.LanguageID = @LanguageID),'')  + ' ' +
						ISNULL((SELECT ISNULL(CCPLN.prsFName, '') + ' ' + ISNULL(CCPLN.prsLName,'') FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons_Lang] CCPLN WHERE CCPLN.CompanyPersonID = CCP.CompanyPersonID AND CCPLN.LanguageID = @LanguageID),'')
				FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
				WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
				)				
		END) CompanyName
		/*RZ14000621- End*/

		,[cmpMainPersonId]
		,(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle_Lang] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId AND PT.LanguageID = @LanguageID),'')  + ' ' +
				 ISNULL((SELECT ISNULL(CCPLN.prsFName, '') + ' ' + ISNULL(CCPLN.prsLName,'') FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons_Lang] CCPLN WHERE CCPLN.CompanyPersonID = CCP.CompanyPersonID AND CCPLN.LanguageID = @LanguageID),'')
		  FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
		  WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
		 ) [cmpMainPerson]
		,[cmpEmail]
		,[cmpMobile]
		,(SELECT [TypeError] FROM @TmpTable TM WHERE TM.[CompanyId] = C.CompanyID) [TypeError]
	FROM  [dbo].[CrmCompany] C  
	WHERE C.[CompanyID] IN(SELECT [CompanyId] FROM @TmpTable)
	AND (ISNULL(C.[cmpEmail],'') = '' AND ISNULL([cmpMobile],'') = '')
END
/*========================================================*/
/**/
END







GO
-- [dbo].[CRMspUpdateCompanyReportOperation] | References permission/temp-filter machinery




/*ADBF9408099411*/
/*RZ960428- Add LanguageID & FirstRegister fields*/
/*RZ14010605- Change 8000 to max*/
/*RZ14011125- Add CompanyChangeHistory*/
/*RZ14021006- Add CnvsStatusResultId*/
Create PROCEDURE [dbo].[CRMspUpdateCompanyReportOperation]
(	
/*
	EXEC CRMspUpdateCompanyReportOperation '3','2','4','13960428',15,'3',1,'dasti' 
*/
@UserId VARCHAR(15),
@Year1 VARCHAR(15),
@Month1 VARCHAR(15),
@RegisterDatePersian VARCHAR(15),
@Mode INT,
@ModeVal VARCHAR(1000),
@Multiopr INT = NULL ,
@AppType NVARCHAR(50)   
)
AS
BEGIN

DECLARE @txt VARCHAR(MAX)/*RZ14010605- Change 8000 to max*/
SET @txt = '0'
SELECT @txt = TXT
FROM dbo.CrmsysDBTempInfo
WHERE UserId = @UserId
AND TypeId = 1
/*---------------------------------------*/
/*RZ14011125- Add CompanyChangeHistory*/
DECLARE @CmpTmpTable TABLE (
	[CompanyId] INT,
	[GroupId] INT,
	[GroupIds] NVARCHAR(MAX)
)
DECLARE @QryGetValue VARCHAR(MAX) ='',@CrsrCmpId INT,@CrsrGroupId INT,@CrsrGroupIds NVARCHAR(MAX),@NewGroupId INT,@NewGroupIds NVARCHAR(MAX)
,@CmpChangeHistoryInsertedId INT = 0,@DefaultLanguageID INT = 0,@RegisterDate DATETIME = GETDATE(),
@CmpChangeHistoryDesc NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL,@CmpChangeHistory_Lang NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL
,@OldValue NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL,@NewValue NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL,@FieldName NVARCHAR(300) = NULL
,@OldValueName NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL,@NewValueName NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL,@UserName NVARCHAR(1000) = NULL
SET @DefaultLanguageID = (SELECT TOP 1 [DefaultLanguageID] FROM [dbo].[CrmsysSetting])
SET @UserName = ISNULL((SELECT TOP(1) ISNULL(FName,'') + ' ' + ISNULL(LName,'') FROM [dbo].[Users] U WHERE U.UserId = @UserId),'')
/*-----------------------------------------------*/
DECLARE @CmpChangeHistoryDescTbl_Lang TABLE(
	[Desc] NVARCHAR(MAX),
	LanguageID INT
)

DECLARE @CmpChangeHistoryDescNoValTbl_Lang TABLE(
	[Txt] NVARCHAR(MAX),
	LanguageID INT
)
INSERT INTO @CmpChangeHistoryDescNoValTbl_Lang([Txt], [LanguageID])
VALUES
	(N'بدون مقدار', 1065),
	(N'بلا قيمة', 1025),
	(N'no value', 1033)		
/*RZ14011125- End*/
/*---------------------------------------*/
IF @Mode = 12 /*GII*/
BEGIN
/*---------------------------------------*/
/*RZ14011125- Add CompanyChangeHistory*/
/*save old value*/
DELETE FROM @CmpTmpTable 
SET @QryGetValue = N'SELECT DISTINCT 
						CC.CompanyID,	
						CC.cmpGroupIdII
					FROM dbo.CrmCompany CC
					WHERE (CC.CompanyID IN ((SELECT CompanyId FROM (' + @txt +') TT)))'

INSERT INTO @CmpTmpTable([CompanyId],[GroupId])
EXECUTE(@QryGetValue)
/*RZ14011125- End*/
/*---------------------------------------*/
IF (@ModeVal = -1)
BEGIN
EXEC (
'UPDATE dbo.CRMCompany 
SET cmpGroupIdII = NULL ' + 
' ,UserId = ' + @UserId + ' , ' +
' RegisterDatePersian = ' + @RegisterDatePersian +' , ' +
' RegisterDate = getdate() , AppType = ''' + @AppType + ''' ' + ' ' + ' 
WHERE CompanyID IN ( 
SELECT CompanyID FROM ( ' + 
@txt
+ ' )t ) ' )	
END
ELSE
IF (@ModeVal > 0)
BEGIN 
EXEC (
'UPDATE dbo.CRMCompany 
SET cmpGroupIdII = ' + @ModeVal +
' ,UserId = ' + @UserId + ' , ' +
' RegisterDatePersian = ' + @RegisterDatePersian +' , ' +
' RegisterDate = getdate() , AppType = ''' + @AppType + ''' ' + ' ' + ' 
WHERE CompanyID IN ( 
SELECT CompanyID FROM ( ' + 
@txt
+ ' )t ) ' )	
END
/*---------------------------------------*/
/*RZ14011125- Add CompanyChangeHistory*/
SET @CrsrCmpId = NULL
SET @CrsrGroupId = NULL
DECLARE CrsrAddCmpChangeHistory CURSOR LOCAL FOR
/**/				
	SELECT 
		[CompanyId],
		[GroupId]
	FROM @CmpTmpTable
	WHERE [CompanyId] <> 0
/**/
OPEN CrsrAddCmpChangeHistory
FETCH NEXT FROM CrsrAddCmpChangeHistory INTO @CrsrCmpId,@CrsrGroupId
WHILE @@Fetch_Status = 0
BEGIN
/**/
	/*-----------------------------------------------------*/
	SET @NewGroupId = NULL		
	SET @CmpChangeHistoryInsertedId = 0	
	SET @FieldName = NULL
	SET @OldValue = NULL
	SET @NewValue = NULL
	SET @CmpChangeHistory_Lang = NULL
	/**/
	SET @NewGroupId = (SELECT TOP(1) [cmpGroupIdII] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany] WHERE CompanyID =  @CrsrCmpId)
	/**/
	IF(ISNULL(@NewGroupId,0) <> ISNULL(@CrsrGroupId,0))
	BEGIN
		/*----------------------------------*/
		SET @FieldName = N'cmpGroupIdII'
		SET @OldValue = CAST(@CrsrGroupId AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) 
		SET @NewValue = CAST(@NewGroupId AS NVARCHAR(MAX))
		/*--------------------------*/					
		/*--------------------------*/					
		DELETE FROM @CmpChangeHistoryDescTbl_Lang
		INSERT INTO @CmpChangeHistoryDescTbl_Lang([Desc], [LanguageID])
		VALUES
			(N'تغییر گروه دوم از ({0}) به ({1}) توسط {2}', 1065),
			(N'تغيير المجموعة الثانية من ({0}) إلى ({1}) بواسطة {2}', 1025),
			(N'Changing the second group from ({0}) to ({1}) by {2}', 1033)
		/*--------------*/
		IF(ISNULL(@CrsrGroupId,0) = 0)
		BEGIN
		/**/
			SET @OldValueName = (SELECT TOP(1) [Txt] FROM @CmpChangeHistoryDescNoValTbl_Lang T WHERE T.LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID)
		/**/
		END
		ELSE
		BEGIN
		/**/
			SET @OldValueName = (SELECT TOP(1) [GroupIIName] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyGroupsII] G WHERE G.CompanyGroupIIID = @CrsrGroupId)
		/**/
		END

		IF(ISNULL(@NewGroupId,0) = 0)
		BEGIN
		/**/
			SET @NewValueName = (SELECT TOP(1) [Txt] FROM @CmpChangeHistoryDescNoValTbl_Lang T WHERE T.LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID)
		/**/
		END
		ELSE
		BEGIN
		/**/
			SET @NewValueName = (SELECT TOP(1) [GroupIIName] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyGroupsII] G WHERE G.CompanyGroupIIID = @NewGroupId)
		/**/
		END

		SET @CmpChangeHistoryDesc = (SELECT TOP(1) [Desc] FROM @CmpChangeHistoryDescTbl_Lang T WHERE T.LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID)
		SET @CmpChangeHistoryDesc = REPLACE((REPLACE((REPLACE(@CmpChangeHistoryDesc,N'{0}',@OldValueName)),N'{1}',@NewValueName)),N'{2}',@UserName)											
		SET @CmpChangeHistory_Lang = ISNULL('[' + ((STUFF((SELECT N',{' + 
														N'"LanguageID":' + CAST(L.[LanguageID] AS NVARCHAR(100)) +
														N',"OldValue":"' + ISNULL(@OldValue,'') + N'"' +
														N',"NewValue":"' + ISNULL(@NewValue,'') + N'"' +																
														N',"Desc":"' +
															ISNULL((REPLACE((ISNULL(REPLACE((REPLACE((SELECT TOP(1) [Desc] FROM @CmpChangeHistoryDescTbl_Lang T WHERE T.LanguageID = L.[LanguageID]),N'{0}',																		
																	ISNULL((CASE WHEN ISNULL(@CrsrGroupId,0) = 0
																			THEN (SELECT TOP(1) [Txt] FROM @CmpChangeHistoryDescNoValTbl_Lang T WHERE T.LanguageID = L.[LanguageID])
																			ELSE (SELECT TOP(1) [GroupIIName] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyGroupsII_Lang] G WHERE G.CompanyGroupIIID = @CrsrGroupId AND G.LanguageID = L.[LanguageID])																		
																	END),'')
																	))																																				
																	,N'{1}',																		
																	ISNULL((CASE WHEN ISNULL(@NewGroupId,0) = 0
																			THEN (SELECT TOP(1) [Txt] FROM @CmpChangeHistoryDescNoValTbl_Lang T WHERE T.LanguageID = L.[LanguageID])
																			ELSE (SELECT TOP(1) [GroupIIName] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyGroupsII_Lang] G WHERE G.CompanyGroupIIID = @NewGroupId AND G.LanguageID = L.[LanguageID])																		
																	END),'')
																		
																	),'')
																												
															),N'{2}',
															ISNULL((SELECT TOP(1) ISNULL(FName,'') + ' ' + ISNULL(LName,'') FROM [dbo].[Users_Lang] U WHERE U.UserId = @UserId AND U.LanguageID = L.[LanguageID]),'')
															)																												
															),'') +																												
													+ N'"}'					
													FROM [dbo].[Languages] L
													WHERE ISNULL(L.IsActive,0) = 1	
													AND L.[LanguageID] <> @DefaultLanguageID
													FOR XML PATH(''), TYPE).value(N'(./text())[1]', N'NVARCHAR(MAX)'), 1, 1, ''))				
										+ ']'),'')					
		/*-----------*/
		EXEC [dbo].[CRMspInsertCompanyChangeHistory]
		@CompanyId = @CrsrCmpId,
		@FieldName = @FieldName,
		@OldValue = @OldValue,
		@NewValue = @NewValue,
		@Desc = @CmpChangeHistoryDesc, 
		@CmpChangeHistory_Lang = @CmpChangeHistory_Lang, 	
		@UserId = @UserId,
		@RegisterDate = @RegisterDate,
		@RegisterDatePersian = @RegisterDatePersian,
		@AppType = @AppType,
		@DefaultLanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID,		
		@InsertedId = @CmpChangeHistoryInsertedId OUTPUT			
		/*----------------------------------*/
	END	   
	/*-----------------------------------------------------*/														    				 					
/**/	
FETCH NEXT FROM CrsrAddCmpChangeHistory INTO @CrsrCmpId,@CrsrGroupId
END
CLOSE CrsrAddCmpChangeHistory
DEALLOCATE CrsrAddCmpChangeHistory
/*RZ14011125- End*/
/*---------------------------------------*/
END

SET @CrsrCmpId = NULL
SET @CrsrGroupIds = NULL
DECLARE CrsrAddCmpChangeHistory CURSOR LOCAL FOR
/**/				
	SELECT 
		[CompanyId],
		[GroupIds]
	FROM @CmpTmpTable
	WHERE [CompanyId] <> 0
/**/
OPEN CrsrAddCmpChangeHistory
FETCH NEXT FROM CrsrAddCmpChangeHistory INTO @CrsrCmpId,@CrsrGroupIds
WHILE @@Fetch_Status = 0
BEGIN
/**/
	/*-----------------------------------------------------*/
	SET @NewGroupIds = NULL		
	SET @CmpChangeHistoryInsertedId = 0	
	SET @FieldName = NULL
	SET @OldValue = NULL
	SET @NewValue = NULL
	SET @CmpChangeHistory_Lang = NULL
	/**/	
	SET @NewGroupIds = ((STUFF((SELECT ',' + CAST(ISNULL([CompanyGroupsMultiID],0) AS NVARCHAR(50))	
								FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyMultiGroups]							
								WHERE [CompanyID] = @CrsrCmpId
								ORDER BY [CompanyGroupsMultiID] ASC
								FOR XML PATH(''), TYPE).value(N'(./text())[1]', N'NVARCHAR(MAX)'), 1, 1, ''))				
						)
	/**/
	IF(ISNULL(@NewGroupIds,'') <> ISNULL(@CrsrGroupIds,''))
	BEGIN
		/*----------------------------------*/
		SET @FieldName = N'cmpGroupsMultiIds'
		SET @OldValue = CAST(@CrsrGroupIds AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) 
		SET @NewValue = CAST(@NewGroupIds AS NVARCHAR(MAX))
		/*--------------------------*/					
		/*--------------------------*/					
		DELETE FROM @CmpChangeHistoryDescTbl_Lang
		INSERT INTO @CmpChangeHistoryDescTbl_Lang([Desc], [LanguageID])
		VALUES
			(N'تغییر گروه ترکیبی از ({0}) به ({1}) توسط {2}', 1065),
			(N'تغيير المجموعة المختلطة من ({0}) إلى ({1}) عن طريق {2}', 1025),
			(N'Changing the combination group from ({0}) to ({1}) by {2}', 1033)
		/*--------------*/
		IF(ISNULL(@CrsrGroupIds,'') ='')
		BEGIN
		/**/
			SET @OldValueName = (SELECT TOP(1) [Txt] FROM @CmpChangeHistoryDescNoValTbl_Lang T WHERE T.LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID)
		/**/
		END
		ELSE
		BEGIN
		/**/			
			SET @OldValueName = ((STUFF((SELECT ',' + ISNULL([CompanyGroupsMultiName],'')		
									FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyGroupsMulti]								
									WHERE  [CompanyGroupsMultiID] IN(SELECT CAST(ISNULL(splitdata,0) AS INT) 
																	 FROM [dbo].[fnSplitString](@CrsrGroupIds,',')
																	 WHERE CAST(ISNULL(splitdata,0) AS INT) <> 0)
									ORDER BY [CompanyGroupsMultiID] ASC
									FOR XML PATH(''), TYPE).value(N'(./text())[1]', N'NVARCHAR(MAX)'), 1, 1, ''))				
								  )			
		/**/
		END

		IF(ISNULL(@NewGroupIds,'') = '')
		BEGIN
		/**/
			SET @NewValueName = (SELECT TOP(1) [Txt] FROM @CmpChangeHistoryDescNoValTbl_Lang T WHERE T.LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID)
		/**/
		END
		ELSE
		BEGIN
		/**/
			SET @NewValueName = ((STUFF((SELECT ',' + ISNULL([CompanyGroupsMultiName],'')		
									FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyGroupsMulti]								
									WHERE  [CompanyGroupsMultiID] IN(SELECT CAST(ISNULL(splitdata,0) AS INT) 
																	 FROM [dbo].[fnSplitString](@NewGroupIds,',')
																	 WHERE CAST(ISNULL(splitdata,0) AS INT) <> 0)
									ORDER BY [CompanyGroupsMultiID] ASC
									FOR XML PATH(''), TYPE).value(N'(./text())[1]', N'NVARCHAR(MAX)'), 1, 1, ''))				
								  )	
		/**/
		END

		SET @CmpChangeHistoryDesc = (SELECT TOP(1) [Desc] FROM @CmpChangeHistoryDescTbl_Lang T WHERE T.LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID)
		SET @CmpChangeHistoryDesc = REPLACE((REPLACE((REPLACE(@CmpChangeHistoryDesc,N'{0}',@OldValueName)),N'{1}',@NewValueName)),N'{2}',@UserName)											
		SET @CmpChangeHistory_Lang = ISNULL('[' + ((STUFF((SELECT N',{' + 
														N'"LanguageID":' + CAST(L.[LanguageID] AS NVARCHAR(100)) +
														N',"OldValue":"' + ISNULL(@OldValue,'') + N'"' +
														N',"NewValue":"' + ISNULL(@NewValue,'') + N'"' +																
														N',"Desc":"' +
															ISNULL((REPLACE((ISNULL(REPLACE((REPLACE((SELECT TOP(1) [Desc] FROM @CmpChangeHistoryDescTbl_Lang T WHERE T.LanguageID = L.[LanguageID]),N'{0}',																		
																	ISNULL((CASE WHEN ISNULL(@CrsrGroupIds,'') = ''
																			THEN (SELECT TOP(1) [Txt] FROM @CmpChangeHistoryDescNoValTbl_Lang T WHERE T.LanguageID = L.[LanguageID])
																			ELSE (((STUFF((SELECT ',' + ISNULL([CompanyGroupsMultiName],'')		
																					FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyGroupsMulti_Lang] G								
																					WHERE  G.[CompanyGroupsMultiID] IN(SELECT CAST(ISNULL(splitdata,0) AS INT) 
																													 FROM [dbo].[fnSplitString](@CrsrGroupIds,',')
																													 WHERE CAST(ISNULL(splitdata,0) AS INT) <> 0)
																					 AND G.LanguageID = L.[LanguageID]
																					ORDER BY [CompanyGroupsMultiID] ASC
																					FOR XML PATH(''), TYPE).value(N'(./text())[1]', N'NVARCHAR(MAX)'), 1, 1, ''))))																		
																	END),'')
																	))																																				
																	,N'{1}',																		
																	ISNULL((CASE WHEN ISNULL(@NewGroupIds,'') = ''
																			THEN (SELECT TOP(1) [Txt] FROM @CmpChangeHistoryDescNoValTbl_Lang T WHERE T.LanguageID = L.[LanguageID])
																			ELSE (((STUFF((SELECT ',' + ISNULL([CompanyGroupsMultiName],'')		
																					FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyGroupsMulti_Lang] G								
																					WHERE  G.[CompanyGroupsMultiID] IN(SELECT CAST(ISNULL(splitdata,0) AS INT) 
																													 FROM [dbo].[fnSplitString](@NewGroupIds,',')
																													 WHERE CAST(ISNULL(splitdata,0) AS INT) <> 0)
																					 AND G.LanguageID = L.[LanguageID]
																					ORDER BY [CompanyGroupsMultiID] ASC
																					FOR XML PATH(''), TYPE).value(N'(./text())[1]', N'NVARCHAR(MAX)'), 1, 1, ''))))																		
																	END),'')
																		
																	),'')
																												
															),N'{2}',
															ISNULL((SELECT TOP(1) ISNULL(FName,'') + ' ' + ISNULL(LName,'') FROM [dbo].[Users_Lang] U WHERE U.UserId = @UserId AND U.LanguageID = L.[LanguageID]),'')
															)																												
															),'') +																												
													+ N'"}'					
													FROM [dbo].[Languages] L
													WHERE ISNULL(L.IsActive,0) = 1	
													AND L.[LanguageID] <> @DefaultLanguageID
													FOR XML PATH(''), TYPE).value(N'(./text())[1]', N'NVARCHAR(MAX)'), 1, 1, ''))				
										+ ']'),'')					
		/*-----------*/
		EXEC [dbo].[CRMspInsertCompanyChangeHistory]
		@CompanyId = @CrsrCmpId,
		@FieldName = @FieldName,
		@OldValue = @OldValue,
		@NewValue = @NewValue,
		@Desc = @CmpChangeHistoryDesc, 
		@CmpChangeHistory_Lang = @CmpChangeHistory_Lang, 	
		@UserId = @UserId,
		@RegisterDate = @RegisterDate,
		@RegisterDatePersian = @RegisterDatePersian,
		@AppType = @AppType,
		@DefaultLanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID,		
		@InsertedId = @CmpChangeHistoryInsertedId OUTPUT			
		/*----------------------------------*/
	END	   
	/*-----------------------------------------------------*/														    				 					
/**/	
FETCH NEXT FROM CrsrAddCmpChangeHistory INTO @CrsrCmpId,@CrsrGroupIds
END
CLOSE CrsrAddCmpChangeHistory
DEALLOCATE CrsrAddCmpChangeHistory

BEGIN 
	/*RZ14021006- Add CnvsStatusResultId*/
	DECLARE @cnvsStatusResultId INT = NULL,@cnvsStatusId INT = NULL,@cnvsStatusResultIdStr VARCHAR(1000) = NULL
	SET @cnvsStatusId = CAST(@ModeVal AS INT)
	SET @cnvsStatusResultId = (SELECT TOP(1)  [ConversationStatusResultId]						
								FROM [dbo].[CrmConversationStatusResult] CSR
								WHERE CSR.[ConversationStatusId] = @cnvsStatusId
								AND ISNULL([IsDefault],0) = 1
								ORDER BY ISNULL([OrderView], [ConversationStatusResultId]), [ConversationStatusId])
	IF(ISNULL(@cnvsStatusResultId,0) = 0)
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SET @cnvsStatusResultId = (SELECT TOP(1)  [ConversationStatusResultId]						
									FROM [dbo].[CrmConversationStatusResult] CSR
									WHERE CSR.[ConversationStatusId] = @cnvsStatusId									
									ORDER BY ISNULL([OrderView], [ConversationStatusResultId]), [ConversationStatusId])
	/**/
	END


	SET @cnvsStatusResultIdStr =  CAST(@cnvsStatusResultId AS VARCHAR(50))
	/*RZ14021006- End*/


EXEC (
'UPDATE dbo.CrmConversation  
SET cnvsStatusId = ' + @ModeVal +
',cnvsStatusResultId = ' + @cnvsStatusResultIdStr +/*RZ14021006- Add CnvsStatusResultId*/
' ,UserId = ' + @UserId + ' , ' +
' RegisterDatePersian = ' + @RegisterDatePersian +' , ' +
' RegisterDate = getdate() , AppType = ''' + @AppType + ''' ' + ' ' + ' 
WHERE cnvsStatusId IN ( 
SELECT cnvsStatusId FROM ( ' + 
@txt
+ ' )t ) ' + ' 
And cnvsCompanyId IN ( 
SELECT CompanyID FROM ( ' + 
@txt
+ ' )tt ) ' )
END

-- [dbo].[CRMspUpdateCompanyShpSettlementTypeFromRptFilterData] | References permission/temp-filter machinery






/*RZ14020830*/
Create PROCEDURE [dbo].[CRMspUpdateCompanyShpSettlementTypeFromRptFilterData]
(
/* 
	exec CRMspUpdateCompanyShpSettlementTypeFromRptFilterData 		
*/
@cmpShpSettlementTypeId INT = NULL,
@cmpShpSettlementCreditIssueType INT = NULL,
@cmpShpSettlementCreditPerBuy NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
@cmpShpSettlementTotalCredit NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL, 
@cmpShpSettlementCreditRemainShow BIT = NULL,
@UserId INT,
@RegisterDate DATETIME,
@RegisterDatePersian NVARCHAR(8),
@Year1 INT,
@Month1 INT,
@AppType NVARCHAR(20)
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
/*======================================================================*/
/*================= Create table base on company Report ================*/
DECLARE @CmpTmpTable TABLE (
	[CompanyID] INT
)

DECLARE @Term VARCHAR(MAX) ='', @Qry VARCHAR(MAX) = '',@Txt VARCHAR(MAX) = ''
/*========================================================*/

SELECT @Txt=Txt FROM dbo.CrmsysDBTempInfo WHERE UserId = @UserId AND TypeId = 1
IF(ISNULL(@Txt,'') <> '')
BEGIN
	SET @Txt = ' AND (CC.CompanyID IN ((SELECT CompanyId FROM (' + @Txt +') TT))) '
END
/*========================================================*/
SET @Qry = @Qry + 'SELECT DISTINCT 
	CC.CompanyID
FROM dbo.CrmCompany CC
WHERE 1 = 1 '
INSERT INTO @CmpTmpTable([CompanyID])
EXECUTE(@Qry + @Txt + @Term)
/*========================================================*/
UPDATE [dbo].[CrmCompany]
SET  [UserId] = @UserId
    ,[Year1] = @Year1
    ,[Month1] = @Month1
    ,[RegisterDate] = @RegisterDate
    ,[RegisterDatePersian] = @RegisterDatePersian
    ,[AppType] = @AppType
    ,[cmpShpSettlementTypeId] = @cmpShpSettlementTypeId
    ,[cmpShpSettlementCreditIssueType] = @cmpShpSettlementCreditIssueType
    ,[cmpShpSettlementCreditPerBuy] = @cmpShpSettlementCreditPerBuy
    ,[cmpShpSettlementTotalCredit] = @cmpShpSettlementTotalCredit
    ,[cmpShpSettlementCreditRemainShow] = @cmpShpSettlementCreditRemainShow
WHERE CompanyID IN(SELECT T.[CompanyID] FROM @CmpTmpTable T)
/*======================================================================*/
/**/
END

)

BEGIN

FROM dbo.CrmsysDBTempInfo
WHERE UserId = @UserId
AND TypeId = 1
/**/
EXEC (
'UPDATE dbo.CrmProjects 
SET prjIsFinished = 0,
  UserId = ' + @UserId + ' , ' +
' RegisterDatePersian = ' + @RegisterDatePersian +' , ' +
' RegisterDate = getdate() , AppType = ''' + @AppType + ''' ' + ' ' + ' 
WHERE ProjectID IN ( 
SELECT ProjectID FROM ( ' + 
@txt
+ ' )t ) ' )
/**/
END

-- [dbo].[CRMspUpdateFilterProjectsToArchive] | References permission/temp-filter machinery





/*ADBF93068411*/
/*RZ14020717- Change Lenth*/
Create PROCEDURE [dbo].[CRMspUpdateFilterProjectsToArchive]
(	/*--	EXEC CRMspUpdateFilterProjectsToArchive 3*/
@UserId INT,
@prjFinishedDate VARCHAR(8),
@prjFinishedDay VARCHAR(15), 
@prjFinishedTime VARCHAR(15), 
@prjFinishedDateTime DATETIME,
@prjFinishedUserId VARCHAR(15),
@RegisterDatePersian VARCHAR(15),
@AppType NVARCHAR(50)   
 )
AS
BEGIN
DECLARE @txt VARCHAR(MAX)/*RZ14020717- Change Lenth(8000)*/
SET @txt = 'CRMProjects'
SELECT @txt = TXT
FROM dbo.CrmsysDBTempInfo
WHERE UserId = @UserId
AND TypeId = 1

/**/
EXEC (
'UPDATE dbo.CrmProjects 
SET prjIsFinished = 1,
  prjFinishedDate = ' + @prjFinishedDate + ' , ' +
' prjFinishedDay = ' + @prjFinishedDay + ' , ' +
' prjFinishedTime = ''' + @prjFinishedTime + ''' , ' +
' prjFinishedDateTime = CAST(''' + @prjFinishedDateTime + ''' As DATETIME) , ' +
' prjFinishedUserId = ' + @prjFinishedUserId +' , ' +
' UserId = ' + @UserId + ' , ' +
' RegisterDatePersian = ' + @RegisterDatePersian +' , ' +
' RegisterDate = getdate() , AppType = ''' + @AppType + ''' ' + ' ' + ' 
WHERE ProjectID IN ( 
SELECT ProjectID FROM ( ' + 
@txt
+ ' )t ) ' )
/**/
END

-- [dbo].[HRMMobspSearchCompanyQuick] | References permission/temp-filter machinery






/*AI14011203*/
/*AI14020128 - Use MobspSetcmpDataPermission instead of CRM*/
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[HRMMobspSearchCompanyQuick]
(		
/* 
	EXEC HRMMobspSearchCompanyQuick 
	@Val = N'صفری', 
	@UserID = 3,
	@LanguageID  = 1065,
	@DefaultLanguageID  = 1065
		
*/
@Val NVARCHAR(200),
@UserID INT,
@LanguageID INT = NULL,
@DefaultLanguageID INT = NULL
)

AS
BEGIN
DECLARE @fld VARCHAR(MAX), @fldLimit VARCHAR(MAX), @from VARCHAR(2000), @term VARCHAR(MAX)
/*--*/
EXEC MobspSetcmpDataPermission @UserID
DECLARE @cmpDataPermision VARCHAR(4000)
SELECT @cmpDataPermision = TXT
FROM dbo.CrmsysDBTempInfo
WHERE TypeId = 20
AND UserId = @UserId
/*--*/

IF(ISNULL(@Val,'') <> '')
BEGIN
	SET @Val = REPLACE(@Val,' ','%')
END

/*--*/
IF(@LanguageID IS NULL OR @LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID)
BEGIN
	SET @fld = 		
		' SELECT DISTINCT TOP 500 CompanyID, CompanyID cmpId,
		ISNULL( 
		ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM dbo.CrmCompanyTitle WHERE CompanyTitleID = C.cmpTitleId), '''') + 
		'' ''+ ISNULL(cmpName, '''') +  
		ISNULL('' - '' +(
		ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM dbo.CrmPersonTitle WHERE PersontitleId = CP.prsTitleId), '''')+ '' '' +
		ISNULL(prsFName, '''') + '' ''+ISNULL(prsLName, '''') ),'''')			
		+ ISNULL( '' - '' + CAST(C.CmpNumber AS NVARCHAR(20)),'''')		
		,'''') CompanyPerson 
		,(CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 0
				THEN ((ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle] CT WHERE CT.[CompanyTitleID] = C.[cmpTitleId]),'''') + '' '' + 
						ISNULL(C.[cmpName],'''')))
				ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId),'''')  + '' '' +
							ISNULL(prsFName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(prsLName,'''')
						FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
						WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
						)				
		END) [CompanyName]
		,[cmpAddress]
		,[cmpCountryId]
		,[cmpProvinceId]
		,[cmpCityId]
		,[cmpCoordinateLatitude]
		,[cmpCoordinateLongitude]
		,[cmpCoordinateZoomLevel]
		'
	/*--------------------------------*/
	SET @fldLimit = 
			' SELECT DISTINCT TOP 500 CompanyID, CompanyID cmpId, 
			ISNULL(
			ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM dbo.CrmCompanyTitle WHERE CompanyTitleID = C.cmpTitleId), '''') + 
			'' ''+ ISNULL(cmpName, '''')  +  	
			ISNULL('' - '' + (
			ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM dbo.CrmPersonTitle WHERE PersontitleId = CP.prsTitleId), '''')+ '' '' +
			ISNULL(prsFName, '''') + '' ''+ISNULL(prsLName, '''')),'''')  + 
			ISNULL('' - '' +CAST(C.CmpNumber AS NVARCHAR(20)),'''')			
			,'''') CompanyPerson 
			,(CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 0
					THEN ((ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle] CT WHERE CT.[CompanyTitleID] = C.[cmpTitleId]),'''') + '' '' + 
							ISNULL(C.[cmpName],'''')))
					ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId),'''')  + '' '' +
								ISNULL(prsFName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(prsLName,'''')
							FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
							WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
							)				
			END) [CompanyName]
			,[cmpAddress]
			,[cmpCountryId]
			,[cmpProvinceId]
			,[cmpCityId]
			,[cmpCoordinateLatitude]
			,[cmpCoordinateLongitude]
			,[cmpCoordinateZoomLevel]
			'
	/*--------------------------------*/					
	SET @term = ' WHERE (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(REPLACE(cmpName, '' '' ,'''') AS NVARCHAR(2000)) ,
	 NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) Like ''%' + 
		REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(@Val AS NVARCHAR(2000)), NCHAR(1740),
		 NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) +'%'')'
	+ ' OR (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(REPLACE(CP.prsLName, '' '' ,'''') AS NVARCHAR(2000)) , NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) Like ''%' + 
		REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(@Val AS NVARCHAR(2000)), NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) +'%'')'

	SET @term += ' OR C.cmpNumber Like ''%' + @Val  +'%'''+
				' OR C.cmpPreTel Like ''%' + @Val  +'%'''+
				' OR C.cmpPreTelII Like ''%' + @Val  +'%'''+   
				' OR C.cmpTel Like ''%' + @Val  +'%'''+
				' OR C.cmpTelII Like ''%' + @Val  +'%'''+
				' OR C.cmpFax Like ''%' + @Val  +'%'''+
				' OR C.cmpMobile Like ''%' + @Val  +'%'''+
				' OR  REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(C.cmpaddress AS NVARCHAR(2000)) , NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) Like ''%' + 
				REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(@Val AS NVARCHAR(2000)), NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) +'%'''+
				' OR  REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(REPLACE(CP.prsFName, '' '' ,'''') AS NVARCHAR(2000)) , NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) Like ''%' + 
				REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(@Val AS NVARCHAR(2000)), NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740))  +'%'''+	    
				' OR CP.prsTel Like ''%' + @Val  +'%'''+
				' OR CP.prsTelII Like ''%' + @Val  +'%'''+
				' OR CP.prsMobile Like ''%' + @Val  +'%'''+
				' OR CP.prsFax Like ''%' + @Val  +'%'''+
				' OR C.CmpCode Like ''%' + @Val  +'%'''+
				' OR C.cmpNationalCode Like ''%' + @Val  +'%'''+
				' OR  REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(REPLACE(C.cmpNameEng, '' '' ,'''') AS NVARCHAR(2000)) , NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) Like ''%' + 
				REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(@Val AS NVARCHAR(2000)), NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740))  +'%'''+	       
				' OR CP.prsNationalCode Like ''%' + @Val  +'%'''
END
ELSE IF(@LanguageID IS NOT NULL AND @LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID)
BEGIN
	SET @fld = 
		' SELECT DISTINCT TOP 500 CompanyID, CompanyID cmpId, 
		ISNULL(
		ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM dbo.CrmCompanyTitle_Lang CTLN WHERE CTLN.CompanyTitleID = C.cmpTitleId  AND CTLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(10)) + '), '''') + 
		'' ''+ ISNULL((SELECT cmpName FROM dbo.CrmCompany_Lang CLN WHERE CLN.CompanyID = C.CompanyID  AND CLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(10)) + '), '''')  + 
		ISNULL('' - '' + (
		ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM dbo.CrmPersonTitle_Lang PTLN WHERE PTLN.PersontitleId = CP.prsTitleId  AND PTLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(10)) + '), '''')+ '' '' +
		ISNULL((SELECT prsFName FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons_Lang CPLN WHERE CPLN.CompanyPersonID = CP.CompanyPersonID  AND CPLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(10)) + '), '''') 
		+ '' ''+ISNULL((SELECT prsLName FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons_Lang CPLN WHERE CPLN.CompanyPersonID = CP.CompanyPersonID  AND CPLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(10)) + '), ''''))  ,'''')		
		+ ISNULL( '' - '' +  CAST(C.CmpNumber AS NVARCHAR(20)),''''),'''') CompanyPerson 
		,(CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 0
				THEN ((ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle_Lang] CT WHERE CT.[CompanyTitleID] = C.[cmpTitleId] AND CT.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(10)) + '),'''') + '' '' + 
						ISNULL((SELECT CLN.[cmpName] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany_Lang] CLN WHERE CLN.CompanyID = C.CompanyID AND CLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(10)) + '),'''')))
				ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle_Lang] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId AND PT.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(10)) + '),'''')  + '' '' +
							ISNULL((SELECT ISNULL(CCPLN.prsFName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(CCPLN.prsLName,'''') FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons_Lang] CCPLN WHERE CCPLN.CompanyPersonID = CCP.CompanyPersonID AND CCPLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(10)) + '),'''')
					FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
					WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
					)				
			END) [CompanyName]
		,(SELECT [cmpAddress] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany_Lang] CCL WHERE CCL.[CompanyID] = C.CompanyID AND CCL.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(10)) + ') [cmpAddress]
		,[cmpCountryId]
		,[cmpProvinceId]
		,[cmpCityId]
		,[cmpCoordinateLatitude]
		,[cmpCoordinateLongitude]
		,[cmpCoordinateZoomLevel]
		'
	/*--------------------------------*/
	SET @fldLimit = 
			' SELECT DISTINCT TOP 500 CompanyID, CompanyID cmpId, 
			ISNULL(
			ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM dbo.CrmCompanyTitle_Lang CTLN WHERE CTLN.CompanyTitleID = C.cmpTitleId AND CTLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(10)) + '), '''') + 
			'' ''+ ISNULL((SELECT cmpName FROM dbo.CrmCompany_Lang CLN WHERE CLN.CompanyID = C.CompanyID  AND CLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(10)) + '), '''')  +
			ISNULL('' - '' + 		
			(ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM dbo.CrmPersonTitle_Lang PTLN WHERE PTLN.PersontitleId = CP.prsTitleId AND PTLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(10)) + '), '''')+ '' '' +
			ISNULL((SELECT prsFName FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons_Lang CPLN WHERE CPLN.CompanyPersonID = CP.CompanyPersonID  AND CPLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(10)) + '), '''') + '' ''
			+ISNULL((SELECT prsLName FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons_Lang CPLN WHERE CPLN.CompanyPersonID = CP.CompanyPersonID  AND CPLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(10)) + '), '''')),'''')   			
			+ ISNULL('' - '' +  CAST(C.CmpNumber AS NVARCHAR(20)),'''')			
			,'''') CompanyPerson 
						,(CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 0
					THEN ((ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle_Lang] CT WHERE CT.[CompanyTitleID] = C.[cmpTitleId] AND CT.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(10)) + '),'''') + '' '' + 
							ISNULL((SELECT CLN.[cmpName] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany_Lang] CLN WHERE CLN.CompanyID = C.CompanyID AND CLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(10)) + '),'''')))
					ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle_Lang] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId AND PT.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(10)) + '),'''')  + '' '' +
								ISNULL((SELECT ISNULL(CCPLN.prsFName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(CCPLN.prsLName,'''') FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons_Lang] CCPLN WHERE CCPLN.CompanyPersonID = CCP.CompanyPersonID AND CCPLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(10)) + '),'''')
						FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
						WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
						)				
				END) [CompanyName]
			,(SELECT [cmpAddress] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany_Lang] CCL WHERE CCL.[CompanyID] = C.CompanyID AND CCL.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(10)) + ') [cmpAddress]
			,[cmpCountryId]
			,[cmpProvinceId]
			,[cmpCityId]
			,[cmpCoordinateLatitude]
			,[cmpCoordinateLongitude]
			,[cmpCoordinateZoomLevel]
			'
	/*--------------------------------*/							
	SET @term = ' WHERE (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(ISNULL((SELECT REPLACE(cmpName, '' '' ,'''') FROM dbo.CrmCompany_Lang CLN WHERE CLN.CompanyID = C.CompanyID  AND CLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(10)) + '), cmpName) AS NVARCHAR(2000)) ,
	 NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) Like ''%' + 
		REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(@Val AS NVARCHAR(2000)), NCHAR(1740),
		 NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) +'%'')'
	+ ' OR (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(ISNULL((SELECT prsLName FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons_Lang CPLN WHERE CPLN.CompanyPersonID = CP.CompanyPersonID  AND CPLN.LanguageID = ' 
	+ CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(10)) + '), CP.prsLName) AS NVARCHAR(2000)) , NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) Like ''%' + 
		REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(@Val AS NVARCHAR(2000)), NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) +'%'')'

	
	SET @term += ' OR C.cmpNumber Like ''%' + @Val  +'%'''+
				' OR C.cmpPreTel Like ''%' + @Val  +'%'''+
				' OR C.cmpPreTelII Like ''%' + @Val  +'%'''+   
				' OR C.cmpTel Like ''%' + @Val  +'%'''+
				' OR C.cmpTelII Like ''%' + @Val  +'%'''+
				' OR C.cmpFax Like ''%' + @Val  +'%'''+
				' OR C.cmpMobile Like ''%' + @Val  +'%'''+
				' OR  ISNULL((SELECT REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(cmpaddress AS NVARCHAR(2000)) , NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) FROM dbo.CrmCompany_Lang CCLN WHERE CCLN.CompanyID = C.CompanyID AND CCLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(MAX)) + '),'''') Like ''%' + 
					REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(@Val AS NVARCHAR(2000)), NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) +'%'''+	       
				' OR  ISNULL((SELECT REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(REPLACE(prsFName, '' '' ,'''') AS NVARCHAR(2000)) , NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons_Lang PPLN WHERE PPLN.CompanyPersonID = CP.CompanyPersonID AND PPLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(MAX)) + '),'''') Like ''%' + 
				REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(@Val AS NVARCHAR(2000)), NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740))  +'%'''+	  
				' OR CP.prsTel Like ''%' + @Val  +'%'''+
				' OR CP.prsTelII Like ''%' + @Val  +'%'''+
				' OR CP.prsMobile Like ''%' + @Val  +'%'''+
				' OR CP.prsFax Like ''%' + @Val  +'%'''+
				' OR C.CmpCode Like ''%' + @Val  +'%'''+
				' OR C.cmpNationalCode Like ''%' + @Val  +'%'''+
				' OR  REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(REPLACE(C.cmpNameEng, '' '' ,'''') AS NVARCHAR(2000)) , NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) Like ''%' + 
				REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(@Val AS NVARCHAR(2000)), NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740))  +'%'''+	     
				' OR CP.prsNationalCode Like ''%' + @Val  +'%'''
END		

SET @from = ' From dbo.CrmCompany C 
			LEFT JOIN 
				dbo.CrmCompanyPersons CP 
					ON (C.CompanyID = CP.prsCompanyId  
						AND	(CP.[FirstRegisterUserId] IN 
							(SELECT ' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(10), @UserID) + ' UNION 
							SELECT LinkedUserId FROM dbo.CrmLinkedUsers 
							WHERE CrmUserId = ' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(10), @UserID) + ' AND LinkedTypeId = 23)
							OR (CP.[FirstRegisterUserId] IS NULL)
							) 
						)'

/*--*/
/*--*/
/*--*/
IF @cmpDataPermision <> ''
BEGIN
EXECUTE(
' SELECT * FROM ( ' +
@fld+ @from+ @term +@cmpDataPermision +
' ) C union ' + 
' SELECT * FROM ( ' +
@fldLimit+ @from+ @term +
') d
where CmpId Not IN ( SELECT CompanyId '+
@from+ @term +@cmpDataPermision +' )'
)
END
ELSE
EXECUTE(@fld+ @from+ @term)

END

-- [dbo].[HRMMobspSelectCompanyInsidePolygon] | References permission/temp-filter machinery






/*AI14010525*/
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[HRMMobspSelectCompanyInsidePolygon]
(	
/* 
	EXEC [HRMMobspSelectCompanyInsidePolygon] 3, '35.732304772975155 51.52599222958088, 35.7156426418645 51.52599222958088, 35.7156426418645 51.51493716984987, 35.732304772975155 51.51493716984987, 35.732304772975155 51.52599222958088', 1065, 1065
*/
@UserId INT,
@Polygon NVARCHAR(MAX),
@LanguageID INT = NULL,
@DefaultLanguageID INT = NULL
)
AS 
BEGIN
/**/

EXEC dbo.MobspSetcmpDataPermission @UserID
DECLARE @clmn VARCHAR(MAX), @FROM VARCHAR(MAX), @Term VARCHAR(MAX)

IF(@LanguageID IS NULL OR @LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID)
BEGIN
	SET @clmn = 
		'SELECT DISTINCT
		C.CompanyID
		,C.[cmpNumber]
		,(CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 0
			THEN ISNULL(C.[cmpName],'''')
			ELSE (SELECT ISNULL(prsFName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(prsLName,'''')
				FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
				WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]			
				)				
			END) [cmpName]
		,C.[cmpReferUserId]
		,C.[cmpCoordinateLatitude]
		,C.[cmpCoordinateLongitude]						
		,(CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 0
			THEN ((ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle] CT WHERE CT.[CompanyTitleID] = C.[cmpTitleId]),'''') + '' '' + 
					ISNULL(C.[cmpName],'''')))
			ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId),'''')  + '' '' +
						ISNULL(prsFName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(prsLName,'''')
				FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
				WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]	
						
				)				
			END) [CompanyName]						
		,(SELECT ISNULL(FName , '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(LName,'''') FROM dbo.Users U WHERE U.UserId = C.cmpReferUserId) [cmpReferUser]						
		'
/*--*/

	SET @Term = '
		AND ISNULL([cmpCoordinateLatitude], '''') <> ''''
		AND ISNULL([cmpCoordinateLongitude], '''') <> ''''
		AND geometry::STGeomFromText(''POLYGON((' + CAST(@Polygon AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) + '))'',0).STContains(
			geometry::STGeomFromText(''POINT('' + CAST([cmpCoordinateLatitude] AS NVARCHAR(100)) + '' '' + CAST([cmpCoordinateLongitude] AS NVARCHAR(100)) + '')'', 0)) = 1
	'

END
ELSE IF(@LanguageID IS NOT NULL AND @LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID)
BEGIN
	SET @clmn = 
		'SELECT DISTINCT
		 C.CompanyID
		,C.[cmpNumber]
		,(CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 0
			THEN (ISNULL((SELECT CLN.[cmpName] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany_Lang] CLN WHERE CLN.CompanyID = C.CompanyID AND CLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(MAX)) + '),''''))
			ELSE (SELECT ISNULL((SELECT ISNULL(CCPLN.prsFName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(CCPLN.prsLName,'''') FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons_Lang] CCPLN WHERE CCPLN.CompanyPersonID = CCP.CompanyPersonID AND CCPLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(MAX)) + '),'''')
				FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
				WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]
				)				
			END) [cmpName]
		,C.[cmpReferUserId]
		,C.[cmpCoordinateLatitude]
		,C.[cmpCoordinateLongitude]
		,(CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 0
			THEN ((ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle_Lang] CT WHERE CT.[CompanyTitleID] = C.[cmpTitleId] AND CT.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(MAX)) + '),'''') + '' '' + 
					ISNULL((SELECT CLN.[cmpName] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany_Lang] CLN WHERE CLN.CompanyID = C.CompanyID AND CLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(MAX)) + '),'''')))
			ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle_Lang] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId AND PT.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(MAX)) + '),'''')  + '' '' +
						ISNULL((SELECT ISNULL(CCPLN.prsFName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(CCPLN.prsLName,'''') FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons_Lang] CCPLN WHERE CCPLN.CompanyPersonID = CCP.CompanyPersonID AND CCPLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(MAX)) + '),'''')
				FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
				WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]
				)				
			END) [CompanyName]
		,(SELECT ISNULL(FName , '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(LName,'''') FROM dbo.Users_Lang U WHERE U.UserId = C.cmpReferUserId AND U.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(MAX)) + ') [cmpReferUser]
		'
/*--*/

	SET @Term = '
		AND ISNULL([cmpCoordinateLatitude], '''') <> ''''
		AND ISNULL([cmpCoordinateLongitude], '''') <> ''''
		AND geometry::STGeomFromText(''POLYGON((''' + CAST(@Polygon AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) + '''))'',0).STContains(
			geometry::STGeomFromText(''POINT('' + CAST([cmpCoordinateLatitude] AS NVARCHAR(100)) + '' '' + CAST([cmpCoordinateLongitude] AS NVARCHAR(100)) + '')'', 0)) = 1
	'
END

/*--*/

	SET @FROM =
	' FROM dbo.CrmCompany C 
			LEFT JOIN 
				dbo.CrmCompanyPersons P 
					ON C.CompanyID = P.prsCompanyId	
	  WHERE 1=1 '

/*--*/	

DECLARE @cmpDataPermision VARCHAR(max)
SELECT @cmpDataPermision = TXT
FROM dbo.CrmsysDBTempInfo
WHERE TypeId = 20
AND UserId = @UserId

/*--*/

EXECUTE(@clmn + @FROM + @Term + @cmpDataPermision)

/*--*/

DELETE dbo.CrmsysDBTempInfo
WHERE TypeId = 10
AND UserId = @UserId

/*--*/

INSERT INTO dbo.CrmsysDBTempInfo (
	UserId,	TXT, TypeId)
 VALUES ( @UserId, ' SELECT CompanyId ' + @from + @Term, 10 ) 
/**/
END







GO
-- [dbo].[MobspCheckActionForUser] | Exact target





/*AI14020529*/
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[MobspCheckActionForUser]
/*
	DECLARE @HasAction BIT
	EXEC MobspCheckActionForUser N'frmPreOrderDataEntry', N'actAutoCalcNumRef', 3, @HasAction OUTPUT
	SELECT @HasAction
*/
(
@FormName NVARCHAR(50),
@ActionName NVARCHAR(50),
@UserId INT,
@HasAction BIT OUTPUT
)
AS
BEGIN
	IF EXISTS (
		SELECT * FROM dbo.CrmsysRollPermission
		WHERE FormId = (SELECT ID FROM dbo.CrmsysForms WHERE FormName = @FormName )
		AND FormActionId = (SELECT Id FROM dbo.CrmsysFormActions WHERE ActionName = @ActionName
							AND FormId = (SELECT ID FROM dbo.CrmsysForms WHERE FormName = @FormName ))
		AND RollId = (SELECT TOP 1 RollId FROM dbo.UsersPermission WHERE UserId = @UserId AND PrjId = 11/*CRM*/))
	BEGIN
		SET @HasAction = 1
	END
	ELSE
	BEGIN
		SET @HasAction = 0
	END
END







GO
-- [dbo].[MobspCheckCmpDataPermissionForUser] | Exact target




/*RZ971221*/
/*AI990313- add user site*/
CREATE Procedure [dbo].[MobspCheckCmpDataPermissionForUser]
(
/*  
	exec MobspCheckCmpDataPermissionForUser 		
*/
@Mode INT,
@Id INT,
@CrmUserId INT,
@IsAccess BIT OUTPUT
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
	DECLARE @CheckForbidden BIT = 0, @CheckJustAccess BIT = 1
	IF(@CrmUserId= -10) /*AI990313 user is site*/
	BEGIN
		SET @IsAccess = 1
	END
	ELSE
	IF(@Mode = 1)/*Province*/
	BEGIN
		/**/
		/*Forbidden*/
		IF((SELECT COUNT(*) FROM dbo.CrmCompanyForbiddenProvince
			WHERE CrmUserId = @CrmUserId) > 0)
		BEGIN
			IF ((SELECT TOP 1 [ForbiddenProvinceId] FROM dbo.CrmCompanyForbiddenProvince
				WHERE CrmUserId = @CrmUserId
				AND [ForbiddenProvinceId] = @Id) IS NOT NULL)
				SET @CheckForbidden = 1 
			ELSE
				SET @CheckForbidden = 0
		END
		/*JustAccess*/
		IF((SELECT COUNT(*) FROM dbo.CrmCompanyJustAccessProvince
			WHERE CrmUserId = @CrmUserId ) > 0)
		BEGIN
			IF((SELECT TOP 1 [JustAccessProvinceId]     
				FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyJustAccessProvince]
				WHERE CrmUserId = @CrmUserId
				AND [JustAccessProvinceId] = @Id) IS NULL)
				SET @CheckJustAccess = 0
			ELSE
				SET @CheckJustAccess = 1
		END
		/*Set Access*/
		IF(@CheckForbidden = 0 AND @CheckJustAccess = 1)
			SET @IsAccess = 1		
		/**/
	END
	/*---------------------------------*/
	ELSE IF(@Mode = 2)/*City*/
	BEGIN
		/**/
		/*Forbidden*/
		IF((SELECT COUNT(*) FROM dbo.CrmCompanyForbiddenCity
			WHERE CrmUserId = @CrmUserId) > 0)
		BEGIN
			IF ((SELECT TOP 1 ForbiddenCityId FROM dbo.CrmCompanyForbiddenCity
				WHERE CrmUserId = @CrmUserId
				AND [ForbiddenCityId] = @Id) IS NOT NULL)
				SET @CheckForbidden = 1 
			ELSE
				SET @CheckForbidden = 0
		END
		/*JustAccess*/
		IF((SELECT COUNT(*) FROM dbo.CrmCompanyJustAccessCity
			WHERE CrmUserId = @CrmUserId ) > 0)
		BEGIN
			IF((SELECT TOP 1 [JustAccessCityId]     
				FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyJustAccessCity]
				WHERE CrmUserId = @CrmUserId
				AND [JustAccessCityId] = @Id) IS NULL)
				SET @CheckJustAccess = 0
			ELSE
				SET @CheckJustAccess = 1
		END
		/*Set Access*/
		IF(@CheckForbidden = 0 AND @CheckJustAccess = 1)
			SET @IsAccess = 1		
		/**/
	END
	/*---------------------------------*/
	ELSE IF(@Mode = 3)/*Group*/
	BEGIN
		/**/
		/*Forbidden*/
		IF((SELECT COUNT(*) FROM dbo.CrmCompanyForbiddenGroup
			WHERE CrmUserId = @CrmUserId) > 0)
		BEGIN
			IF ((SELECT TOP 1 ForbiddenGroupId FROM dbo.CrmCompanyForbiddenGroup
				WHERE CrmUserId = @CrmUserId
				AND ForbiddenGroupId = @Id) IS NOT NULL)
				SET @CheckForbidden = 1 
			ELSE
				SET @CheckForbidden = 0
		END
		/*JustAccess*/
		IF((SELECT COUNT(*) FROM dbo.CrmCompanyJustAccessGroup
			WHERE CrmUserId = @CrmUserId ) > 0)
		BEGIN
			IF((SELECT TOP 1 [JustAccessGroupId]     
				FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyJustAccessGroup]
				WHERE CrmUserId = @CrmUserId
				AND [JustAccessGroupId] = @Id) IS NULL)
				SET @CheckJustAccess = 0
			ELSE
				SET @CheckJustAccess = 1
		END
		/*Set Access*/
		IF(@CheckForbidden = 0 AND @CheckJustAccess = 1)
			SET @IsAccess = 1		
		/**/
	END
	/*---------------------------------*/
	ELSE IF(@Mode = 4)/*SubGroup*/
	BEGIN
		/**/
		/*Forbidden*/
		IF((SELECT COUNT(*) FROM dbo.CrmCompanyForbiddenSubGroup
			WHERE CrmUserId = @CrmUserId) > 0)
		BEGIN
			IF ((SELECT TOP 1 ForbiddenSubGroupId FROM dbo.CrmCompanyForbiddenSubGroup
				WHERE CrmUserId = @CrmUserId
				AND ForbiddenSubGroupId = @Id) IS NOT NULL)
				SET @CheckForbidden = 1 
			ELSE
				SET @CheckForbidden = 0
		END
		/*JustAccess*/
		IF((SELECT COUNT(*) FROM dbo.CrmCompanyJustAccessSubGroup
			WHERE CrmUserId = @CrmUserId ) > 0)
		BEGIN
			IF((SELECT TOP 1 [JustAccessSubGroupId]     
				FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyJustAccessSubGroup]
				WHERE CrmUserId = @CrmUserId
				AND [JustAccessSubGroupId] = @Id) IS NULL)
				SET @CheckJustAccess = 0
			ELSE
				SET @CheckJustAccess = 1
		END
		/*Set Access*/
		IF(@CheckForbidden = 0 AND @CheckJustAccess = 1)
			SET @IsAccess = 1		
		/**/
	END
	/*---------------------------------*/
	ELSE IF(@Mode = 5)/*GroupIV*/
	BEGIN
		/**/
		/*Forbidden*/
		IF((SELECT COUNT(*) FROM dbo.CrmCompanyForbiddenGroupIV
			WHERE CrmUserId = @CrmUserId) > 0)
		BEGIN
			IF ((SELECT TOP 1 ForbiddenGroupIVId FROM dbo.CrmCompanyForbiddenGroupIV
				WHERE CrmUserId = @CrmUserId
				AND ForbiddenGroupIVId = @Id) IS NOT NULL)
				SET @CheckForbidden = 1 
			ELSE
				SET @CheckForbidden = 0
		END
		/*JustAccess*/
		IF((SELECT COUNT(*) FROM dbo.CrmCompanyJustAccessGroupIV
			WHERE CrmUserId = @CrmUserId ) > 0)
		BEGIN
			IF((SELECT TOP 1 [JustAccessGroupIVId]     
				FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyJustAccessGroupIV]
				WHERE CrmUserId = @CrmUserId
				AND [JustAccessGroupIVId] = @Id) IS NULL)
				SET @CheckJustAccess = 0
			ELSE
				SET @CheckJustAccess = 1
		END
		/*Set Access*/
		IF(@CheckForbidden = 0 AND @CheckJustAccess = 1)
			SET @IsAccess = 1		
		/**/
	END
	/*---------------------------------*/
	ELSE IF(@Mode = 6)/*Company*/
	BEGIN
		/**/
		/*Forbidden*/
		IF((SELECT COUNT(*) FROM dbo.CrmCompanyForbiddenCompany
			WHERE CrmUserId = @CrmUserId) > 0)
		BEGIN
			IF ((SELECT TOP 1 ForbiddenCompanyId FROM dbo.CrmCompanyForbiddenCompany
				WHERE CrmUserId = @CrmUserId
				AND ForbiddenCompanyId = @Id) IS NOT NULL)
				SET @CheckForbidden = 1 
			ELSE
				SET @CheckForbidden = 0
		END
		/*JustAccess*/
		IF((SELECT COUNT(*) FROM dbo.CrmCompanyJustAccessCompany
			WHERE CrmUserId = @CrmUserId ) > 0)
		BEGIN
			IF((SELECT TOP 1 [JustAccessCompanyId]     
				FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyJustAccessCompany]
				WHERE CrmUserId = @CrmUserId
				AND [JustAccessCompanyId] = @Id) IS NULL)
				SET @CheckJustAccess = 0
			ELSE
				SET @CheckJustAccess = 1
		END
		/*Set Access*/
		IF(@CheckForbidden = 0 AND @CheckJustAccess = 1)
			SET @IsAccess = 1		
		/**/
	END
	/*---------------------------------*/
	ELSE IF(@Mode = 7)/*cmpReferUser*/
	BEGIN
		/**/
		
		/*Set Access*/
		IF(
			(SELECT * FROM
			(SELECT @CrmUserId ReferUserId 
			 UNION 
			 SELECT LinkedUserId FROM dbo.CrmLinkedUsers 
			 WHERE CrmUserId = @CrmUserId 
			 AND LinkedTypeId = 14 ) as tmp
			 WHERE ReferUserId = @Id			 			 
			 ) IS NOT NULL		
		  )
			SET @IsAccess = 1		
		/**/
	END
	/*---------------------------------*/
	ELSE IF(@Mode = 8)/*Access see company with all permission*/
	BEGIN
		/**/
		DECLARE @Qry NVARCHAR(MAX) = '' , @cmpDataPermision VARCHAR(max) = '', @CmpId INT		
		/*SET DataPermission*/		 
		EXEC dbo.MobspSetcmpDataPermission @CrmUserId
		SELECT @cmpDataPermision = TXT
		FROM dbo.CrmsysDBTempInfo
		WHERE TypeId = 20
		AND UserId = @CrmUserId
		/*SET Query*/
		SET @Qry = 'SELECT @CmpId = [CompanyID] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany] C 
					WHERE CompanyID = ' + CAST(@Id AS VARCHAR(MAX)) + ' ' + ISNULL(@cmpDataPermision,'')		
		/*RUN Query*/
        EXECUTE sp_executesql @Qry, N' @CmpId INT OUTPUT', @CmpId OUTPUT			
		/*Set Access*/
		IF(@CmpId IS NOT NULL)
			SET @IsAccess = 1		
		/**/
	END
/**/
END




GO
-- [dbo].[MobspCheckDuplicateCompany] | References permission/temp-filter machinery







/*RZ960926*/

/*AI14001030 - Change sp - Copied from web and remove unnecessary fields*/
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[MobspCheckDuplicateCompany]
(		
/*
	EXEC MobspCheckDuplicateCompany 0, 0, 't', 0, 2, 99, N'lack', 1065, 1065
*/
@Type int, @Id int, @Val NVarChar(50), @TitleID INT, @CityId INT, @UserID INT ,
@LackOfShowText NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
@LanguageID INT = NULL,
@DefaultLanguageID INT = NULL
)

AS
BEGIN
/**/
IF(@LackOfShowText IS NULL OR @LackOfShowText = '')
	SET @LackOfShowText = N'عدم نمايش'

DECLARE @fld VARCHAR(MAX), @fldLimit VARCHAR(MAX), @from VARCHAR(MAX), @term VARCHAR(MAX)
/*--*/
EXEC CRMspSetcmpDataPermission @UserID
DECLARE @cmpDataPermision VARCHAR(4000)
SELECT @cmpDataPermision = TXT
FROM dbo.CrmsysDBTempInfo
WHERE TypeId = 20
AND UserId = @UserId
/*--*/
/*--*/
DECLARE @Word NVARCHAR(50)
IF(@LanguageID IS NULL OR @LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID)
BEGIN
	SET @fld = 
		' Select DISTINCT TOP 100 CompanyID, CompanyID cmpId,
		(CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 0
			THEN (ISNULL(C.[cmpName],''''))
			ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL(prsFName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(prsLName,'''')
				FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
				WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
				)				
		END)cmpName,
		(CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 0
			THEN ((ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle] CT WHERE CT.[CompanyTitleID] = C.[cmpTitleId]),'''')))
			ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId),'''')
				FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
				WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
				)				
		END)Title,
		(CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 0
			THEN ((ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle] CT WHERE CT.[CompanyTitleID] = C.[cmpTitleId]),'''') + '' '' + 
					ISNULL(C.[cmpName],'''')))
			ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId),'''')  + '' '' +
						ISNULL(prsFName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(prsLName,'''')
				FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
				WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
				)				
		END)CompanyWithTitle,
		cmpNumber'
		/*--------------------------------*/
	/*DECLARE @Word NVARCHAR(50)*/
	SET @Word = @LackOfShowText
	SET @fldLimit = 
		' Select DISTINCT TOP 100 CompanyID, CompanyID cmpId, 
		(CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 0
			THEN (ISNULL(C.[cmpName],''''))
			ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL(prsFName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(prsLName,'''')
				FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
				WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
				)				
		END)cmpName,
		(CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 0
			THEN ((ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle] CT WHERE CT.[CompanyTitleID] = C.[cmpTitleId]),'''')))
			ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId),'''')
				FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
				WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
				)				
		END)Title,
		(CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 0
			THEN ((ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle] CT WHERE CT.[CompanyTitleID] = C.[cmpTitleId]),'''') + '' '' + 
					ISNULL(C.[cmpName],'''')))
			ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId),'''')  + '' '' +
						ISNULL(prsFName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(prsLName,'''')
				FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
				WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
				)				
		END)CompanyWithTitle,
		cmpNumber'
	/*--------------------------------*/						
	IF @Type = 0  /*For Search Likely*/
	BEGIN
		IF @Id > 0 /*Edit Mode*/
		BEGIN
		SET @from = 
				' From CRMCompany C LEFT JOIN dbo.CrmCompanyPersons CP ON C.CompanyID = CP.prsCompanyId '
		SET @term= 
				' Where ( REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(cmpName AS NVARCHAR(2000)) ,
					NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) Like (''%'+ REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(@Val AS NVARCHAR(2000)), NCHAR(1740),
					NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) +'%'')    
				OR cmpTel = '''+@Val+
				''' OR cmpTelII = '''+@Val+''' OR cmpFax = '''+@Val+''' OR prsTel = '''+@Val+
				''' OR prsTelII = '''+@Val+''' OR prsFax = '''+@Val+''' OR prsMobile = '''+@Val+
				''' OR prsEmail = '''+@Val+''' OR prsEmailII = '''+@Val+''' 
				OR REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(CP.prsLName AS NVARCHAR(2000)) , NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) Like (''%'+
					REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(@Val AS NVARCHAR(2000)), NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) +'%'') ) 
				  And CompanyID <> '+Cast(@Id AS VARCHAR(50))
		END
		ELSE IF @id = 0 /*Insert Mode*/
		BEGIN 
		SET @from =
				' From CRMCompany C LEFT JOIN dbo.CrmCompanyPersons CP ON C.CompanyID = CP.prsCompanyId'
		SET @term = 
				' Where ( REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(cmpName AS NVARCHAR(2000)) ,
					NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) Like (''%'+ REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(@Val AS NVARCHAR(2000)), NCHAR(1740),
					NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) +'%'') 
				OR cmpTel = '''+@Val+
				''' OR cmpTelII = '''+@Val+''' OR cmpFax = '''+@Val+''' OR prsTel = '''+@Val+
				''' OR prsTelII = '''+@Val+''' OR prsFax = '''+@Val+''' OR prsMobile = '''+@Val+
				''' OR prsEmail = '''+@Val+''' OR prsEmailII = '''+@Val+''' 
				OR REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(CP.prsLName AS NVARCHAR(2000)) , NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) Like (''%'+
				REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(@Val AS NVARCHAR(2000)), NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) +'%'') )' 
			end
		END 
	ELSE IF (@Type = 1  AND @TitleID <> 0)  /*For Search "=" (before post)*/
	BEGIN 
		SET @from =
			' From CRMCompany C  LEFT JOIN dbo.CrmCompanyPersons CP ON C.CompanyID = CP.prsCompanyId'
		SET @term = 	
			' Where ( REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(cmpName AS NVARCHAR(2000)) ,
				NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) = '''+ REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(@Val AS NVARCHAR(2000)), NCHAR(1740),
				NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) + '''  
			OR cmpTel = '''+@Val+
				''' OR cmpTelII = '''+@Val+''' OR cmpFax = '''+@Val+''' OR prsTel = '''+@Val+ ''''+
			 ') AND cmpTitleId = ' + Cast(@TitleID AS VARCHAR(50))+
			 ' AND cmpCityId = ' + Cast(@CityId AS VARCHAR(50))+
			 ' And CompanyID <> ' + Cast(@Id AS VARCHAR(50))
	END
	ELSE IF (@Type = 1  AND @TitleID = 0)  /*For Search "=" (before post)*/
	BEGIN
		SET @from =
			' From CRMCompany C  LEFT JOIN dbo.CrmCompanyPersons CP ON C.CompanyID = CP.prsCompanyId'
		SET @term = 	
			' Where ( REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(cmpName AS NVARCHAR(2000)) ,
				NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) = '''+ REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(@Val AS NVARCHAR(2000)), NCHAR(1740),
				NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) + '''  
			OR cmpTel = '''+@Val+
				''' OR cmpTelII = '''+@Val+''' OR cmpFax = '''+@Val+''' OR prsTel = '''+@Val+ ''''+
			  /*AND cmpTitleId = @TitleID*/
			') AND cmpCityId = ' + Cast(@CityId AS VARCHAR(50))+
			' And CompanyID <> ' + Cast(@Id AS VARCHAR(50))
END
/*--*/
END
ELSE IF(@LanguageID IS NOT NULL AND @LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID)
BEGIN
	SET @fld = 
		' Select DISTINCT TOP 100 CompanyID, CompanyID cmpId, 
		(CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 0
			THEN (ISNULL((SELECT CLN.[cmpName] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany_Lang] CLN WHERE CLN.CompanyID = C.CompanyID AND CLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) + '),''''))
			ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT ISNULL(CCPLN.prsFName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(CCPLN.prsLName,'''') FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons_Lang] CCPLN WHERE CCPLN.CompanyPersonID = CCP.CompanyPersonID AND CCPLN.LanguageID =' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) + '),'''')
				FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
				WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
				)				
		END)cmpName,
		(CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 0
			THEN ((ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle_Lang] CT WHERE CT.[CompanyTitleID] = C.[cmpTitleId] AND CT.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) + '),'''')))
			ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle_Lang] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId AND PT.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) + '),'''')
				FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
				WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
				)				
		END)Title,
		(CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 0
			THEN ((ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle_Lang] CT WHERE CT.[CompanyTitleID] = C.[cmpTitleId] AND CT.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) + '),'''') + '' '' + 
					ISNULL((SELECT CLN.[cmpName] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany_Lang] CLN WHERE CLN.CompanyID = C.CompanyID AND CLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) + '),'''')))
			ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle_Lang] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId AND PT.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) + '),'''')  + '' '' +
						ISNULL((SELECT ISNULL(CCPLN.prsFName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(CCPLN.prsLName,'''') FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons_Lang] CCPLN WHERE CCPLN.CompanyPersonID = CCP.CompanyPersonID AND CCPLN.LanguageID =' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) + '),'''')
				FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
				WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
				)				
		END)CompanyWithTitle,
		cmpNumber'
	/*--------------------------------*/	
	SET @Word = @LackOfShowText
	SET @fldLimit = 
		' Select DISTINCT TOP 100 CompanyID, CompanyID cmpId, 
		(CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 0
			THEN (ISNULL((SELECT CLN.[cmpName] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany_Lang] CLN WHERE CLN.CompanyID = C.CompanyID AND CLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) + '),''''))
			ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT ISNULL(CCPLN.prsFName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(CCPLN.prsLName,'''') FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons_Lang] CCPLN WHERE CCPLN.CompanyPersonID = CCP.CompanyPersonID AND CCPLN.LanguageID =' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) + '),'''')
				FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
				WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
				)				
		END)cmpName,
		(CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 0
			THEN ((ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle_Lang] CT WHERE CT.[CompanyTitleID] = C.[cmpTitleId] AND CT.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) + '),'''')))
			ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle_Lang] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId AND PT.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) + '),'''')
				FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
				WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
				)				
		END)Title,
		(CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 0
			THEN ((ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle_Lang] CT WHERE CT.[CompanyTitleID] = C.[cmpTitleId] AND CT.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) + '),'''') + '' '' + 
					ISNULL((SELECT CLN.[cmpName] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany_Lang] CLN WHERE CLN.CompanyID = C.CompanyID AND CLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) + '),'''')))
			ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle_Lang] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId AND PT.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) + '),'''')  + '' '' +
						ISNULL((SELECT ISNULL(CCPLN.prsFName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(CCPLN.prsLName,'''') FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons_Lang] CCPLN WHERE CCPLN.CompanyPersonID = CCP.CompanyPersonID AND CCPLN.LanguageID =' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) + '),'''')
				FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
				WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
				)				
		END)CompanyWithTitle,, 
		cmpNumber'
	/*--------------------------------*/						
	IF @Type = 0  /*For Search Likely*/
	BEGIN
		IF @Id > 0 /*Edit Mode*/
		BEGIN
		SET @from = 
				' From CRMCompany C LEFT JOIN dbo.CrmCompanyPersons CP ON C.CompanyID = CP.prsCompanyId '
		SET @term= 
				' Where ( REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(ISNULL((SELECT cmpName FROM dbo.CrmCompany_Lang CLN WHERE CLN.CompanyID = C.CompanyID  AND CLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) + '), cmpName) AS NVARCHAR(2000)) ,
					NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) Like (''%'+ REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(@Val AS NVARCHAR(2000)), NCHAR(1740),
					NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) +'%'')  
				OR cmpTel = '''+@Val+
				''' OR cmpTelII = '''+@Val+''' OR cmpFax = '''+@Val+''' OR prsTel = '''+@Val+
				''' OR prsTelII = '''+@Val+''' OR prsFax = '''+@Val+''' OR prsMobile = '''+@Val+
				''' OR prsEmail = '''+@Val+''' OR prsEmailII = '''+@Val+''' 
				OR REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(ISNULL((SELECT prsLName FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons_Lang CPLN WHERE CPLN.CompanyPersonID = CP.CompanyPersonID  AND CPLN.LanguageID = ' 
					+ CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) + '), CP.prsLName) AS NVARCHAR(2000)) , NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) Like (''%'+
					REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(@Val AS NVARCHAR(2000)), NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) +'%'') ) 
				  And CompanyID <> '+Cast(@Id AS VARCHAR(50))
		END
		ELSE IF @id = 0 /*Insert Mode*/
		BEGIN 
		SET @from =
				' From CRMCompany C LEFT JOIN dbo.CrmCompanyPersons CP ON C.CompanyID = CP.prsCompanyId'
		SET @term = 
				' Where ( REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(ISNULL((SELECT cmpName FROM dbo.CrmCompany_Lang CLN WHERE CLN.CompanyID = C.CompanyID  AND CLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) + '), cmpName) AS NVARCHAR(2000)) ,
						NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) Like (''%'+ 
						REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(@Val AS NVARCHAR(2000)), NCHAR(1740),
						NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) +'%'')  
				OR cmpTel = '''+@Val+
				''' OR cmpTelII = '''+@Val+''' OR cmpFax = '''+@Val+''' OR prsTel = '''+@Val+
				''' OR prsTelII = '''+@Val+''' OR prsFax = '''+@Val+''' OR prsMobile = '''+@Val+
				''' OR prsEmail = '''+@Val+''' OR prsEmailII = '''+@Val+''' 
				OR REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(ISNULL((SELECT prsLName FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons_Lang CPLN WHERE CPLN.CompanyPersonID = CP.CompanyPersonID  AND CPLN.LanguageID = ' 
					+ CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) + '), CP.prsLName) AS NVARCHAR(2000)) , NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) Like (''%'+
					REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(@Val AS NVARCHAR(2000)), NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) +'%'') )' 
			end
		END 
	ELSE IF (@Type = 1  AND @TitleID <> 0)  /*For Search "=" (before post)*/
	BEGIN 
		SET @from =
			' From CRMCompany C  LEFT JOIN dbo.CrmCompanyPersons CP ON C.CompanyID = CP.prsCompanyId'
		SET @term = 	
			' Where ( REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(ISNULL((SELECT cmpName FROM dbo.CrmCompany_Lang CLN WHERE CLN.CompanyID = C.CompanyID  AND CLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) + '), cmpName) AS NVARCHAR(2000)) ,
					NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) = '''+ REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(@Val AS NVARCHAR(2000)), NCHAR(1740),
					NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) + '''  
			OR cmpTel = '''+@Val+
				''' OR cmpTelII = '''+@Val+''' OR cmpFax = '''+@Val+''' OR prsTel = '''+@Val+ ''''+
			 ') AND cmpTitleId = ' + Cast(@TitleID AS VARCHAR(50))+
			 ' AND cmpCityId = ' + Cast(@CityId AS VARCHAR(50))+
			 ' And CompanyID <> ' + Cast(@Id AS VARCHAR(50))
	END
	ELSE IF (@Type = 1  AND @TitleID = 0)  /*For Search "=" (before post)*/
	BEGIN
		SET @from =
			' From CRMCompany C  LEFT JOIN dbo.CrmCompanyPersons CP ON C.CompanyID = CP.prsCompanyId'
		SET @term = 	
			' Where ( REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(ISNULL((SELECT cmpName FROM dbo.CrmCompany_Lang CLN WHERE CLN.CompanyID = C.CompanyID  AND CLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) + '), cmpName) AS NVARCHAR(2000)) ,
					NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) = '''+ 
					REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(@Val AS NVARCHAR(2000)), NCHAR(1740),
					NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) + '''  
			OR cmpTel = '''+@Val+
				''' OR cmpTelII = '''+@Val+''' OR cmpFax = '''+@Val+''' OR prsTel = '''+@Val+ ''''+
			  /*AND cmpTitleId = @TitleID*/
			') AND cmpCityId = ' + Cast(@CityId AS VARCHAR(50))+
			' And CompanyID <> ' + Cast(@Id AS VARCHAR(50))
END
END


/*--*/
/*--*/
IF @cmpDataPermision <> ''
BEGIN
EXECUTE(
' SELECT * FROM ( ' +
@fld+ @from+ @term +@cmpDataPermision +
' ) C union ' + 
' SELECT * FROM ( ' +
@fldLimit+ @from+ @term +
') d
where CmpId Not IN ( SELECT CompanyId '+
@from+ @term +@cmpDataPermision +' )'
)
END
ELSE
EXECUTE(@fld+ @from+ @term)
/**/
END






GO

		'''+@Word+''' cmpTel, 
		'''+@Word+''' cmpTelII,

		(SELECT City FROM dbo.City_LAng CI WHERE CI.CityId = cmpCityId AND CI.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) + ') City,
		'''+@Word+''' cmpAddress,
		ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM dbo.CrmPersonTitle_Lang PT WHERE PT.PersontitleId = prsTitleId AND PT.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) + '), '''')+ '' '' +

		'''+@Word+''' prsTel, 
		'''+@Word+''' prsTelII, 
		'''+@Word+''' prsFax, 
		'''+@Word+''' prsMobile, 
		'''+@Word+''' prsEmail, 
		'''+@Word+''' prsEmailII,

		FROM dbo.Users_Lang ULN WHERE ULN.UserId =  C.cmpReferUserId AND ULN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) + ') ReferUser '

					NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) +'%'')  
				OR cmpTel = '''+@Val+
				''' OR cmpTelII = '''+@Val+''' OR cmpFax = '''+@Val+''' OR prsTel = '''+@Val+
				''' OR prsTelII = '''+@Val+''' OR prsFax = '''+@Val+''' OR prsMobile = '''+@Val+
				''' OR prsEmail = '''+@Val+''' OR prsEmailII = '''+@Val+''' 
				OR REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(ISNULL((SELECT prsLName FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons_Lang CPLN WHERE CPLN.CompanyPersonID = CP.CompanyPersonID  AND CPLN.LanguageID = ' 
					+ CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) + '), CP.prsLName) AS NVARCHAR(2000)) , NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) Like (''%'+
				REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(@Val AS NVARCHAR(2000)), NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) +'%'') ) 
				  And CompanyID <> '+Cast(@Id AS VARCHAR(50))
		END
		ELSE IF @id = 0 /*Insert Mode*/
		BEGIN 
		SET @from =
				' From CRMCompany C LEFT JOIN dbo.CrmCompanyPersons CP ON C.CompanyID = CP.prsCompanyId'
		SET @term = 
				' Where ( REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(ISNULL((SELECT cmpName FROM dbo.CrmCompany_Lang CLN WHERE CLN.CompanyID = C.CompanyID  AND CLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) + '), cmpName) AS NVARCHAR(2000)) ,
							NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) Like (''%'+ 
							REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(@Val AS NVARCHAR(2000)), NCHAR(1740),
							NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) +'%'')  
				OR cmpTel = '''+@Val+
				''' OR cmpTelII = '''+@Val+''' OR cmpFax = '''+@Val+''' OR prsTel = '''+@Val+
				''' OR prsTelII = '''+@Val+''' OR prsFax = '''+@Val+''' OR prsMobile = '''+@Val+
				''' OR prsEmail = '''+@Val+''' OR prsEmailII = '''+@Val+''' 
				OR REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(ISNULL((SELECT prsLName FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons_Lang CPLN WHERE CPLN.CompanyPersonID = CP.CompanyPersonID  AND CPLN.LanguageID = ' 
					+ CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) + '), CP.prsLName) AS NVARCHAR(2000)) , NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) Like (''%'+
				REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(@Val AS NVARCHAR(2000)), NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) +'%'') )' 
			end
		END 
	ELSE IF (@Type = 1  AND @TitleID <> 0)  /*For Search "=" (before post)*/
	BEGIN 
		SET @from =
			' From CRMCompany C  LEFT JOIN dbo.CrmCompanyPersons CP ON C.CompanyID = CP.prsCompanyId'
		SET @term = 	
			' Where ( REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(ISNULL((SELECT cmpName FROM dbo.CrmCompany_Lang CLN WHERE CLN.CompanyID = C.CompanyID  AND CLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) + '), cmpName) AS NVARCHAR(2000)) ,
						NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) = '''+ REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(@Val AS NVARCHAR(2000)), NCHAR(1740),
						NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) + '''  
			OR cmpTel = '''+@Val+
				''' OR cmpTelII = '''+@Val+''' OR cmpFax = '''+@Val+''' OR prsTel = '''+@Val+ ''''+
			 ') AND cmpTitleId = ' + Cast(@TitleID AS VARCHAR(50))+
			 ' AND cmpCityId = ' + Cast(@CityId AS VARCHAR(50))+
			 ' And CompanyID <> ' + Cast(@Id AS VARCHAR(50))
	END
	ELSE IF (@Type = 1  AND @TitleID = 0)  /*For Search "=" (before post)*/
	BEGIN
		SET @from =
			' From CRMCompany C  LEFT JOIN dbo.CrmCompanyPersons CP ON C.CompanyID = CP.prsCompanyId'
		SET @term = 	
			' Where ( REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(ISNULL((SELECT cmpName FROM dbo.CrmCompany_Lang CLN WHERE CLN.CompanyID = C.CompanyID  AND CLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) + '), cmpName) AS NVARCHAR(2000)) ,
						NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) = '''+ 
						REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(@Val AS NVARCHAR(2000)), NCHAR(1740),
					NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) + '''  
			OR cmpTel = '''+@Val+
				''' OR cmpTelII = '''+@Val+''' OR cmpFax = '''+@Val+''' OR prsTel = '''+@Val+ ''''+
			  /*AND cmpTitleId = @TitleID*/
			') AND cmpCityId = ' + Cast(@CityId AS VARCHAR(50))+
			' And CompanyID <> ' + Cast(@Id AS VARCHAR(50))
	END
END

/*--*/
/*--*/
/*--*/
IF @cmpDataPermision <> ''
BEGIN
EXECUTE(
' SELECT * FROM ( ' +
@fld+ @from+ @term +@cmpDataPermision +
' ) C union ' + 
' SELECT * FROM ( ' +
@fldLimit+ @from+ @term +
') d
where CmpId Not IN ( SELECT CompanyId '+
@from+ @term +@cmpDataPermision +' )'
)
END
ELSE
EXECUTE(@fld+ @from+ @term)
/**/
END

@ObjectId INT,

)

BEGIN

	IF(LOWER(@SysPart) = 'cmp')

	END

	ELSE IF(LOWER(@SysPart) = 'ssec')

	END

		SET @Trem  = N' [MasterObjectId] = ' + CAST(@ObjectId AS NVARCHAR(50))		
	/**/
	END
	ELSE IF(LOWER(@SysPart) = 'whdraft')
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SET @Trem  = N' [MasterObjectId] = ' + CAST(@ObjectId AS NVARCHAR(50))		
	/**/
	END
	ELSE IF(LOWER(@SysPart) = 'whmovement')
	BEGIN

		SET @Trem  = N' [MasterObjectId] = ' + CAST(@ObjectId AS NVARCHAR(50))

	END

	BEGIN
	/**/
		SET @Trem  = N' [MasterObjectId] = ' + CAST(@ObjectId AS NVARCHAR(50))		
	/**/
	END

	/**/
	END
	/**/

			(SELECT FormFieldValueId 
			 FROM dbo.[CrmDyFormFieldValues] 
			 WHERE MasterValueId IN(
									SELECT [FormFieldMasterValueId]
									FROM [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldMasterValues]

											SELECT [FormId]
											FROM [dbo].[CrmDyForms]

											)
									) ) /**/ '

	SET @Qry = @Qry + N'DELETE dbo.[CrmDyFormFieldValues] 
						WHERE MasterValueId IN(
								SELECT [FormFieldMasterValueId]
								FROM [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldMasterValues]
								WHERE ' + @Trem +
								' AND [FormId] IN (
										SELECT [FormId]

										OR [FOSysPartId] IN (SELECT [SystemPartId] FROM [dbo].[CrmSystemParts] WHERE LOWER([Part]) = LOWER('''+@SysPart+'''))  
										)
							) /**/ '

	SET @Qry = @Qry + N'DELETE dbo.[CrmDyFormFieldMasterValueDetails] 
						WHERE MasterValueId IN(
								SELECT [FormFieldMasterValueId]
								FROM [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldMasterValues]

										)
