						WHERE ' + @Trem +
						' AND [FormId] IN (
						SELECT [FormId]
						FROM [dbo].[CrmDyForms]
						WHERE [CrmSysPartId] IN (SELECT [SystemPartId] FROM [dbo].[CrmSystemParts] WHERE LOWER([Part]) = LOWER('''+@SysPart+'''))

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[MobspInsertDocFull]
/*
	declare @p13 nvarchar(max)
	set @p13=N'#|^|#Tracing=New|^|NewTracing=newtra'
	declare @p14 int
	set @p14=NULL
	declare @p15 nvarchar(max)
	set @p15=NULL
	declare @p16 nvarchar(max)
	set @p16=NULL
	exec [dbo].[MobspInsertDocFull] @DocParams=N'{"DocID":1,"OprId":1,"DocNumber":null,"DocDate":"1402/05/30","SenderId":null,"SenderName":null,"FactorNumber":"سفارش","Description":"توض","IsFix":null,"WHId":2,"OrderId":41394,"BuyOrderId":null,"SendAddress":null,"DriverName":null,"DriverTel":null,"FreightName":null,"BijakNumber":null,"CarNumber":null,"DocDateTime":"2023-08-21 13:24:43.000","WHIdI":null,"CompanyId":68805,"CompanyPersonId":41582,"ProjectId":18195,"cmpTitle":null,"DocNumRef":null,"IsVerified":null,"VerifierId":54,"VerifiedRegisterDate":null,"VerifiedRegisterDatePersian":null,"VerifierRequestId":null,"VerificationWorkFlowId":22,"PreOrderId":null}',@DocItemParams=N'[{"DocItemID":1,"DocId":1,"PrdId":7909,"Quantity":3.0,"CostId":null,"Cost":null,"Description":"","IsFix":null,"WHId":2,"NotVisible":null,"FCR":1.0,"FCRI":2.0,"Tracing":"newtra","PrdUnitId":3,"QuantityTotal":null,"PrdUnitIdI":3,"PrdUnitIRateResult":1.0,"PrdNickname":"مستعار"},{"DocItemID":2,"DocId":1,"PrdId":7910,"Quantity":1.0,"CostId":null,"Cost":null,"Description":"","IsFix":null,"WHId":2,"NotVisible":null,"FCR":0.0,"FCRI":0.0,"Tracing":null,"PrdUnitId":null,"QuantityTotal":null,"PrdUnitIdI":null,"PrdUnitIRateResult":null,"PrdNickname":null}]',@DocItemSerialParams=N'[]',@UserId=3,@FPId=30,@Month1=5,@RegisterDate='2023-08-21 13:54:43.7000923',@RegisterDatePersian=N'14020530',@AppType='mob',@LanguageID=1065,@DefaultLanguageID=1065,@ResErrorMessage=@p13 output,@ResInsertedDocId=@p14 output,@ResInsertedDocItemIds=@p15 output,@ResInsertedDocItemSerialIds=@p16 output
	select @p13, @p14, @p15, @p16
*/
(
@DocParams NVARCHAR(MAX),
@DocItemParams NVARCHAR(MAX),
@DocItemSerialParams NVARCHAR(MAX),
@UserId INT,
@FPId INT,
@Month1 SMALLINT,
@RegisterDate DATETIME,
@RegisterDatePersian NVARCHAR(8),
@AppType VARCHAR(20), 
@LanguageID INT = NULL,
@DefaultLanguageID INT = NULL,
/**/
@ResErrorMessage NVARCHAR(MAX) OUTPUT,
@ResInsertedDocId INT OUTPUT,
@ResInsertedDocItemIds NVARCHAR(MAX) OUTPUT,
@ResInsertedDocItemSerialIds NVARCHAR(MAX) OUTPUT
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
	BEGIN TRANSACTION Main
	BEGIN TRY
		SET @ResErrorMessage = ''

		/**************************************************************************/
		/***********************************HELPER*********************************/
		/**************************************************************************/
		DECLARE @RegisterTime NVARCHAR(8)
		SET @RegisterTime = (CONVERT(CHAR(8), @RegisterDate, 108))	

		DECLARE @DupDocNumber INT, @actAutoCalcNumRef BIT, @actNotDupNumRef BIT,
				@SysMustFullTracingCode BIT, @PrdMustFullTracingCode BIT, @actLoadTracingCodeFromAllPrd BIT,
				@actCanNotAddNewTracingCode BIT, @tracingPrdId INT, @DocItemTracingExist NVARCHAR(150),
				@OprType INT, @TracingRemainCount FLOAT, @SysAddTracingCodeFromSerial BIT

		SELECT
			@SysMustFullTracingCode = [MustFullTracingCode],
			@SysAddTracingCodeFromSerial = [AddTracingCodeFromSerial]
		FROM [dbo].[CrmsysSetting]

		DECLARE @CheckDuplicateDocNumRefTmpTbl TABLE(
			[DocNumber] INT,
			[DocNumRef] NVARCHAR(150)
		)

		DECLARE @DocItemTracingTmpTbl TABLE(
			[Tracing] NVARCHAR(200)
		)

		DECLARE @ReturnPrdQuantityTmpTbl TABLE(
			[QuantityRemain] FLOAT,
			[QuantityIn] FLOAT,
			[QuantityOut] FLOAT,
			[PrdId] INT,
			[PrdName] NVARCHAR(1000),
			[RemainCount] FLOAT,
			[NetPreOrderCount] FLOAT,
			[RemainCountAllWH] FLOAT,
			[RemainCurrAndAll] NVARCHAR(200),
			[TracingRemainCount] FLOAT
		)

		DECLARE @DuplicateSerialTmpTbl TABLE(
			DocItemSerialID INT,
			ItemId INT,
			Serial NVARCHAR(50),
			SerialId INT,
			StatusId INT,
			PrdId INT,
			PrdName NVARCHAR(1000),
			DocNumber INT
		)

		DECLARE @diInsertedDocItemID INT, @diDocItemID INT, @diDocId INT, @diPrdId INT, @diQuantity FLOAT, @diCostId INT, @diCost NUMERIC(18,0),
				@diDescription NVARCHAR(MAX), @diIsFix SMALLINT, @diWHId INT, @diNotVisible BIT, @diFCR FLOAT, @diFCRI FLOAT, @diTracing NVARCHAR(200),
				@diPrdUnitId INT, @diQuantityTotal FLOAT, @diPrdUnitIdI INT, @diPrdUnitIRateResult FLOAT, @diPrdNickname NVARCHAR(1000)

		DECLARE @disInsertedDocItemSerialID INT, @disDocItemSerialID INT, @disItemId INT, @disSerial NVARCHAR(50), @disStatusId INT,
				@disDescription NVARCHAR(300), @disIsFix SMALLINT, @disWHId INT, @disSerialId INT, @disIsAutoBarcode BIT,
				@distblTmpBarcodeId INT, @disExpDate DATETIME,
				@disExpDatePersian NVARCHAR(8), @disInfoI NVARCHAR(200), @disInfoII NVARCHAR(200), @disInfoIII NVARCHAR(200),
				@disInfoIV NVARCHAR(200), @disInfoV NVARCHAR(200), @disInfoVI NVARCHAR(200), @disRootId INT, @disTracing NVARCHAR(200),
				@disPrdId INT


		/**************************************************************************/
		/***********************************DOC************************************/
		/**************************************************************************/

		BEGIN TRY
			DECLARE
				@InsertedDocId INT
				,@DocID INT
				,@OprId INT
				,@DocNumber INT
				,@DocDate NVARCHAR(8)
				,@SenderId INT
				,@SenderName NVARCHAR(500)
				,@FactorNumber NVARCHAR(50)
				,@Description NVARCHAR(4000)
				,@IsFix SMALLINT
				,@WHId INT
				,@OrderId INT
				,@BuyOrderId INT
				,@SendAddress NVARCHAR(500)
				,@DriverName NVARCHAR(100)
				,@DriverTel NVARCHAR(50)
				,@FreightName NVARCHAR(100)
				,@BijakNumber NVARCHAR(50)
				,@CarNumber NVARCHAR(50)
				,@DocDateTime DATETIME
				,@WHIdI INT
				,@CompanyId INT
				,@CompanyPersonId INT
				,@ProjectId INT
				,@cmpTitle NVARCHAR(300)
				,@DocNumRef NVARCHAR(150)
				,@IsVerified BIT
				,@VerifierId INT
				,@VerifiedRegisterDate DATETIME
				,@VerifiedRegisterDatePersian NVARCHAR(8)
				,@VerifierRequestId INT
				,@VerificationWorkFlowId INT
				,@PreOrderId INT

			IF(ISNULL(@DocParams, '') <> '')
			BEGIN
				SELECT
					@DocID = DJ.DocID,
					@OprId = DJ.OprId,
					@DocNumber = DJ.DocNumber,
					@DocDate = DJ.DocDate,
					@SenderId = DJ.SenderId,
					@SenderName = DJ.SenderName,
					@FactorNumber = DJ.FactorNumber,
					@Description = DJ.[Description],
					@IsFix = DJ.IsFix,
					@WHId = DJ.WHId,
					@OrderId = DJ.OrderId,
					@BuyOrderId = DJ.BuyOrderId,
					@SendAddress = DJ.SendAddress,
					@DriverName = DJ.DriverName,
					@DriverTel = DJ.DriverTel,
					@FreightName = DJ.FreightName,
					@BijakNumber = DJ.BijakNumber,
					@CarNumber = DJ.CarNumber,
					@DocDateTime = DJ.DocDateTime,
					@WHIdI = DJ.WHIdI,
					@CompanyId = DJ.CompanyId,
					@CompanyPersonId = DJ.CompanyPersonId,
					@ProjectId = DJ.ProjectId,
					@cmpTitle = DJ.cmpTitle,
					@DocNumRef = DJ.DocNumRef,
					@IsVerified = DJ.IsVerified,
					@VerifierId = DJ.VerifierId,
					@VerifiedRegisterDate = DJ.VerifiedRegisterDate,
					@VerifiedRegisterDatePersian = DJ.VerifiedRegisterDatePersian,
					@VerifierRequestId = DJ.VerifierRequestId,
					@VerificationWorkFlowId = DJ.VerificationWorkFlowId,
					@PreOrderId = DJ.PreOrderId
				FROM OPENJSON(@DocParams)
				WITH(
					DocID INT '$.DocID',
					OprId INT '$.OprId',
					DocNumber INT '$.DocNumber',
					DocDate NVARCHAR(8) '$.DocDate',
					SenderId INT '$.SenderId',
					SenderName NVARCHAR(500) '$.SenderName',
					FactorNumber NVARCHAR(50) '$.FactorNumber',
					[Description] NVARCHAR(4000) '$.Description',
					IsFix SMALLINT '$.IsFix',
					WHId INT '$.WHId',
					OrderId INT '$.OrderId',
					BuyOrderId INT '$.BuyOrderId',
					SendAddress NVARCHAR(500) '$.SendAddress',
					DriverName NVARCHAR(100) '$.DriverName',
					DriverTel NVARCHAR(50) '$.DriverTel',
					FreightName NVARCHAR(100) '$.FreightName',
					BijakNumber NVARCHAR(50) '$.BijakNumber',
					CarNumber NVARCHAR(50) '$.CarNumber',
					DocDateTime DATETIME '$.DocDateTime',
					WHIdI INT '$.WHIdI',
					CompanyId INT '$.CompanyId',
					CompanyPersonId INT '$.CompanyPersonId',
					ProjectId INT '$.ProjectId',
					cmpTitle NVARCHAR(300) '$.cmpTitle',
					DocNumRef NVARCHAR(150) '$.DocNumRef',
					IsVerified BIT '$.IsVerified',
					VerifierId INT '$.VerifierId',
					VerifiedRegisterDate DATETIME '$.VerifiedRegisterDate',
					VerifiedRegisterDatePersian NVARCHAR(8) '$.VerifiedRegisterDatePersian',
					VerifierRequestId INT '$.VerifierRequestId',
					VerificationWorkFlowId INT '$.VerificationWorkFlowId',
					PreOrderId INT '$.PreOrderId'
				) AS DJ
			END

			/**************************************************************************/
			/********************************DOC ITEM**********************************/
			/**************************************************************************/

			DECLARE @DocItemsTbl TABLE(
				[InsertedDocItemID] INT,
				[DocItemID] INT,
				[DocId] INT,
				[PrdId] INT,
				[Quantity] FLOAT,
				[CostId] INT,
				[Cost] NUMERIC(18,0),
				[Description] NVARCHAR(MAX),
				[IsFix] SMALLINT,
				[WHId] INT,
				[NotVisible] BIT,
				[FCR] FLOAT,
				[FCRI] FLOAT,
				[Tracing] NVARCHAR(200),
				[PrdUnitId] INT,
				[QuantityTotal] FLOAT,
				[PrdUnitIdI] INT,
				[PrdUnitIRateResult] FLOAT,
				[PrdNickname] NVARCHAR(1000)
			)

			IF(ISNULL(@DocItemParams, '') <> '')
			BEGIN
				INSERT INTO @DocItemsTbl
				SELECT
					NULL
					,DIJ.[DocItemID]
					,DIJ.[DocId]
					,DIJ.[PrdId]
					,DIJ.[Quantity]
					,DIJ.[CostId]
					,DIJ.[Cost]
					,DIJ.[Description]
					,DIJ.[IsFix]
					,DIJ.[WHId]
					,DIJ.[NotVisible]
					,DIJ.[FCR]
					,DIJ.[FCRI]
					,DIJ.[Tracing]
					,DIJ.[PrdUnitId]
					,DIJ.[QuantityTotal]
					,DIJ.[PrdUnitIdI]
					,DIJ.[PrdUnitIRateResult]
					,DIJ.[PrdNickname]
				FROM OPENJSON(@DocItemParams)
				WITH(
					[DocItemID] INT '$.DocItemID',
					[DocId] INT '$.DocId',
					[PrdId] INT '$.PrdId',
					[Quantity] FLOAT '$.Quantity',
					[CostId] INT '$.CostId',
					[Cost] NUMERIC(18,0) '$.Cost',
					[Description] NVARCHAR(MAX) '$.Description',
					[IsFix] SMALLINT '$.IsFix',
					[WHId] INT '$.WHId',
					[NotVisible] BIT '$.NotVisible',
					[FCR] FLOAT '$.FCR',
					[FCRI] FLOAT '$.FCRI',
					[Tracing] NVARCHAR(200) '$.Tracing',
					[PrdUnitId] INT '$.PrdUnitId',
					[QuantityTotal] FLOAT '$.QuantityTotal',
					[PrdUnitIdI] INT '$.PrdUnitIdI',
					[PrdUnitIRateResult] FLOAT '$.PrdUnitIRateResult',
					[PrdNickname] NVARCHAR(1000) '$.PrdNickname'
				) AS DIJ
			END

			/**************************************************************************/
			/****************************DOC ITEM SERIAL*******************************/
			/**************************************************************************/


			DECLARE @DocItemSerialsTbl TABLE (
				[InsertedDocItemSerialID] INT,
				[DocItemSerialID] INT,
				[ItemId] INT,
				[Serial] NVARCHAR(50),
				[StatusId] INT,
				[Description] NVARCHAR(300),
				[IsFix] SMALLINT,
				[WHId] INT,
				[SerialId] INT,
				[IsAutoBarcode] BIT,
				[tblTmpBarcodeId] INT,
				[ExpDate] DATETIME,
				[ExpDatePersian] NVARCHAR(8),
				[InfoI] NVARCHAR(200),
				[InfoII] NVARCHAR(200),
				[InfoIII] NVARCHAR(200),
				[InfoIV] NVARCHAR(200),
				[InfoV] NVARCHAR(200),
				[InfoVI] NVARCHAR(200),
				[RootId] INT,
				[Tracing] NVARCHAR(200)
			)

			IF(ISNULL(@DocItemSerialParams, '') <> '')
			BEGIN
				INSERT INTO @DocItemSerialsTbl
				SELECT
					NULL,
					DISJ.[DocItemSerialID],
					DISJ.[ItemId],
					DISJ.[Serial],
					DISJ.[StatusId],
					DISJ.[Description],
					DISJ.[IsFix],
					DISJ.[WHId],
					DISJ.[SerialId],
					DISJ.[IsAutoBarcode],
					DISJ.[tblTmpBarcodeId],
					DISJ.[ExpDate],
					DISJ.[ExpDatePersian],
					DISJ.[InfoI],
					DISJ.[InfoII],
					DISJ.[InfoIII],
					DISJ.[InfoIV],
					DISJ.[InfoV],
					DISJ.[InfoVI],
					DISJ.[RootId],
					DISJ.[Tracing]
				FROM OPENJSON(@DocItemSerialParams)
				WITH(
					[DocItemSerialID] INT '$.DocItemSerialID',
					[ItemId] INT '$.ItemId',
					[Serial] NVARCHAR(50) '$.Serial',
					[StatusId] INT '$.StatusId',
					[Description] NVARCHAR(300) '$.Description',
					[IsFix] SMALLINT '$.IsFix',
					[WHId] INT '$.WHId',
					[SerialId] INT '$.SerialId',
					[IsAutoBarcode] BIT '$.IsAutoBarcode',
					[tblTmpBarcodeId] INT '$.tblTmpBarcodeId',
					[ExpDate] DATETIME '$.ExpDate',
					[ExpDatePersian] NVARCHAR(8) '$.ExpDatePersian',
					[InfoI] NVARCHAR(200) '$.InfoI',
					[InfoII] NVARCHAR(200) '$.InfoII',
					[InfoIII] NVARCHAR(200) '$.InfoIII',
					[InfoIV] NVARCHAR(200) '$.InfoIV',
					[InfoV] NVARCHAR(200) '$.InfoV',
					[InfoVI] NVARCHAR(200) '$.InfoVI',
					[RootId] INT '$.RootId',
					[Tracing] NVARCHAR(200) '$.Tracing'
				) AS DISJ
			END

		END TRY
		BEGIN CATCH
			SET @ResErrorMessage = @ResErrorMessage + '#|^|#' + 'ParseData=Error'
			GOTO EndOfScript
		END CATCH

		/**************************************************************************/
		/*******************************DocPreCheck********************************/
		/**************************************************************************/

		SET @OprType = ISNULL((SELECT [OprType] FROM [dbo].[WSDocOpr] WHERE [DocOprID] = @OprId), 0)

		/**/
		IF(ISNULL(@DocNumber, 0) = 0)
		BEGIN
			SET @DocNumber = (SELECT [dbo].[MobfnReturnDocNumber](@OprId))
		END
		SET @DupDocNumber = (SELECT TOP 1 [DocNumber] FROM [dbo].[MobfnCheckDuplicateDocNumber](@DocNumber, 0, @OprId))
		IF(ISNULL(@DupDocNumber, 0) <> 0)
		BEGIN
			SET @ResErrorMessage = @ResErrorMessage + '#|^|#' + 'DocNumber=Dup|^|DupDocNumber=' + CAST(@DupDocNumber AS NVARCHAR(10))
			SET @DupDocNumber = NULL
			GOTO EndOfScript
		END

		/**/

		IF(@OprType = 1)
		BEGIN
			EXEC [dbo].[MobspCheckActionForUser]
				@FormName = 'frmDocDataEntry',
				@ActionName = 'actAutoCalcNumRef',
				@UserId = @UserId,
				@HasAction = @actAutoCalcNumRef OUTPUT
		END
		ELSE IF(@OprType = 2)
		BEGIN
			EXEC [dbo].[MobspCheckActionForUser]
				@FormName = 'frmDraftDataEntry',
				@ActionName = 'actAutoCalcNumRef',
				@UserId = @UserId,
				@HasAction = @actAutoCalcNumRef OUTPUT
		END
		ELSE IF(@OprType = 3)
		BEGIN
			EXEC [dbo].[MobspCheckActionForUser]
				@FormName = 'frmWareHouseMovementDataEntry',
				@ActionName = 'actAutoCalcNumRef',
				@UserId = @UserId,
				@HasAction = @actAutoCalcNumRef OUTPUT
		END

		IF(@actAutoCalcNumRef = 1)
		BEGIN
			EXEC [dbo].[MobspReturnDocNumRef]
				@DocCompanyId = @CompanyId,
				@DocProjectId = @ProjectId,
				@UserId = @UserId,
				@DocNumber = @DocNumber,
				@LanguageID = @LanguageID,
				@DefaultLanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID,
				@SelectedNumRef = @DocNumRef OUTPUT
		END

		IF(ISNULL(@DocNumRef, '') <> '')
		BEGIN
			IF(@OprType = 1)
			BEGIN
				EXEC [dbo].[MobspCheckActionForUser]
					@FormName = 'frmDocDataEntry',
					@ActionName = 'actNotDupNumRef',
					@UserId = @UserId,
					@HasAction = @actNotDupNumRef OUTPUT
			END
			ELSE IF(@OprType = 2)
			BEGIN
				EXEC [dbo].[MobspCheckActionForUser]
					@FormName = 'frmDraftDataEntry',
					@ActionName = 'actNotDupNumRef',
					@UserId = @UserId,
					@HasAction = @actNotDupNumRef OUTPUT
			END
			ELSE IF(@OprType = 3)
			BEGIN
				EXEC [dbo].[MobspCheckActionForUser]
					@FormName = 'frmWareHouseMovementDataEntry',
					@ActionName = 'actNotDupNumRef',
					@UserId = @UserId,
					@HasAction = @actNotDupNumRef OUTPUT
			END

			IF(@actNotDupNumRef = 1)
			BEGIN
				INSERT INTO @CheckDuplicateDocNumRefTmpTbl
				EXEC [dbo].[MobspCheckDuplicateDocNumRef]
					@DocNumRef = @DocNumRef,
					@CurrDocId = 0,
					@DocOprId = @OprId

				SELECT TOP 1 
					@DupDocNumber = [DocNumber]
				FROM @CheckDuplicateDocNumRefTmpTbl

				IF(ISNULL(@DupDocNumber, 0) <> 0)
				BEGIN
					SET @ResErrorMessage = @ResErrorMessage + '#|^|#' + 'DocNumRef=Dup|^|DupDocNumber=' + CAST(@DupDocNumber AS NVARCHAR(10))
					SET @DupDocNumber = NULL
					GOTO EndOfScript
				END

			END
		END


		/**************************************************************************/
		/*******************************Doc Insert*********************************/
		/**************************************************************************/

		IF(ISNULL(@DocDate, '') <> '')
		BEGIN
			SET @DocDateTime = dbo.fnPersianToGregorianDate(@DocDate, @RegisterTime)
		END

		INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSDoc]
			   ([OprId]
			   ,[DocNumber]
			   ,[DocDate]
			   ,[SenderId]
			   ,[SenderName]
			   ,[FactorNumber]
			   ,[Description]
			   ,[IsFix]
			   ,[WHId]
			   ,[FirstRegisterUserId]
			   ,[UserId]
			   ,[Month1]
			   ,[FPId]
			   ,[RegisterDate]
			   ,[RegisterDatePersian]
			   ,[OrderId]
			   ,[BuyOrderId]
			   ,[SendAddress]
			   ,[DriverName]
			   ,[DriverTel]
			   ,[FreightName]
			   ,[BijakNumber]
			   ,[CarNumber]
			   ,[DocDateTime]
			   ,[WHIdI]
			   ,[CompanyId]
			   ,[CompanyPersonId]
			   ,[ProjectId]
			   ,[cmpTitle]
			   ,[AppType]
			   ,[FirstRegisterDatePersian]
			   ,[LanguageID]
			   ,[FirstRegisterDate]
			   ,[DocNumRef]
			   ,[IsVerified]
			   ,[VerifierId]
			   ,[VerifiedRegisterDate]
			   ,[VerifiedRegisterDatePersian]
			   ,[VerifierRequestId]
			   ,[VerificationWorkFlowId]
			   ,[PreOrderId])
		 VALUES
			   (@OprId
			   ,@DocNumber
			   ,@DocDate
			   ,@SenderId
			   ,@SenderName
			   ,@FactorNumber
			   ,@Description
			   ,@IsFix
			   ,@WHId
			   ,@UserId
			   ,@UserId
			   ,@Month1
			   ,@FPId
			   ,@RegisterDate
			   ,@RegisterDatePersian
			   ,@OrderId
			   ,@BuyOrderId
			   ,@SendAddress
			   ,@DriverName
			   ,@DriverTel
			   ,@FreightName
			   ,@BijakNumber
			   ,@CarNumber
			   ,@DocDateTime
			   ,@WHIdI
			   ,@CompanyId
			   ,@CompanyPersonId
			   ,@ProjectId
			   ,@cmpTitle
			   ,@AppType
			   ,@RegisterDatePersian
			   ,@DefaultLanguageID
			   ,@RegisterDate
			   ,@DocNumRef
			   ,@IsVerified
			   ,@VerifierId
			   ,@VerifiedRegisterDate
			   ,@VerifiedRegisterDatePersian
			   ,@VerifierRequestId
			   ,@VerificationWorkFlowId
			   ,@PreOrderId
			   )
		SET @InsertedDocId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()

		UPDATE @DocItemsTbl SET
			[DocId] = @InsertedDocId

		/******* Insert to lang table ********/
		INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSDoc_Lang]
			(
			[DocID]
			,[LanguageID]
			,[SenderName]
			,[Description]
			,[SendAddress]
			,[DriverName]
			,[FreightName]
			,[cmpTitle]
			)
		SELECT
			@InsertedDocId
			,LN.LanguageID
			,@SenderName
			,@Description
			,@SendAddress
			,@DriverName
			,@FreightName
			,@cmpTitle
		FROM dbo.Languages LN
		WHERE LN.LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID
		AND ISNULL(LN.IsActive,0) =1

		/**************************************************************************/
		/*****************************Doc Item insert******************************/
		/**************************************************************************/

		DECLARE CrsrDocItem CURSOR LOCAL FOR
		SELECT 
			[InsertedDocItemID], [DocItemID],[DocId],[PrdId],[Quantity],[CostId]
			,[Cost],[Description],[IsFix],[WHId],[NotVisible],[FCR],[FCRI],[Tracing]
			,[PrdUnitId],[QuantityTotal],[PrdUnitIdI],[PrdUnitIRateResult],[PrdNickname]
		FROM @DocItemsTbl OPEN CrsrDocItem FETCH NEXT
		FROM CrsrDocItem INTO @diInsertedDocItemID, @diDocItemID, @diDocId, @diPrdId, @diQuantity, @diCostId, @diCost,
						@diDescription, @diIsFix, @diWHId, @diNotVisible, @diFCR, @diFCRI, @diTracing, @diPrdUnitId, @diQuantityTotal,
						@diPrdUnitIdI, @diPrdUnitIRateResult, @diPrdNickname
		WHILE @@FETCH_STATUS = 0 BEGIN

			SELECT
				@PrdMustFullTracingCode = [PrdMustFullTracingCode]
			FROM [dbo].[WSProducts]
			WHERE ProductID = @diPrdId

			IF(ISNULL(@diTracing,'') = '')
			BEGIN
				IF(ISNULL(@SysMustFullTracingCode, 0) <> 0)
				BEGIN
					SET @ResErrorMessage = @ResErrorMessage + '#|^|#' + 'Tracing=Force|^|PrdId=' + CAST(@diPrdId AS NVARCHAR(10))
					GOTO EndOfScript
				END
			END
			ELSE IF(ISNULL(@PrdMustFullTracingCode, 0) <> 0)
			BEGIN
				SET @ResErrorMessage = @ResErrorMessage + '#|^|#' + 'Tracing=Force|^|PrdId=' + CAST(@diPrdId AS NVARCHAR(10))
				GOTO EndOfScript
			END

			IF(@OprType = 1)
			BEGIN
				IF(ISNULL(@diTracing,'') <> '')
				BEGIN
					EXEC [dbo].[MobspCheckActionForUser]
						@FormName = 'frmDocItemDataEntry',
						@ActionName = 'actLoadTracingCodeFromAllPrd',
						@UserId = @UserId,
						@HasAction = @actLoadTracingCodeFromAllPrd OUTPUT

					EXEC [dbo].[MobspCheckActionForUser]
						@FormName = 'frmDocItemDataEntry',
						@ActionName = 'actCanNotAddNewTracingCode',
						@UserId = @UserId,
						@HasAction = @actCanNotAddNewTracingCode OUTPUT

					SET @tracingPrdId = @diPrdId
					IF(@actLoadTracingCodeFromAllPrd = 1)
					BEGIN
						SET @tracingPrdId = 0
					END

					IF(@actCanNotAddNewTracingCode = 1)
					BEGIN
						SET @DocItemTracingExist = NULL
						DELETE @DocItemTracingTmpTbl

						INSERT INTO @DocItemTracingTmpTbl
						EXEC [dbo].[MobspSelectDocItemTracing]
							@Tracing = @diTracing,
							@PrdId = @tracingPrdId

					
						SELECT TOP 1
							@DocItemTracingExist = [Tracing]
						FROM @DocItemTracingTmpTbl

						IF(@DocItemTracingExist IS NULL)
						BEGIN
							SET @ResErrorMessage = @ResErrorMessage + '#|^|#' + 'Tracing=New|^|NewTracing=' + @diTracing + 
								'|^|PrdId=' + CAST(@diPrdId AS NVARCHAR(10))
							GOTO EndOfScript
						END
					END
				END
			END

			IF(@OprType = 2 OR @OprType = 3)
			BEGIN
				SET @TracingRemainCount = NULL
				DELETE @ReturnPrdQuantityTmpTbl

				DECLARE @QtyWhId INT = NULL
				IF(@OprType = 3)
				BEGIN
					SET @QtyWhId = @WHIdI
				END
				ELSE
				BEGIN
					SET @QtyWhId = @WHId
				END
				
				INSERT INTO @ReturnPrdQuantityTmpTbl
				EXEC [dbo].[MobspReturnPrdQuantity]
					@PrdId = @diPrdId,
					@FPId = @FPId,
					@WHId = @QtyWhId,
					@CurrId = 0,
					@LanguageID = @LanguageID,
					@DefaultLanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID,
					@FCR = @diFCR,
					@FCRI = @diFCRI,
					@Tracing = @diTracing,
					@QuantityRemain = NULL

				SELECT TOP 1
					@TracingRemainCount = [TracingRemainCount]
				FROM @ReturnPrdQuantityTmpTbl

				IF(ISNULL(@diQuantity, 1) > ISNULL(@TracingRemainCount, 0))
				BEGIN
					SET @ResErrorMessage = @ResErrorMessage + '#|^|#' + 'QtyRemainLimit|^|PrdId=' + CAST(@diPrdId AS NVARCHAR(10))
					GOTO EndOfScript
				END
					
			END


			INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSDocItems]
				([DocId]
				,[PrdId]
				,[Quantity]
				,[CostId]
				,[Cost]
				,[Description]
				,[IsFix]
				,[WHId]
				,[UserId]
				,[Month1]
				,[FPId]
				,[RegisterDate]
				,[RegisterDatePersian]
				,[NotVisible]
				,[AppType]
				,[LanguageID]
				,[FirstRegisterUserId]
				,[FirstRegisterDatePersian]
				,[FirstRegisterDate]
				,[FCR]
				,[FCRI]
				,[Tracing]
				,[PrdUnitId]
				,[QuantityTotal]
				,[PrdUnitIdI]
				,[PrdUnitIRateResult]
				,[PrdNickname])
			VALUES
				(@diDocId
				,@diPrdId
				,@diQuantity
				,@diCostId
				,@diCost
				,@diDescription
				,@diIsFix
				,@diWHId
				,@UserId
				,@Month1
				,@FPId
				,@RegisterDate
				,@RegisterDatePersian
				,@diNotVisible
				,@AppType
				,@LanguageID
				,@UserId
				,@RegisterDatePersian
				,@RegisterDate
				,@diFCR
				,@diFCRI
				,@diTracing
				,@diPrdUnitId
				,@diQuantityTotal
				,@diPrdUnitIdI
				,@diPrdUnitIRateResult
				,@diPrdNickname
				)

				SET @diInsertedDocItemID = SCOPE_IDENTITY()

				UPDATE @DocItemsTbl SET
					[InsertedDocItemID] = @diInsertedDocItemID
				WHERE [DocItemID] = @diDocItemID

				UPDATE @DocItemSerialsTbl SET 
					[ItemId] = @diInsertedDocItemID
				WHERE [ItemId] = @diDocItemID

				/******* Insert to lang table ********/
				INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSDocItems_Lang]
					(
					[DocItemID]
					,[LanguageID]
					,[Description]
					,[PrdNickname]
					)
				SELECT
					@diInsertedDocItemID
					,LN.LanguageID
					,@diDescription
					,@diPrdNickname
				FROM dbo.Languages LN 
				WHERE LN.LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID	
				AND ISNULL(LN.IsActive,0) = 1
	
		FETCH NEXT FROM CrsrDocItem
		INTO @diInsertedDocItemID, @diDocItemID, @diDocId, @diPrdId, @diQuantity, @diCostId, @diCost,
				@diDescription, @diIsFix, @diWHId, @diNotVisible, @diFCR, @diFCRI, @diTracing, @diPrdUnitId,
				@diQuantityTotal, @diPrdUnitIdI, @diPrdUnitIRateResult, @diPrdNickname
   
		END CLOSE CrsrDocItem DEALLOCATE CrsrDocItem



		/**************************************************************************/
		/***************************Doc Item serial inset**************************/
		/**************************************************************************/

		DECLARE CrsrDocItemSerial CURSOR LOCAL FOR
		SELECT 
			[InsertedDocItemSerialID], [DocItemSerialID],[ItemId],[Serial],[StatusId],[Description],[IsFix],
			[WHId],[SerialId],[IsAutoBarcode],[tblTmpBarcodeId],[ExpDate],[ExpDatePersian],[InfoI],[InfoII],
			[InfoIII],[InfoIV],[InfoV],[InfoVI],[RootId], [Tracing]
		FROM @DocItemSerialsTbl OPEN CrsrDocItemSerial FETCH NEXT
		FROM CrsrDocItemSerial INTO @disInsertedDocItemSerialID, @disDocItemSerialID, @disItemId, @disSerial, @disStatusId,
							@disDescription, @disIsFix, @disWHId, @disSerialId, @disIsAutoBarcode, @distblTmpBarcodeId, @disExpDate,
							@disExpDatePersian, @disInfoI, @disInfoII, @disInfoIII, @disInfoIV, @disInfoV, @disInfoVI, @disRootId,
							@disTracing
		WHILE @@FETCH_STATUS = 0 BEGIN 

			IF(ISNULL(@disExpDatePersian, '') <> '')
			BEGIN
				SET @disExpDate = dbo.fnPersianToGregorianDate(@disExpDatePersian, NULL)
			END

			IF(@OprType = 2)
			BEGIN
				DELETE @DuplicateSerialTmpTbl
				SET @DupDocNumber = NULL

				SET @disPrdId = (SELECT DI.[PrdId] FROM [dbo].[WSDocItems] DI WHERE [DocItemID] = @disItemId)

				INSERT INTO @DuplicateSerialTmpTbl
				EXEC [dbo].[MobspCheckDuplicatePrdSerial]
					@OprId = @OprId,
					@FPId = @FPId,
					@WHID = @disWHId,
					@PrdId = @disPrdId,
					@Serial = @disSerial,
					@CurrId = 0,
					@LanguageID = @LanguageID,
					@DefaultLanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID,
					@DocItemSerialID = @disSerialId

				SELECT TOP 1
					@DupDocNumber = [DocNumber]
				FROM @DuplicateSerialTmpTbl

				IF(ISNULL(@DupDocNumber, 0) <> 0)
				BEGIN
					SET @ResErrorMessage = @ResErrorMessage + '#|^|#' + 'Serial=Dup|^|DocNumber=' + CAST(@DupDocNumber AS NVARCHAR(10)) + 
						'|^|Serial=' + @disSerial
					GOTO EndOfScript
				END
			END
					  
			INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSDocItemSerials]
			   ([ItemId]
			   ,[Serial]
			   ,[StatusId]
			   ,[Description]
			   ,[IsFix]
			   ,[WHId]
			   ,[UserId]
			   ,[Month1]
			   ,[FPId]
			   ,[RegisterDate]
			   ,[RegisterDatePersian]
			   ,[SerialId]
			   ,[IsAutoBarcode]
			   ,[tblTmpBarcodeId]
			   ,[ExpDate]
			   ,[ExpDatePersian]
			   ,[AppType]
			   ,[InfoI]
			   ,[InfoII]
			   ,[InfoIII]
			   ,[InfoIV]
			   ,[InfoV]
			   ,[InfoVI]
			   ,[LanguageID]
			   ,[FirstRegisterUserId]
			   ,[FirstRegisterDatePersian]
			   ,[FirstRegisterDate]
			   ,[RootId])
			VALUES
			   (@disItemId
			   ,@disSerial
			   ,@disStatusId
			   ,@disDescription
			   ,@disIsFix
			   ,@disWHId
			   ,@UserId
			   ,@Month1
			   ,@FPId
			   ,@RegisterDate
			   ,@RegisterDatePersian
			   ,@disSerialId
			   ,@disIsAutoBarcode
			   ,@distblTmpBarcodeId
			   ,@disExpDate
			   ,@disExpDatePersian
			   ,@AppType
			   ,@disInfoI
			   ,@disInfoII
			   ,@disInfoIII
			   ,@disInfoIV
			   ,@disInfoV
			   ,@disInfoVI
			   ,@DefaultLanguageID
			   ,@UserId
			   ,@RegisterDatePersian
			   ,@RegisterDate
			   ,@disRootId
			   )

			SET @disInsertedDocItemSerialID = SCOPE_IDENTITY()

			UPDATE @DocItemSerialsTbl SET
				[InsertedDocItemSerialID] = @disInsertedDocItemSerialID
			WHERE [DocItemSerialID] = @disDocItemSerialID
			AND [ItemId] = @disItemId

			/******* Insert to lang table ********/
			INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSDocItemSerials_Lang]
				(
				[DocItemSerialID]
				,[LanguageID]
				,[Description]
				,[InfoI]
				,[InfoII]
				,[InfoIII]
				,[InfoIV]
				,[InfoV]
				,[InfoVI]
				)
			SELECT
				@disInsertedDocItemSerialID
				,LN.LanguageID
				,@disDescription
				,@disInfoI
				,@disInfoII
				,@disInfoIII
				,@disInfoIV
				,@disInfoV
				,@disInfoVI
			FROM dbo.Languages LN
			WHERE LN.LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID
			AND ISNULL(LN.IsActive,0) = 1
			/******* Update docitem serial info ********/
			IF(@OprType = 2 OR @OprType = 3)
			BEGIN
				EXEC [dbo].[MobspUpdateDocItemSerialInfoById]
					@DocItemSerialId = @disInsertedDocItemSerialID,
					@UserId = @UserId,
					@Month1 = @Month1,
					@FPId = @FPId,
					@RegisterDate = @RegisterDate,
					@RegisterDatePersian = @RegisterDatePersian,
					@AppType = @AppType
			END
			/******* Add tracing code from serial ********/
			IF(@OprType = 2 OR @OprType = 3)
			BEGIN
				IF(ISNULL(@SysAddTracingCodeFromSerial, 0) = 1)
				BEGIN
					IF(ISNULL(@disTracing, '') <> '')
					BEGIN
						EXEC [dbo].[MobspUpdateDocItemTracing]
							@DocItemID = @disItemId,
							@Tracing = @disTracing,
							@UserId = @UserId,
							@Month1 = @Month1,
							@RegisterDate = @RegisterDate,
							@RegisterDatePersian = @RegisterDatePersian,
							@AppType = @AppType
					END
				END
			END


					  
		FETCH NEXT FROM CrsrDocItemSerial INTO @disInsertedDocItemSerialID, @disDocItemSerialID, @disItemId, @disSerial,
							@disStatusId,@disDescription, @disIsFix, @disWHId, @disSerialId, @disIsAutoBarcode, @distblTmpBarcodeId,
							@disExpDate, @disExpDatePersian,
							@disInfoI, @disInfoII, @disInfoIII, @disInfoIV, @disInfoV, @disInfoVI, @disRootId, @disTracing
						  END CLOSE CrsrDocItemSerial DEALLOCATE CrsrDocItemSerial


	END TRY
	BEGIN CATCH
		SET @ResErrorMessage = @ResErrorMessage + '#|^|#' + 'Error'
	END CATCH


	EndOfScript:
		PRINT 'EndOfScript:'

	IF((ISNULL(@ResErrorMessage, '') <> ''))
	BEGIN
		ROLLBACK TRANSACTION Main
		SET @ResInsertedDocId = NULL
		SET @ResInsertedDocItemIds = NULL
		SET @ResInsertedDocItemSerialIds = NULL
		SELECT 0
	END
	ELSE
	BEGIN
		COMMIT TRANSACTION Main
		SET @ResInsertedDocId = @InsertedDocId
		SET @ResInsertedDocItemIds = 
			(SELECT [DocItemID] AS [Id], [InsertedDocItemID] AS [InsertId] FROM @DocItemsTbl FOR JSON PATH)
		SET @ResInsertedDocItemSerialIds = 
			(SELECT [DocItemSerialID] AS [Id], [InsertedDocItemSerialID] AS [InsertId] FROM @DocItemSerialsTbl FOR JSON PATH)
		SELECT 1
	END

/**/
END







GO
-- [dbo].[MobspInsertQuickCmpPrsPrjCnvs] | References permission/temp-filter machinery

/*AI14021009*/
DECLARE @CompanyAddressTmpTable TABLE(
	[CompanyAddressId] INT,
	[cmpaddCompanyId] INT,
	[cmpaddCityId] INT,
	[CompanyAddress] NVARCHAR(1000),
	[cmpaddDesc] NVARCHAR(500),
	[cmpaddZipCode] NVARCHAR(50),
	[cmpaddPostBox] NVARCHAR(50),
	[cmpaddCoordinateLatitude] NVARCHAR(100),
	[cmpaddCoordinateLongitude] NVARCHAR(100),
	[cmpaddCoordinateLastDate] DATETIME,
	[cmpaddCoordinateLastDatePersian] VARCHAR(8),
	[cmpaddCoordinateZoomLevel] INT,
	[cmpaddCoordinateLastUserId] INT,
	[cmpaddTypeId] INT
)
/*AI14021009-End*/
/********************************************************************************/
/***************************** Split fields *************************************/
/********************************************************************************/
SET @IsDuplicate  = 0
/********************************************************************************/
/****************************** Company *****************************************/
/********************************************************************************/
BEGIN TRAN
/**/
/*RZ961003-chang |&| to |#|*/
DECLARE @CmpField NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL
WHILE LEN(@Company) > 0
BEGIN
    IF PATINDEX('%|#|%', @Company) > 0
    BEGIN
        SET @CmpField = SUBSTRING(@Company,
                                    0,
                                    PATINDEX('%|#|%', @Company))
        /*SELECT @CmpField*/
        /*--------------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
        /*Get parameter name and split value form parameter*/
        /*For example => cmpTypeId=102 => cmpTypeId= => cmpTypeId => Compare => value*/
        DECLARE @CmpFieldValue NVARCHAR(MAX)
        DECLARE @CmpFieldName NVARCHAR(200)
        SET @CmpFieldName = SUBSTRING(@CmpField, 1, CHARINDEX('=',@CmpField)-1)                                    
		SET @CmpFieldValue = SUBSTRING(@CmpField, CHARINDEX('=', @CmpField)+1, Len(@CmpField))
        /*--------------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
        /*---------------------------- BEGIN Fill variabele ------------------------------*/
        /*BEGIN COMPANY*/
        IF @CmpFieldName = 'CompanyID'
        BEGIN
			SELECT @CompanyId = CASE WHEN @CmpFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE CAST(@CmpFieldValue AS INT) END
			/*RZ970508- Add rollback when have error Start*/
			SET @CompanyIdInError =  @CompanyId
			/*RZ970508- Add rollback when have error END*/
        END
        /*--------------------------*/
		IF @CmpFieldName = 'cmpImportDate'
        BEGIN
			SET @cmpImportDate = CASE WHEN @CmpFieldValue = '' OR @CmpFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE REPLACE(@CmpFieldValue, '/', '') END
			/*RZ961206- Add time to date*/							
			SET @cmpImportDateTime = CASE WHEN @cmpImportDate IS NULL THEN NULL ELSE dbo.fnPersianToGregorianDate(@cmpImportDate, @RegisterTime) END			 			
        END
        /*--------------------------*/
		IF @CmpFieldName = 'cmpName'
        BEGIN
			SET @cmpName = CASE WHEN @CmpFieldValue = '' OR @CmpFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE @CmpFieldValue END	
			SET @cmpName = (SELECT dbo.[fnConvertArabicCharacterToPersian](@cmpName)) /*AI14011027*/
			/*IF(@cmpName IS NOT NULL AND @cmpName <> @PersonLabel)/*check duplicate*/
			BEGIN					
				DECLARE	@ResultCheckDuplicate INT = 0	
				EXEC [dbo].[MobspCheckDuplicateCompany]
						@Type  = 0, 
						@Id = 0, 
						@Val = @cmpName, 
						@TitleID = 0, 
						@CityId = 0, 
						@UserID  = @UserId ,							
						@Result = @ResultCheckDuplicate OUTPUT,
						/*RZ961129- Multi language*/
						@LanguageID = @LanguageID,
						@DefaultLanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID

				IF(@ResultCheckDuplicate > 0)
					SET @IsDuplicate = 1						
			END*/			
        END
        /*--------------------------*/
		IF @CmpFieldName = 'cmpNameEng'
        BEGIN
			SET @cmpNameEng = CASE WHEN @CmpFieldValue = '' OR @CmpFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE @CmpFieldValue END
        END
        /*--------------------------*/
		IF @CmpFieldName = 'cmpTitleId'
        BEGIN
			SELECT @cmpTitleId = CASE WHEN @CmpFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE CAST(@CmpFieldValue AS INT) END
        END
        /*--------------------------*/
		IF @CmpFieldName = 'cmpFame'
        BEGIN
			SET @cmpFame = CASE WHEN @CmpFieldValue = '' OR @CmpFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE @CmpFieldValue END
        END
        /*--------------------------*/
		IF @CmpFieldName = 'cmpActivityType'
        BEGIN
			SET @cmpActivityType = CASE WHEN @CmpFieldValue = '' OR @CmpFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE @CmpFieldValue END
        END
        /*--------------------------*/
		IF @CmpFieldName = 'cmpGroupId'
        BEGIN
			SELECT @cmpGroupId = CASE WHEN @CmpFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE CAST(@CmpFieldValue AS INT) END

			/*RZ971222- Add check access to cmp and prjTitle*/
			IF(@cmpGroupId IS NOT NULL)
			BEGIN
				DECLARE @IsAccessGroupId BIT = 0

				EXEC dbo.MobspCheckCmpDataPermissionForUser 	
				@Mode = 3,
				@Id = @cmpGroupId,
				@CrmUserId = @UserId,
				@IsAccess = @IsAccessGroupId OUTPUT
				
				IF(@IsAccessGroupId = 0)
				BEGIN
					DECLARE @GroupName NVARCHAR(1000)
					SET @GroupName = (SELECT
										CASE  WHEN (@LanguageID IS NULL OR @LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID)
											THEN (SELECT GroupName FROM dbo.CrmCompanyGroups CG WHERE CG.CompanyGroupID = @cmpGroupId)
											ELSE (SELECT GroupName FROM dbo.CrmCompanyGroups_Lang CG WHERE CG.CompanyGroupID = @cmpGroupId AND CG.LanguageID = @LanguageID)
										END
									 )

					SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + '|Msg=CmpGroupIdAccess|' + ISNULL(@GroupName,'') + '|Msg=CmpGroupIdAccess|'
				END	 
			END
			/*RZ971222- END*/
        END
        /*--------------------------*/
		IF @CmpFieldName = 'cmpSubGroupId'
        BEGIN
			SELECT @cmpSubGroupId = CASE WHEN @CmpFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE CAST(@CmpFieldValue AS INT) END

			/*RZ971222- Add check access to cmp and prjTitle*/
			IF(@cmpSubGroupId IS NOT NULL)
			BEGIN
				DECLARE @IsAccessSubGroupId BIT = 0

				EXEC dbo.MobspCheckCmpDataPermissionForUser 	
				@Mode = 4,
				@Id = @cmpSubGroupId,

/************************** Check duplicate before post *************************/
/*RZ961027- change search cmpname*/
IF(@cmpName IS NOT NULL AND @cmpName <> @PersonLabel)/*check duplicate*/
BEGIN
DECLARE @TitleId INT , @CityId INT
SELECT @TitleId = CASE WHEN @cmpTitleId IS NULL THEN 0 ELSE @cmpTitleId END
SELECT @CityId = CASE WHEN @cmpCityId IS NULL THEN 0 ELSE @cmpCityId END
					
	DECLARE	@ResultCheckDuplicateForCmpName INT = 0	
	EXEC [dbo].[MobspCheckDuplicateCompany]
			@Type  = 1, 
			@Id = 0, 
			@Val = @cmpName, 
			@TitleID = @TitleId, 
			@CityId = @CityId, 
			@UserID  = @UserId ,							
			/*@Result = @ResultCheckDuplicateForCmpName OUTPUT, AI14010529 - Because sp changed*/
			/*RZ961129- Multi language*/
			@LanguageID = @LanguageID,
			@DefaultLanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID

	SET @ResultCheckDuplicateForCmpName = @@ROWCOUNT
	IF(@ResultCheckDuplicateForCmpName > 0)
		SET @IsDuplicate = 1						
END	
/**/
/*AI990730 - below checks added*/
/*AI990730- check cmpCode duplicate*/
IF(@cmpCode IS NOT NULL)
BEGIN
	IF EXISTS (
	SELECT * FROM dbo.CrmsysRollPermission
	WHERE FormId = (SELECT ID FROM dbo.CrmsysForms WHERE FormName = N'frmCompanyI' )
	AND FormActionId = (SELECT Id FROM dbo.CrmsysFormActions WHERE ActionName = N'actCmpCodeNotDup'
						AND FormId = (SELECT ID FROM dbo.CrmsysForms WHERE FormName = N'frmCompanyI' ))
	AND RollId = (SELECT TOP 1 RollId FROM dbo.UsersPermission WHERE UserId = @UserId AND PrjId = 11/*CRM*/))
	BEGIN
		DECLARE	@ResultCheckDuplicateForCmpCode NVARCHAR(700) = ''	
		EXEC [dbo].[MobspCheckDuplicateCompanyCode] 
			@cmpCode, 
			0, 
			@LanguageID, 
			@DefaultLanguageID, 
			@RecordCount = @ResultCheckDuplicateForCmpCode OUTPUT;
			
		IF(@ResultCheckDuplicateForCmpCode <> '')
			SET @IsDuplicate = 1
	END
END
/*AI990730- check cmpTel duplicate*/
IF(@cmpTel IS NOT NULL)
BEGIN
	DECLARE @HavePermissionDuplicatecmpTel INT
	IF EXISTS (
	SELECT * FROM dbo.CrmsysRollPermission
	WHERE FormId = (SELECT ID FROM dbo.CrmsysForms WHERE FormName = N'frmCompanyI' )
	AND FormActionId = (SELECT Id FROM dbo.CrmsysFormActions WHERE ActionName = N'actCanSaveTelDupAtTimeLimit'
						AND FormId = (SELECT ID FROM dbo.CrmsysForms WHERE FormName = N'frmCompanyI' ))
	AND RollId = (SELECT TOP 1 RollId FROM dbo.UsersPermission WHERE UserId = @UserId AND PrjId = 11/*CRM*/))
	SET @HavePermissionDuplicatecmpTel = 1
		ELSE
	SET @HavePermissionDuplicatecmpTel = 0

	IF(@CantSaveDupTel = 1 AND @HavePermissionDuplicatecmpTel = 0)
	BEGIN
		/*AI14000304 - preTel + Tel for checking duplicate instead of Tel*/
		DECLARE @chckCmpTel NVARCHAR(100)
		SET @chckCmpTel = ISNULL(@cmpPreTel, '') + @cmpTel

		DECLARE @ResultCheckDuplicateForCmpTel INT
		EXEC MobspCheckDuplicateTel @chckCmpTel,1, 0,@LanguageID,@DefaultLanguageID /*AI14010329*/
		SET @ResultCheckDuplicateForCmpTel = @@ROWCOUNT
		IF(@ResultCheckDuplicateForCmpTel > 0)
			SET @IsDuplicate = 1
	END
END
/**/
/*AI990730- check cmpTelII duplicate*/
IF(@cmpTelII IS NOT NULL)
BEGIN
	DECLARE @HavePermissionDuplicatecmpTelII INT
	IF EXISTS (
	SELECT * FROM dbo.CrmsysRollPermission
	WHERE FormId = (SELECT ID FROM dbo.CrmsysForms WHERE FormName = N'frmCompanyI' )
	AND FormActionId = (SELECT Id FROM dbo.CrmsysFormActions WHERE ActionName = N'actCanSaveTelDupAtTimeLimit'
						AND FormId = (SELECT ID FROM dbo.CrmsysForms WHERE FormName = N'frmCompanyI' ))
	AND RollId = (SELECT TOP 1 RollId FROM dbo.UsersPermission WHERE UserId = @UserId AND PrjId = 11/*CRM*/))
	SET @HavePermissionDuplicatecmpTelII = 1

	ELSE
	SET @HavePermissionDuplicatecmpTelII = 0

	IF(@HavePermissionDuplicatecmpTelII = 0 AND @CantSaveDupTel = 1)
	BEGIN
		/*AI14000304 - preTel + Tel for checking duplicate instead of Tel*/
		DECLARE @chckCmpTelII NVARCHAR(100)
		SET @chckCmpTel = ISNULL(@cmpPreTelII, '') + @cmpTelII

		DECLARE @ResultCheckDuplicateForCmpTelII INT
		EXEC MobspCheckDuplicateTel @chckCmpTelII,1, 0,@LanguageID,@DefaultLanguageID /*AI14010329*/
		SET @ResultCheckDuplicateForCmpTelII = @@ROWCOUNT
		IF(@ResultCheckDuplicateForCmpTelII > 0)
			SET @IsDuplicate = 1
	END
END

	AND RollId = (SELECT TOP 1 RollId FROM dbo.UsersPermission WHERE UserId = @UserId AND PrjId = 11/*CRM*/))

	ELSE
	SET @HavePermissionDuplicatecmpFax = 0

	IF(@HavePermissionDuplicatecmpFax = 0 AND @CantSaveDupCmpFax = 1)
	BEGIN

		/*AI14000304 - preTel + Tel for checking duplicate instead of Tel*/
		DECLARE @chckCmpFax NVARCHAR(100)

			SET @IsDuplicate = 1
	END
END
